### [1] [WINSO: A Large-Size Very-High-Resolution Remote Sensing Dataset for Wind Turbine Detection and Solar Panel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27241)
*Di Wang,Ke Li,Zhaokun Li,Weiping Ni,Junzheng Wu,Yahu Liu,Quan Wang,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WINSO，亚太地区首个基于0.5–0.7 m甚高分辨率卫星影像的大规模风能与太阳能设施基准数据集，规模286.36吉像素、166,473个标注实例，并提供多类模型的基线评测与跨数据集泛化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风光能源是全球净零排放转型的核心，精确的空间清单是能源规划的前提。卫星遥感可规模化获取地理空间数据，但大尺寸影像上的设施制图受限于高质量标注数据集稀缺与系统性评测协议缺失。

Method: 构建 WINSO 基准：10,962幅大尺寸VHR影像（GSD 0.5–0.7 m），覆盖亚太多地、≥71,600 km²、共286.36吉像素，单图最大44,032×62,208像素；经人工标注与专家核验，风力机用水平边界框（HBB）、光伏板用多边形掩膜，共166,473实例；评测SOTA检测与分割模型、超高分（UHR）专用架构及视觉语言模型在多种推理策略下的表现，并开展两个外部公开数据集的跨数据集评估。

Result: 建立了清洁能源设施制图基线，SOTA模型、UHR专用架构与视觉语言模型均被系统评测；跨数据集实验显示WINSO训练的模型具备较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: WINSO 提供了标准化基准，推动自动化清洁能源制图与广域清洁能源转型监测研究。

Abstract: Wind and solar energy are central to the global transition toward net-zero emissions and sustainable development. Accurate spatial inventories of wind and solar facilities are essential for future energy planning. Satellite remote sensing provides a practical and efficient means to acquire such geospatial data at scale. However, reliable facility mapping from large-size satellite images remains constrained by the scarcity of carefully annotated datasets and the lack of systematic evaluation protocols. To address these limitations, we introduce \textbf{WINSO}, a large-scale wind and solar benchmark based on very-high-resolution (VHR) satellite imagery with ground sampling distances (GSDs) of 0.5--0.7\,m. WINSO comprises 10,962 large-size images sampled from diverse geographic locations across the Asia-Pacific region, covering at least 71,600\,$\text{km}^2$ and totaling 286.36 gigapixels, with individual image dimensions reaching 44,032$\times$62,208 pixels. Through manual annotation and expert verification, a total of 166,473 instances are labeled using horizontal bounding boxes (HBBs) for wind turbines and polygon masks for solar panels. To build baselines for clean-energy facility mapping, we evaluate state-of-the-art detection and segmentation models, specialized ultra-high-resolution (UHR) architectures, and vision-language models across different inference strategies. Furthermore, cross-dataset evaluations on two external public datasets illustrate the strong generalization capability of the WINSO. WINSO establishes a standardized benchmark to advance automated clean-energy mapping, paving the way for wide-area monitoring of the clean-energy transition.

</details>


### [2] [Mem-X: A Memory-Dependent Cross-Episode Benchmark for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27166)
*Zhikang Shi,Yuk Tung Samuel Fang,Hongyu Ding,Zixuan Chen,Yujin Wang,Junhui Guo,You Wu,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mem-X，一个面向具身智能体的、以记忆依赖为核心的跨回合视觉语言导航基准，并给出模块化基线 MemBridge。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨回合 VLN 基准难以区分导航成功究竟依赖历史记忆还是当前探测，缺乏对"记忆依赖型"导航能力的明确评测。

Method: 将回合组织为会话，后续指令引用早期回合中出现过的物体或路线；设置三个难度层级：历史物体回忆、记忆内定位与回合索引、脱离记忆的重定位与历史路线复现；后两级仅提供回合内位姿而无回合间坐标变换，要求智能体自主建立与历史记忆的空间对应。规模为 34 个场景、710 个会话、2,071 个回合（其中 1,204 个记忆依赖回合）。并构建 MemBridge 基线，融合语义场景记忆、跨回合定位与由粗到细检索。

Result: 在现有 SOTA VLN 方法上集成 MemBridge 均带来性能提升，但在记忆依赖型导航上仍存在显著提升空间。

Conclusion: Mem-X 为记忆依赖的跨回合 VLN 提供了可控、可量化的评测平台，MemBridge 验证了外部记忆模块的有效性，并揭示了该方向的重要研究缺口。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables embodied agents to reuse past observations across tasks. However, existing cross-episode vision-and-language navigation (VLN) benchmarks often leave it unclear whether successful navigation relies on historical memory or current exploration. We introduce Mem-X, a benchmark designed to evaluate memory-dependent cross-episode VLN. Mem-X organizes episodes into sessions in which subsequent instructions refer to objects or routes encountered in earlier episodes. Its three levels evaluate historical-object recall, in-memory localization and episodic indexing, and off-memory relocalization and historical-route reproduction. For the latter two levels, agents receive episode-local poses but no transformations between episode frames, requiring them to establish spatial correspondence with historical memory. Mem-X comprises 2,071 episodes across 710 sessions in 34 scenes, including 1,204 memory-dependent episodes. We also provide MemBridge, a modular baseline that combines semantic scene memory, cross-episode localization, and coarse-to-fine retrieval to support existing VLN agents. Experiments with state-of-the-art VLN methods demonstrate the benefits of MemBridge while revealing substantial room for improvement on memory-dependent navigation. The benchmark and baseline code are available at https://github.com/NJU-R-L-Group-Embodied-Lab/Mem-X.

</details>


### [3] [TrajRealm: A Large-Scale Point Tracking Dataset with Diverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27136)
*Ni Yang,Weiguang Zhao,Jiafei Cao,Yankai Jiang,Haoyu Guo,Tao Yang,Chunhua Shen,Junting Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出千万帧量级的合成点跟踪数据集 TrajRealm，融合游戏与具身仿真，提供 RGB、深度、相机参数及持续的 2D/3D 点轨迹标注，并构建 TrajRealm-Bench 基准，验证其可提升现有跟踪器在合成与真实基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用点跟踪需要大规模、动态多样的训练数据，但现有数据集难以同时兼顾丰富的动态内容、准确的场景几何与足够的规模，三者难以兼得。

Method: 从现代游戏与具身仿真中构建数据：利用游戏环境与运动系统采集多样目标、活动与交互，并用具身仿真补充机器人操作与室内导航数据；提供 RGB 观测、深度、相机参数以及前景物体与静态环境表面上的持续 2D/3D 点轨迹标注，且几何一致；同时构建含 Interaction 与 Locomotion 两大类共八个子类的 TrajRealm-Bench。

Result: Benchmark 评测揭示了现有跟踪器在复杂运动与长期点对应保持方面的优势与局限；在 TrajRealm 上训练可提升现有跟踪方法在合成与真实基准上的性能。

Conclusion: TrajRealm 是首个达到千万帧规模的公开合成点跟踪数据集，其高精度、几何一致的轨迹标注兼具训练与评测价值，可有效增强现有跟踪模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: General-purpose point tracking requires large-scale training data with diverse dynamics, yet existing datasets struggle to combine rich dynamic content, accurate scene geometry, and sufficient scale. We introduce TrajRealm, the first public synthetic point-tracking dataset to reach the ten-million-frame scale, constructed from games and embodied simulations. TrajRealm uses the rich environments and motion systems of modern games to capture diverse targets, activities, and interactions across diverse worlds, while embodied simulations add robot manipulation and indoor navigation data. The dataset provides RGB observations, depth, camera parameters, and persistent 2D and 3D point trajectories. These annotations provide precise, geometrically consistent supervision across foreground objects and static environmental surfaces. We also build TrajRealm-Bench with eight subcategories under Interaction and Locomotion to evaluate tracking across diverse scenes. Benchmark evaluations reveal the strengths and limitations of existing trackers in handling complex motion and maintaining long-term point correspondences. Training on TrajRealm improves the performance of existing tracking methods on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [4] [Rethinking Cross-Sensor Domain Adaptation for Bridge Point Cloud Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27060)
*Linghui Huang,Zipeng Wang,Shunjie Chen,Peng Guan,Jiandong Wang,Siqing Li,Luming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 复检SemanticBridge的TLS→MLS迁移协议，揭示8-cm稀疏体素表示下采样密度、粗糙度、颜色与视角的表达方式，并表明域适应增益依赖于源模型收敛程度与评估变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁点云分割模型在TLS数据上训练后迁移到MLS数据时精度下降，但此前结论（尤其是域适应方法的收益）未区分表示尺度、源模型收敛与评估噪声的贡献，需重新审视该协议的可靠性与可解释性。

Method: 采用单一稀疏卷积U-Net，结合原始点统计与受控输入扰动（如对TLS点施加4 mm扰动），比较自适应批归一化、熵最小化、Mean Teacher与置信度过滤自训练，在100与300 epoch训练计划下评估。

Result: 预处理后TLS与MLS最近邻间距相近（12.08 vs 12.02 mm），4 mm扰动仅改变4.4%体素占用；颜色分布系统性差异但置零仅带来+0.02 mIoU（尺度校正后+0.06）；两类传感器观测区域不同导致类别比例差异；300 epoch仅源域模型在配对桥梁协议上达57.80 mIoU；置信度自训练可提升100 epoch基线，但从收敛模型出发反而下降（源选检查点−0.97，末50 epoch均值−2.44）；重复评估标准差0.27–0.64，三随机种子间变异1.88。

Conclusion: 该基准上的域适应结果受表示尺度、源模型收敛程度与评估变异性共同支配，方法间差异常与随机波动同量级，比较需谨慎且应报告多种子与重复评估。

Abstract: Segmentation models for bridge point clouds lose accuracy when trained on terrestrial laser scanning (TLS) data and applied to mobile laser scanning (MLS) data. This study re-examines the SemanticBridge TLS-to-MLS protocol to determine how sampling density, surface roughness, color, and acquisition viewpoint are expressed in the 8-cm sparse-voxel representation, and how adaptation gains depend on source-model convergence. Using one sparse-convolution U-Net, we combine raw-point statistics with controlled input perturbations and compare adaptive batch normalization, entropy minimization, Mean Teacher, and confidence-filtered self-training under 100- and 300-epoch schedules. TLS and MLS have similar nearest-neighbor spacing after preprocessing (12.08 and 12.02 mm), and a 4-mm perturbation of TLS points changes voxel occupancy by only 4.4%. Color distributions differ systematically, but zeroing color on identical voxels changes target mean intersection over union (mIoU) by only +0.02, and by +0.06 after the color augmentation is scale-corrected. The sensors also observe different portions of the bridge, yielding different class proportions. A 300-epoch source-only model obtains 57.80 mIoU on the paired-bridge protocol. Confidence-filtered self-training improves the 100-epoch baseline, but from a converged model it changes target mIoU by -0.97 (source-selected checkpoints) and -2.44 (final-50-epoch means). Repeated evaluation (standard deviation 0.27-0.64) and seed variation (1.88 across three seeds) are comparable to several method differences. Adaptation results on this benchmark therefore depend on representation scale, source-model convergence, and evaluation variability.

</details>


### [5] [Towards Text-Mask Consistency in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27050)
*Jie Gui,Hang Tu,Wen Sha,Xiuquan Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出C2Seg两阶段分割框架，通过聚类感知对比学习和双向互补注意力缓解文本-掩码不一致问题，在四个公开医学影像数据集上显著提升一致性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割的视觉-语言模型常产生与文本冲突的掩码，尤其在多部位/多病灶描述下。原因有二：(i) 高度模板化、重复的临床语言使一对一硬对比学习产生大量假阴性，削弱跨模态对齐；(ii) 以视觉为主导的单向交叉注意力缺乏语言主导且具空间感知的通路，难以将文本语义有效注入空间视觉域。

Method: 两阶段框架：预训练阶段用冻结的强基线构建批内文本相似度矩阵作为软标签，实现聚类感知对比学习；融合阶段引入双向互补注意力模块，各模态在自身通路上主导注意力；并采用基于KAN的注意力门控增强多模态特征表达力；不更新语言编码器。

Result: 在四个公开医学影像数据集上显著提升文本-掩码一致性与分割精度，且无需更新语言编码器。

Conclusion: 通过软标签对比学习与双向注意力设计可有效改善跨模态对齐与结构一致性，为文本引导医学分割提供参数高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models for medical image segmentation often produce masks that conflict with the accompanying text, especially under multi-site/multi-lesion descriptions. We trace this failure to two factors: (i) highly templated and repetitive clinical language causes one-to-one hard contrastive learning to yield numerous false negatives, weakening cross-modal alignment; and (ii) predominantly vision-driven, one-way cross-attention lacks a language-dominant, spatially aware pathway, hindering effective injection of textual semantics into the spatial visual domain. To this end, we propose Consistency-enhanced Two-stage Segmentation (C2Seg). In the pretraining stage, Cluster-aware Contrastive Learning uses a frozen strong baseline to construct an intra-batch text similarity matrix as soft labels, thereby alleviating false negative conflicts and producing more discriminative visual representations. In the fusion stage, we introduce a Bidirectional Complementary Attention Module, where each modality dominates attention along its own path, fostering deep interaction and structural consistency between visual and textual representations. In order to enhance the expressive power of multimodal features, we further adopt KAN-based Attention Gating. Without updating the language encoder, our approach significantly improves text-mask consistency and segmentation accuracy on four public medical imaging datasets. The source code is available at https://github.com/JGuiidev/C2Seg.

</details>


### [6] [TSIR: Ray-Traced Triangle Splats for Inverse Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27030)
*Wentao Yang,Chentao Shen,Zhengqi Zhang,Bolin Chen,Yan Ye,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段逆渲染方法 TSIR，从多视图图像重建可重光照的三角 splat，通过直接光线追踪表面估计材质，提升重光照质量并高效抗锯齿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆渲染支持视觉特效与增强现实中的真实重光照，但现有基于高斯图元的方法存在重叠高斯导致表面恢复困难、且需昂贵的多层遍历与合成来模拟光传输的问题。

Method: 两阶段流程：第一阶段构建带可学习顶点着色法线的不透明三角 splat；第二阶段直接对这些表面做光线追踪，通过直接光照与间接反射的蒙特卡洛积分估计材质，减少对多层合成的依赖；并引入抗锯齿以在保持效率的同时提升渲染质量。

Result: 实验表明重光照质量优于所评估的基线方法，且抗锯齿比超采样更高效。

Conclusion: 以三角 splat 替代重叠高斯的可追踪表面表示，可有效简化光传输计算并兼顾质量与效率，为逆渲染重光照提供实用方案。

Abstract: Inverse rendering enables realistic relighting for visual effects and augmented reality. However, overlapping Gaussian primitives complicate surface recovery and require costly multi-layer traversal and compositing for light transport. We propose TSIR, a two-stage inverse rendering method that reconstructs relightable triangle splats from multi-view images. The first stage prepares opaque triangle splats with learnable vertex shading normals. The second stage directly traces these surfaces to estimate materials through Monte Carlo integration of direct illumination and indirect reflections, reducing reliance on multi-layer compositing. Anti-aliasing improves rendering quality while maintaining efficiency. Experiments demonstrate higher relighting quality than the evaluated baselines and more efficient anti-aliasing than supersampling.

</details>


### [7] [ReactDrive: Self-Reactive Driving World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27024)
*Yifan Wang,Xiaoqi Wang,Cho-Ying Wu,Wenbin He,Chenyu You,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出驾驶世界模型的自反应性（self-reactivity）概念，并给出ReactDrive框架：通过共享智能体状态表示联合学习智能体动力学与视频生成，实现由自车动作驱动的反应式未来预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界由实体及其交互的动力学演化，交互式世界模型需在给定动作下刻画其他智能体的反应。自动驾驶中若反应建模错误，会误判机动安全性，削弱生成视频用于策略评估与学习的可靠性。为此需正式定义“自反应性”这一内在能力要求。

Method: 定义self-reactivity为：基于观测历史与指定自车动作建模智能体反应，并在不预设未来智能体轨迹的条件下生成反映该反应的视觉未来。实现上提出ReactDrive，用共享的智能体状态表示联合学习动力学与视觉生成：动力学监督将反应式动力学先验锚定在观测到的智能体状态上，视频目标同时优化自反应性与视觉真实感。

Result: 实验显示智能体反应、自车可控性与视频质量均有提升；在五条基线的人体对比中，智能体反应性偏好率达96.0%；将生成视频用于数据增强可改善下游规划。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区将self-reactivity视为交互式世界模型开发与评估的基础能力。

Abstract: The world evolves through the dynamics of its constituent entities and their interactions, with changes in one entity influencing the behavior of others. To simulate this evolution under different actions, interactive world models need to capture the resulting agent responses as part of the world's own dynamics. In autonomous driving, errors in modeling these responses can misrepresent maneuver safety and undermine the reliability of generated videos used for policy evaluation and learning. To formalize this intrinsic requirement for driving world model, we define self-reactivity as the capability to model agent responses from observed history and a specified ego action, and generate visual futures that reflect those responses without prescribed future agent trajectories. To realize this capability, we introduce ReactDrive, a driving world model that jointly learns agent dynamics and visual generation through a shared agent state representation. Within this framework, dynamics supervision grounds a reactive dynamics prior in observed agent states, while the video objective jointly optimizes the self-reactivity and visual realism. Experiments show improved agent responses, ego controllability, and video quality, a 96.0% preference for agent reactivity in human comparisons across five baselines, and better downstream planning through augmentation with generated videos. We hope this work encourages the community to recognize self-reactivityas a foundational capability for developing and evaluating interactive world models. Project page: https://reactdrive.github.io/ .

</details>


### [8] [Real-Time On-Drone Hyperspectral Anomaly Detection for Wilderness Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27017)
*Jacqueline Liu,Gyabaah Kyere,Chengyan Liu,Kenneth Chiu,Lynn Marie,Church,Sean Gleason,Jayson Boubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SABL是一种面向无人机端的边缘计算流水线，结合ERX高光谱异常检测与DBSCAN空间聚类，在真实荒野搜救部署中实现了满足相机10ms采集节拍的实时处理，并有效筛除背景、定位难以察觉的搜索指示物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荒野搜救中，失踪人员指示物往往稀少、与背景混叠或无热特征，UAS常规手段难以发现；高光谱成像信息丰富但数据量大，难以在无人机端实时处理，因此需要一种可机载运行的实时检测与定位方案。

Method: 提出SABL流水线：将高光谱流式采集与指数滑动Reed-Xiaoli（ERX）异常检测相结合，再用DBSCAN对像素级异常进行空间滤波聚类，形成候选搜索区域；并在阿拉斯加偏远地区与Quinhagak搜救队（QSAR）为期10天的部署中采集的真实数据进行评估。

Result: 处理高光谱扫描线耗时5.67–5.73 ms/行，满足相机10 ms采集间隔；DBSCAN每100行更新仅需6.5–12.5 ms。在两个场景中，对象级精确率约56%与99%，对应召回率55%与29%，在显著过滤背景的同时识别出难以发现的异常指示物。

Conclusion: SABL验证了高光谱异常检测在无人机边缘端实时搜救的可行性，兼具实时性与背景抑制能力，但不同场景下召回率波动较大（55% vs 29%），跨场景鲁棒性仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Wilderness search-and-rescue (WSAR) teams use uncrewed aerial systems (UASs) to survey terrain for indicators of missing persons that are rare, blend into the background, or lack thermal signatures. Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) provides rich spectral information, but its high data volume challenges real-time on-drone processing. We present SABL, an edge-computing pipeline for on-drone Spectral Anomaly-Based Localization. SABL integrates hyperspectral streaming with exponentially moving Reed-Xiaoli (ERX) anomaly detection and DBSCAN spatial filtering to group pixel-level anomalies into candidate search regions. SABL is evaluated on data collected during a 10-day deployment with Quinhagak Search and Rescue (QSAR) in remote Alaska. It processes hyperspectral scanlines at 5.67-5.73 ms per line, meeting the camera's 10-ms acquisition interval; DBSCAN requires only 6.5-12.5 ms per 100-line update for filtering. SABL achieves approximately 56% and 99% object-level precision on QSAR-selected search indicators across two scenes, with corresponding recall of 55% and 29%, providing significant background filtering while identifying difficult-to-see anomalous search indicators.

</details>


### [9] [SceneRuler: Measuring Physical Quantities in Video via Minimal Sufficient Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26994)
*Yumeng He,Gezheng Kang,Fanlian Zeng,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneRuler tackles quantitative physical reasoning from video by reconstructing a minimal, measurable 3D scene and answering questions grounded on that explicit reconstruction, achieving 0.709 mean relative accuracy on QuantiPhy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Many video questions require numeric answers (height, distance, speed), but end-to-end video-language models lack explicit metric scale and physical consistency, and their errors cannot be inspected or traced.

Method: Proposes Minimal Sufficient Reconstruction: for each question, build only the smallest scene sufficient to measure the asked quantity, written as a program whose structure a VLM writes while magnitudes are left to measurement. Physics-guided Fitting measures parameters via physical motion laws (gravity anchors metric scale), and marks each parameter and the read-out as determined or undetermined. Outputs a read-out (asked quantity evaluated on the scene; empty if undetermined) plus a final answer from a model using the scene as evidence.

Result: On QuantiPhy validation split, final answer reaches 0.709 mean relative accuracy vs. 0.624 for the strongest baseline; reconstruction determines the asked quantity on 47 of 158 questions, where the answer reaches 0.781. Also applied to 27 in-the-wild videos without ground truth.

Conclusion: Explicit intermediate quantities make every answer traceable to the objects, scale, and motion behind it, improving both accuracy and interpretability over end-to-end baselines.

Abstract: Many questions about videos ask for a number, such as how tall a person is, how far a cart travels, or how fast a ball leaves a ramp. We study this task, quantitative physical reasoning from video, where the inputs are a video, a question, and a prior of known facts such as object sizes, speeds, and distances from the camera to named objects. End-to-end video-language models lack an explicit metric scale and physical consistency, and their errors cannot be inspected. We present SceneRuler, which instead recovers a measurable physical scene and answers with that scene as evidence. Its central principle, Minimal Sufficient Reconstruction, builds, for each question, only the smallest scene that suffices to measure the asked quantity, and tests sufficiency on measurements rather than assuming it. The scene is written as a program whose structure a vision-language model writes and whose magnitudes are left to measurement. Physics-guided Fitting measures them through physical motion laws, so that gravity itself can anchor the metric scale, and marks every parameter, and the read-out of the asked quantity, as determined or undetermined. Each question receives a read-out and an answer. The read-out is the asked quantity evaluated on the scene, and it is empty when the scene does not fix that quantity. The answer is always given by a model that takes the scene as evidence. We evaluate on QuantiPhy and also apply the method to 27 in-the-wild videos that we collected, which have no ground-truth answers. On its validation split, the final answer reaches a mean relative accuracy of 0.709 against 0.624 for the strongest baseline, and the reconstruction determines the asked quantity on 47 of 158 questions, where the final answer reaches 0.781. Because every intermediate quantity is explicit, each answer can be traced to the objects, scale, and motion behind it.

</details>


### [10] [Causal Diffusion for Extreme Super-Resolution in Interactive Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26963)
*Christina Zhang,Jenna Kang,Colin Groth,Akshay Jindal,Stavros Diolatzis,Qi Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CIDER：面向交互式游戏的因果扩散框架，利用当前及历史低分辨率帧和G-buffer引导，实现从120p到960p的8×极端超分辨率（约13 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏超分方法多在中等放大倍数下工作，难以从极低分辨率（如120p）输入中恢复细节；此时超分任务从"重建已有信息"转变为"合成缺失视觉细节"。视频扩散模型虽具备强生成先验，但依赖未来帧，无法用于交互式游戏。

Method: 提出因果扩散框架CIDER：仅利用当前与过去低分辨率帧合成高频细节，并以G-buffer（深度图与运动向量）引导，保持场景结构与时间一致性。为降低推理负载，设计适配超低分辨率输入的轻量隐空间投影编码器，在高分辨率输出上微调解码器（TAE），并对扩散模型进行蒸馏以达到交互帧率。数据采集自《赛博朋克2077》与《黑神话：悟空》两款3A游戏。

Result: 实验实现120p至960p的8×高保真超分，约13 FPS，在视觉质量上优于现有最先进游戏超分器，并展现出16×放大的潜力。

Conclusion: 因果扩散结合G-buffer引导与工程加速，可在交互帧率下实现极端倍率的游戏超分，为低分辨率实时渲染提供可行路径。

Abstract: Existing game super-resolution methods typically operate at moderate upscaling factors, struggling to recover fine visual details from extremely low-resolution inputs (e.g., 120p). At such resolutions, super-resolution shifts from reconstructing existing information toward synthesizing missing visual details. While video diffusion models offer powerful generative priors for this challenging task, their dependence on future frames limits their applicability to interactive games. We present CIDER, a causal diffusion framework for extreme game super-resolution that synthesizes high-frequency details from current and past low-resolution frames, guided by G-buffers (depth and motion vectors) to preserve scene structure and temporal consistency. We collect data from two AAA games (Cyberpunk 2077 and Black Myth: Wukong). To reduce inference load, we develop a lightweight latent projection encoder tailored to ultra-low-resolution inputs, fine-tune a decoder (TAE) on our high-resolution outputs, and distill the diffusion model to reach interactive frame rates. Our experiments demonstrate high-fidelity 8$\times$ upscaling from 120p to 960p at around 13 FPS, outperforming state-of-the-art game upscalers in visual quality and showing potential for 16$\times$ magnification.

</details>


### [11] [Cecilia: Towards Layout-Inclusive Optical Music Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27465)
*Congren Dai,Zhancheng Guo,Xiaobing Li,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出紧凑的规范化 MusicXML 表示 Cecilia，并设计 Cecilia-OMR 将其识别分解为元数据与按小节切分的音乐片段，借助共享视觉记忆预测；在统一分词器下 MusicXML/Cecilia 平均序列长度比为 27.73，OpenScore 四条件宏平均归一化编辑距离 0.2232，优于 Legato 2 的 0.2998。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重建可编辑乐谱需同时包含音乐事件、歌词文本及页面布局指令，而现有识别目标常遗漏部分信息；直接预测 MusicXML 又会重复大量结构性标记，序列冗长。

Method: 引入 Cecilia——保留音乐内容、歌词附着与布局指令的规范化 MusicXML 紧凑表示；Cecilia-OMR 在共享视觉记忆基础上，以元数据与按小节边界的音乐片段为预测单元，并用可学习计数头决定输出片段数。

Result: 同一分词器下 MusicXML 与 Cecilia 平均序列长度比为 27.73；在 Camera String Quartets 与两个 Lieder 条件上优于 Legato 2，OpenScore 四条件宏平均归一化编辑距离 0.2232（对比 0.2998）。对歌词、记谱树与渲染布局的分项评测揭示了重构的互补方面，并量化了布局指令的贡献；训练阶段、奖励与候选选择实验进一步考察了模型适配与解码过程中的识别变化。

Conclusion: 紧凑规范表示结合分段式视觉预测可显著压缩输出序列并提升乐谱识别精度；多视角评测表明内容、歌词与布局应分开评估，布局指令对完整重建具有可量化贡献。

Abstract: Reconstructing an editable music score requires musical events, sung text and the instructions that organise them on the page. Existing recognition targets often omit part of this information, while direct MusicXML prediction repeats substantial structural markup. We introduce Cecilia, a compact representation of canonicalised MusicXML that retains musical content, lyric attachments and layout instructions. Cecilia-OMR predicts this representation through metadata and measure-bounded music segments from shared visual memory. A learned count head determines the number of output segments. Across paired complete scores under a common tokeniser, the ratio of mean MusicXML to Cecilia sequence lengths is 27.73. Cecilia-OMR outperforms Legato 2 on Camera String Quartets and both Lieder conditions, with a four-condition OpenScore macro normalised edit distance of 0.2232 against 0.2998. Separate evaluations of lyrics, notation trees and rendered layout reveal complementary aspects of reconstruction and quantify the contribution of layout instructions. Training-stage, reward and candidate-selection studies further examine how recognition changes across model adaptation and decoding.

</details>


### [12] [What Stays in the Picture? From Redundant Instructions to Dependency-Aware Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27388)
*Zi-Jun Wang,Yu Lin,Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"复杂冗余指令图像编辑"任务并提出CRIE-Agent，通过将修订历史归约为对象级最终意图来联合指导规划与验证，在自建CRIE-S基准上使完整编辑成功率由直接编辑的0%提升至27.5%–50.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单条请求中常包含多对象、属性、空间关系，并伴随对先前编辑要求的修订或取消。直接编辑难以还原真实意图；而将请求拆分为逐步执行的子步骤又会保留已废弃的编辑并累积视觉错误。

Method: 核心思路是将修订历史解析为"最终编辑目标"，使其同时指导规划与验证。CRIE-Agent据此学习将修订合并为对象级最终意图，并按"引用优先"分块执行（先建立被引用对象，再处理依赖对象）；验证阶段以条件化布局提示引导从源图像的完整重执行，避免失败尝试的视觉错误被带入后续步骤。同时构建CRIE-S基准，含10,000条复杂冗余编辑指令及精确最终意图标注。

Result: 在CRIE-S上评测五种图像编辑后端，CRIE-Agent将完整编辑成功率从直接编辑的0%提升至27.5%–50.0%。

Conclusion: 显式建模并归约修订历史、结合引用优先的块式执行与从源图的条件化重执行验证，是处理复杂冗余编辑指令的有效范式；但绝对成功率仍较低，该任务尚存较大提升空间。

Abstract: We introduce complex redundant-instruction image editing, where a single request specifies multiple objects, their attributes, and spatial relations while revising or canceling earlier editing requirements. Direct editing can fail to recover this intent, while splitting the request into successive steps can preserve obsolete edits and visual errors. Our key insight is to resolve the revision history into a final editing target that jointly guides planning and verification, so that obsolete requests do not become visual edits. Based on this insight, CRIE-Agent learns to consolidate revisions into object-level final intent and executes the retained objects in reference-first blocks, establishing referenced objects before their dependents. Verification then guides complete re-execution from the source image, with conditional layout hints, preventing visual errors from failed attempts from being carried into subsequent ones. We also construct CRIE-S, a benchmark containing 10,000 complex and redundant editing instructions with exact final intent annotations. Across five image-editing backends on CRIE-S, CRIE-Agent raises complete editing success from 0% under direct editing to 27.5--50.0%.

</details>


### [13] [Unimodal Shortcuts and Joint Scene-Intent Dependence in Vision-Language Safety Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27339)
*Guijia Zhang,Hongbo Qiu,Guohao Cai,Hao Zheng,Jiajun Zha,Hanqing Wang,Zhenhao Zhang,Huihao Jing,Shing-Chi Cheung,Haoxuan Che,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有情境安全基准存在单模态捷径：仅凭图像或仅凭文本即可高准确率预测安全标签；作者构建了安全/危险场景×观察/行动请求的交叉控制基准，使任何单模态规则的最高分组排序准确率不超过0.500，并借此审计冻结的VLM安全表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM请求的安全性往往取决于图像（新鲜食物无害、腐败食物有害），而高基准准确率无法说明安全表征是否真正编码了"场景+意图"的联合信息，因为基准常常可以只靠单一模态求解。作者要检验既有情境安全基准是否被单模态捷径"刷分"。

Method: 审计5个公开情境安全基准，验证单模态捷径（图像单独可预测标签；同图提示对上不看图像的探针达0.9645 AUROC）；构建受控交叉评测（安全/危险场景×观察/行动请求），保证无单模态规则可超0.500分组排序准确率；在Qwen3-VL-8B与SmolVLM2-2.2B中间层状态上训练线性探针，并对比分别编码的图像/文本状态拼接。

Result: 控制基准上线性探针达0.958与0.930分组排序准确率，表明"场景–意图"合取是线性可解码的；拼接独立编码的图像与文本状态已能恢复大部分信号；探针廉价但泛化差（对未见图像与其他基准）；单模态探针在同图提示对上达0.9645 AUROC。

Conclusion: 可解码性不等于融合机制存在；多模态安全评测在将准确率解读为联合理解证据之前，必须引入排除单模态捷径的对照设计。

Abstract: The safety of a request to a vision-language model often depends on the image: asking how to serve a dish is harmless for fresh food and harmful for spoiled food. High accuracy on situational safety benchmarks does not tell us whether a model's safety representation encodes both the scene and the user's intent, because a benchmark can often be solved from one modality alone. Studying frozen vision-language safety representations, we audit five public situational benchmarks and find that each admits a unimodal shortcut: images alone predict the labels, and on same-image prompt pairs a probe that never sees the photograph reaches 0.9645 AUROC. We therefore build a controlled evaluation that crosses safe and hazardous scenes with observation and action requests, so that no single-modality rule can exceed 0.500 group-ranking accuracy. On this control, linear probes on intermediate states of Qwen3-VL-8B and SmolVLM2-2.2B reach 0.958 and 0.930, showing that the scene-intent conjunction is linearly decodable; concatenating separately encoded image and text states already recovers most of this signal, so decodability is not evidence of a fusion mechanism. The same probes screen cheaply but generalize poorly to unseen images and other benchmarks. Reliable multimodal safety evaluation therefore needs controls that rule out unimodal shortcuts before accuracy is read as evidence of joint understanding.

</details>


### [14] [GAAFormer: Geometry-Anchored Attention Transformer for Joint Point Cloud Denoising and Upsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27281)
*Haoxi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GAAFormer，一个几何锚定注意力的统一八叉树生成式Transformer，用同一网络同时完成点云去噪与上采样，并保持骨架的参数规模不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云去噪与上采样都要求从噪声或稀疏观测中准确恢复底层曲面，核心难点是在细化点位置的同时保持表面覆盖度；且两类任务通常需要分别设计网络，缺乏统一架构。

Method: GAAFormer采用统一生成式八叉树Transformer，编码器—解码器共享。解码器将注意力邻域锚定到几何上，为占用预测与点生成提供空间上下文；用固定体素窗口与交替移位，在输出树变化时保持存活节点的分组一致性。设计上保留串行化注意力骨架的编码器、位置编码、预测头与参数量，并给出邻域规则的删除一致性性质。

Result: PUNet上去噪平均CD为6.0643×10⁻⁵，相对串行化骨架CD降低3.35%、Hausdorff距离降低19.34%；上采样在PU-GAN、PU1K、Sketchfab上CD分别为0.8475、1.7057、1.0147（×10⁻⁵），在PU-GAN与Sketchfab上CD、Hausdorff与点到面距离均优于同骨架。

Conclusion: 共享编码器—解码器中同一几何锚定注意力模块即可同时提升去噪与稠密点生成，无需任务特定重设计或额外参数，确立了单一架构处理两类任务的可行性。

Abstract: Point cloud denoising and upsampling require samples that accurately recover the underlying surface from noisy or sparse observations. A central challenge in both tasks is preserving surface coverage while refining point locations. We introduce GAAFormer (Geometry-Anchored Attention Transformer), a unified generative octree transformer that performs both tasks with a shared network. Its decoder anchors attention neighborhoods to geometry, providing spatial context for occupancy prediction and point generation. Fixed voxel windows and alternating shifts preserve the grouping of surviving nodes as the output tree changes. The design retains the encoder, positional encoding, prediction heads, and parameter count of a serialized-attention backbone. We establish a deletion-consistency property of the neighborhood rule and evaluate both tasks using the same model. On PUNet, GAAFormer achieves a mean Chamfer distance (CD) of $6.0643\times10^{-5}$, reducing CD and Hausdorff distance by 3.35% and 19.34%, respectively, relative to the serialized backbone. For upsampling, it achieves CD values of $0.8475$, $1.7057$, and $1.0147$ ($\times10^{-5}$) on PU-GAN, PU1K, and Sketchfab, respectively, with improvements in CD, Hausdorff distance, and point-to-surface distance over the same backbone on PU-GAN and Sketchfab. These results establish a single architecture for point cloud denoising and upsampling. Within the shared encoder-decoder, the same geometry-anchored attention module improves both noise removal and dense point generation without task-specific redesign or additional parameters.

</details>


### [15] [Looking Right Is Not Being Right: Evaluating the Geometry of Agent-Built 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26937)
*Ziyang Long,Yen-Liang Lin,Shifan Zhu,Allen Tu,Meher Gitika Karumuri,Chenqi Li,Srikumar Sastry,Sarosij Bose,Mellon M. Zhang,Hsin-Jung Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出激光扫描为参照的几何评测（0-100分，30个真实房间），发现视觉语言智能体从视频重建的可编辑3D场景几何精度有限（GPT-6 Astra 51.4 vs 稠密重建75.9），且会继承输入误差；智能体虽频繁测量输入却很少与自建场景对比，为此引入几何误差报告，可使Terra与Sol的修订增益分别提升3.9与5.5分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言智能体能从视频重建可编辑3D房间场景并渲染出可信图像，但"渲染逼真"不等于"几何准确"，缺乏以真实扫描为基准的定量评测，也不清楚几何输入的作用及智能体能否自行校验纠错。

Method: 构建以30个真实房间激光扫描为参照的几何评分（综合表面、深度、家具摆放一致性，0-100）；开展受控实验：纯视频重建 vs 同视频稠密重建 vs 给定点云的重建，并人为缩放输入几何以检验误差继承；分析工具调用日志考察修订行为；提出几何误差报告，将每个保存场景与智能体输入点云比对并列出最大偏差。

Result: 最强智能体GPT-6 Astra仅凭视频得51.4分，同视频稠密重建为75.9分；给定该点云时Astra场景与点云相差不到3分。缩放输入几何会导致场景尺度近似等比例变化，说明误差被继承。修订阶段查看渲染对表观改善更稳定，对几何改善有限；日志显示智能体反复测量输入但很少与所建场景比对。加入误差报告后，在不提供新几何证据的情况下，GPT-6.1 Terra与Sol的修订增益较同期对照组分别提升3.9与5.5分。

Conclusion: 渲染可信度不能代表几何精度，需扫描参照的量化评测；智能体的几何能力高度依赖输入几何质量并继承其误差，且缺乏自校验；一个简洁的几何误差报告即可显著改善修订效果，提示"输入—产物一致性检查"是提升智能体重建可靠性的关键方向。

Abstract: Vision-language agents can reconstruct rooms from video as editable 3D scenes, but convincing renders do not establish geometric accuracy. We introduce a scan-referenced evaluation and use controlled experiments to study reconstruction accuracy, the role of geometric inputs, and agents' ability to verify and correct their scenes. Our geometric score measures surface, depth, and furniture placement agreement with laser scans of 30 real rooms on a 0-100 scale. The strongest tested agent, GPT-6 Astra, scores 51.4 from video alone, while a dense reconstruction from the same video scores 75.9. Given the point cloud used for that reconstruction, Astra produces an editable scene that scores within three points of it. Agents also inherit input errors: rescaling the supplied geometry changes scene scale almost proportionally. During revision, viewing renders improves appearance more consistently than geometry. Logs of their tool calls show that agents repeatedly measure their inputs but rarely compare them with the scenes they build. To provide this missing check, we introduce a geometric error report that compares each saved scene with the agent's input points and lists the largest discrepancies. Without new geometric evidence, the report raises the revision gains of GPT-6.1 Terra and Sol by 3.9 and 5.5 points over concurrent controls.

</details>


### [16] [Effectiveness of Synthetic Defects for Anomaly Detection in Industrial Small Load Carriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26868)
*Vincent Wahyudi,Kuniko Paxton,Amila Akagić,Martin Storath,Dhavalkumar Thakker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统评估了用程序化噪声合成缺陷来训练工业异常检测模型的有效性，兼含监督与无监督方法、不同噪声类型，并与真实SLC数据集表现对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工业异常检测方法常用程序化噪声生成合成缺陷，但其在真实场景中的有效性、不同噪声生成方式的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 在监督与无监督设定下均仅用合成缺陷训练；比较不同噪声生成方法（Perlin噪声、自定义配方、fBm配方及另外九种噪声类型）；并在基准数据集与真实小型装载架（SLC）数据集上对比检测性能，辅以特征空间角度分析。

Result: 仅用合成缺陷训练时，监督分类器在真实SLC缺陷上达0.65 AUROC，而采用同类Perlin噪声的三种无监督方法达0.87–0.90；除SuperSimpleNet在使用前景受限的fBm配方时失效外，自定义配方或其余九种噪声与Perlin噪声训练的结果无可靠差异；基准数据集上的合成缺陷结果无法迁移到SLC数据集，可能因合成缺陷与真实缺陷差异过大（特征空间角度更大）。

Conclusion: 对合成缺陷图像训练模型应持谨慎态度：无监督方法优于监督方法，噪声类型选择影响有限，但基准数据集的合成缺陷结论难以推广到真实产线数据。

Abstract: Recent industrial anomaly detection methods use procedural noise to generate synthetic training defects. Their effectiveness in real-world settings and the impact of different noise generation methods have not been sufficiently investigated. We evaluate the effectiveness of training using synthetic defects for both supervised and unsupervised methods, the impact of different noise generation methods, and the differences in detection performance between benchmark datasets and a real-world small load carrier (SLC) dataset. Trained on synthetic defects only, a supervised classifier reaches 0.65 AUROC on real SLC defects, while three unsupervised methods using the same kind of noise (Perlin) reach 0.87--0.90. Except for SuperSimpleNet, which fails with the foreground-confined fractal Brownian motion (fBm) recipe, models trained with the custom recipe or with nine other noise types do not differ reliably from those trained with Perlin noise. Finally, results with synthetic defects on benchmark datasets do not carry over to the SLC dataset, possibly because the synthetic defects differ too much from the real ones (a larger angle in feature space). These results call for caution when training models on synthetic defect images.

</details>


### [17] [FACET: A Data-Efficient Regional Parametric Head Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26773)
*Mathieu Lamarre,Elly Akhoundi,Thibault Goyallon,Patrick Anderson,Étienne Danvoye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FACET 是一个参数化头部模型，在固定、少量身份数据下，通过"架构局部性"（16个解剖区域身份分解 + 学习式FACS表达式模型 + 独立颈肩区域）显著优于匹配的全局PCA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级面部管线需要可表达、FACS可控、且能在少量固定身份池上构建的参数化头部模型。增加训练身份始终有益，但高质量配准头部扫描采集成本高昂；当数据无法扩展时，架构（而非数据）成为唯一可用的杠杆。

Method: 在自有拓扑、训练集与归一化下，将每项设计与匹配的全局PCA消融进行隔离对比：(1) 将身份空间分解为16个解剖区域的区域身份分解；(2) 带逐身份神经个性化的学习式FACS动作单元表情模型；(3) 专用颈肩区域。

Result: (1) 区域分解绕过全局PCA的N-1秩上限，将留出中性拟合误差从1.95 mm的下限降至0.23 mm（同一批训练身份）；(2) 学习式FACS表情模型在所有分量数下均优于恰当正则化的全局表情PCA，证明FACS并非因过完备而无法拟合；(3) 颈肩区域减少了解姿时姿态泄漏到姿态不变的上半脸。

Conclusion: 在固定身份预算下，架构局部性是构建生产级头部模型的有效杠杆。

Abstract: Production face pipelines need parametric head models that are expressive, FACS-controllable, and buildable on a small, fixed pool of identities. More training identities would always help, but high-quality registered head scans are expensive to capture; when scaling the data is not an option, the remaining lever is architecture. We present FACET, a parametric head model that makes the most of a fixed identity pool through architectural locality rather than more scans, and makes three contributions, each isolated against a matched global-PCA ablation on our own topology, training set, and normalization. (1) A regional identity decomposition into 16 anatomical regions bypasses the N-1 rank ceiling of a global PCA, driving held-out neutral-fit error from a 1.95 mm floor down to 0.23 mm on the same training identities. (2) A learned FACS action-unit expression model with per-identity neural personalization not only stays FACS-controllable but beats a properly-regularized global expression PCA at every component count -- refuting the argument that FACS is too overcomplete to fit. (3) A dedicated neck/shoulder region reduces pose leakage into the pose-invariant upper face during unposing. Together, these show that architectural locality is the effective lever for a production head model under a fixed identity budget.

</details>


### [18] [INCISE: Training-Free In-Context Segmentation of Surgical Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26734)
*Yifan Xu,Bofang Zheng,Guangyu Meng,Nishchal Sapkota,Xianshi Ma,Xu Cao,Tianjiao Yu,Danny Z Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的手术视频上下文分割框架INCISE，通过跨视频挑选能共同覆盖目标视频局部外观的参考帧，仅用1.7%训练帧标注即达67.2% mIoU，超越最优微调方法9.2个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术视频分析需要视频目标分割（VOS），但现有方法依赖大量专家标注对预训练模型进行微调，成本高昂。上下文分割（ICS）可用少量已标注参考帧分割目标视频、无需微调，但这些参考是冻结模型的唯一手术知识来源；现有ICS方法通常随机抽取参考帧，集中于主导手术视频的少数视角，易遗漏瞬时器械、细小结构等其他感兴趣目标。

Method: INCISE是一个免训练的手术视频ICS框架：从多个手术视频中挑选未标注帧，使其联合覆盖目标视频的局部外观（local appearances），随后用这些参考帧对目标视频进行分割；目标视频本身不做任何帧标注。

Result: 仅选取并标注1.7%的训练帧作为参考、目标视频零标注，INCISE取得67.2%平均mIoU，比最佳SOTA微调方法高出9.2个点。

Conclusion: INCISE有望推动ICS成为手术视频分析及其他标注负担昂贵领域中替代微调的实用方案。

Abstract: Surgical video analysis requires video object segmentation (VOS), yet existing methods are commonly based on pretrained model fine-tuning using a large number of expert labels. In-context segmentation (ICS) offers the promise of bypassing this high cost by segmenting a target video using a few annotated references, which are the sole source of surgical knowledge for a frozen model. However, existing ICS methods tend to draw such references at random, which concentrate on a few views that dominate a surgical video and can miss other objects of interest (e.g., transient instruments and small structures). To address these challenges, we propose INCISE, a training-free ICS framework for surgical videos. INCISE selects unlabeled frames from multiple surgical videos that jointly cover the local appearances of a target video, and then segments the target video using these references. With only 1.7% of training frames selected and annotated as references and no target video frame labeled, INCISE obtains 67.2% mean mIoU and outperforms the best SOTA fine-tuned method by 9.2 points. We hope INCISE can help establish ICS as a practical alternative to fine-tuning in surgical video analysis and other domains with a costly labeling burden.

</details>


### [19] [SCATR-ITS: a scalable transformer for large-area individual tree segmentation from LiDAR point clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26715)
*Lassi Ruoppa,Verneri Seppänen,Antero Kukko,Juha Hyyppä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCATR-ITS 是一个面向大范围机载 LiDAR 点云的超点级 Transformer 单木分割模型，在保持 SOTA 精度的同时显著加速推理并降低显存占用，已在地市级实用尺度验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度学习的单木分割（ITS）虽在多数基准上达到最优精度，但计算复杂度高、推理耗时长，难以用于大规模生产化流程，而传统无监督方法精度与分割质量不足。

Method: 在 ForestFormer3D 框架基础上，将处理粒度转为基于学习嵌入构建的超点（superpoint）级以加速推理；引入尺寸感知的超点级后处理、可动态调整查询数量的模块以应对林分结构剧烈变化，以及最大输入步长以最小化预测冗余。

Result: 在 FGI-EMIT 与 FOR-InstanceV2 数据集上精度与 SOTA 持平，推理速度最高提升 8.5×；训练与推理的 GPU 显存占用显著降低，并具良好零样本泛化；应用于芬兰 142 km² 国家激光扫描数据，平均吞吐 13.8 min/km²，较 ForestFormer3D 快约 18.6×，接近分水岭算法速度但精度与分割质量远优。

Conclusion: SCATR-ITS 在可支撑生产化部署的速度下达到 SOTA 精度，为大范围乃至全国尺度的单木分割铺平道路；代码与预训练权重已开源。

Abstract: Individual tree segmentation (ITS) from LiDAR point clouds is one of the most widely studied segmentation tasks in forestry. Although deep learning methods define the state of the art on the majority of ITS benchmarks, their high computational complexity and long runtime currently prevent their adoption in large-scale operational workflows. In this work, we present SCATR-ITS, a scalable transformer model specifically designed for efficient ITS over large geographical areas. Based on the ForestFormer3D framework, SCATR-ITS transitions to superpoint-level processing to accelerate inference, with the superpoints constructed from learned embeddings. The architecture incorporates size-aware superpoint-level postprocessing, a dynamic query count module to handle high variation in forest structure, and a maximal input stride designed to minimize prediction redundancy. SCATR-ITS achieves accuracy on par with the state of the art on the FGI-EMIT and FOR-InstanceV2 datasets with an up to 8.5$\times$ higher inference speed. In addition, SCATR-ITS has a significantly lower GPU VRAM footprint during both training and inference, and exhibits excellent zero-shot generalization across multiple datasets. To demonstrate large-area ITS capabilities, SCATR-ITS is applied to a 142 km$^2$ region of Finnish national laser scanning data where it achieves an average throughput of 13.8 min/km$^2$, $\sim$18.6$\times$ faster than ForestFormer3D and approaching the speed of the unsupervised watershed algorithm, which it vastly outperforms in accuracy and segment quality. Overall, SCATR-ITS delivers state-of-the-art accuracy at speeds where operational-scale adoption becomes viable, paving the way toward countrywide deployment. The source code for SCATR-ITS and pretrained weights are available at https://github.com/ruoppa/scatr_its.

</details>


### [20] [EchoAlign: Echocardiography foundation model with multilevel report alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27517)
*Ahmed Aly,Darya Taratynova,Lotfi Abdelkrim Mecharbat,Roman A. Sandler,Joseph Z. Sokol,Joshua Penn,Klaus H. Maier-Hein,Numan Saeed,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoAlign是一个基于287万超声心动图影像数据的视觉-语言基础模型，通过可见性矩阵将监督限制在真正显示某结构的视图上，实现结构与文本对齐，在10个外部数据集上取得分类AUROC 0.890与严重度分级QWK 0.530的最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超声心动图基础模型将整段视频与完整临床报告配对训练，但报告包含该视图并未显示的结构与所见，导致模型把发现与偶然或虚假信号关联，而非与真实视觉证据关联，从而损害准确性、解剖定位性与临床可信度。

Method: 构建EchoAlign视觉-语言基础模型，训练数据为来自159家临床中心的287万段超声心动图视频与图像；以心脏结构、视频、检查三个层级与报告文本对齐，并引入可见性矩阵，将监督信号限定于确实呈现该结构的视图。

Result: 在超过25家临床中心、10个未见外部数据集的47万测试视频上，与5个公开基础模型对比：分类平均AUROC最高，为0.890（95% CI 0.881–0.897）；严重度分级平均二次加权kappa最高，为0.530（95% CI 0.511–0.544）。单一模型覆盖62项临床所见，可完成发现检测、严重度分级、测量估计与心脏结构分割。

Conclusion: 通过结构—视图—文本的细粒度对齐与可见性约束监督，EchoAlign在外部数据上表现出更强泛化与解剖对应性，并以统一模型支持检测、分级、测量与分割等多任务，提升临床应用价值与可信度。

Abstract: Echocardiography assesses the structure and function of the heart. Cardiologists read each study across several standard views and report the findings visible in those views by cardiac structure. AI foundation models can learn to analyze these studies from clinical reports, reducing annotation burden and supporting clinical interpretation. However, existing models are trained by pairing each video with the full clinical report, including structures and findings its view does not show. This mismatch lets a model associate a finding with incidental or spurious signals rather than its visual evidence, which limits accuracy, anatomical grounding and clinical trust. Here we introduce EchoAlign, a vision-language foundation model developed on 2.87 million echocardiographic videos and images from 159 clinical centers. EchoAlign aligns each cardiac structure, video and study with its report text, and a visibility matrix restricts supervision to the views that actually depict each structure. We compare it with five public foundation models on 0.47 million test videos across ten unseen external datasets from more than 25 clinical centers. EchoAlign achieves the highest mean AUROC of 0.890 (95% CI, 0.881 to 0.897) for classification and the highest mean quadratic weighted kappa of 0.530 (95% CI, 0.511 to 0.544) for severity grading. Across 62 clinical findings, EchoAlign provides one model that detects findings, grades their severity, estimates measurements and segments cardiac structures.

</details>


### [21] [Fix Where It Fails: Root-Cause-Guided Agentic Harness Optimization for Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27529)
*Jee Eun Kim,Donghyeon Choi,Sangjune An,Hae Beom Lee,Kyong Hwan Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RoHa-SGG，将智能体式 harness 优化扩展到场景图生成，通过根因分析定位冻结模型的失败组件并搜索推理代码修复，在五个模型-数据集组合上全面提升指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PredCls/SGCls/SGDet 评测只反映性能下降幅度，无法说明模型在哪里失败、为何失败；若能定位失败原因，就可在不重训练的情况下针对性修复模型缺陷。

Method: RoHa-SGG 优化冻结 SGG 模型外围的 harness（推理代码），包含两个 LLM 智能体：根因分析智能体识别失败组件并诊断原因，提案智能体据此搜索可修复这些原因的 harness；在 Visual Genome 与 PSG 上，对两阶段、单阶段和多模态 LLM 模型进行评估。

Result: 分析发现真值三元组丢失的组件随模型和数据集而不同。RoHa-SGG 在全部五个模型-数据集组合上提升所有指标：多模态 LLM 的图约束 R@100 与 mR@100 最高提升 15.8 和 26.4 点，两阶段与单阶段模型最高提升 3.6 和 10.1 点；根因引导使 harness 搜索达到无引导搜索最终分数的速度最快提升三倍。

Conclusion: 根因引导的 harness 优化可在不重训练 SGG 模型的前提下有效弥补性能损失，并显著加速推理代码搜索；失败组件因模型与数据集而异，说明需针对具体失败点进行定制修复。

Abstract: Scene graph generation (SGG) aims to detect objects in an image and predict the relationships between them. The standard PredCls, SGCls, and SGDet settings show how much performance drops without ground-truth boxes and labels, but not where or why a model fails. Knowing this would allow fixing a model where it fails, without retraining. We propose RoHa-SGG (Root-cause-guided Harness optimization for SGG), which extends agentic harness optimization to SGG. It optimizes the harness, the inference code around a frozen SGG model. RoHa-SGG has two main LLM agents. A root-cause analysis (RCA) agent finds the components where the model fails and diagnoses why, and a proposer agent searches for a harness that fixes these causes. We evaluate RoHa-SGG on Visual Genome and PSG with representative two-stage, one-stage, and multimodal-LLM SGG models. The analysis shows that the components where ground-truth triplets are lost differ across models and datasets. RoHa-SGG improves every metric on all five model-dataset combinations. Graph-constrained R@100 and mR@100 rise by up to 15.8 and 26.4 points for the multimodal LLM and by up to 3.6 and 10.1 points for the two-stage and one-stage models. With root-cause guidance, the harness search reaches the final score of an unguided search up to three times faster.

</details>


### [22] [Beyond a Single Human Demonstration: Learning from Scored Trajectory Alternatives for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27515)
*Xinyao Rao,Zecheng Tang,Liuxuan Sun,Zheyuan Lai,Minjun Yu,Xun Cao,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveSTA通过离线生成并评分的替代轨迹来监督自回归提案生成与残差流式精炼，并用共享JEPA编码器降低推理成本，在NAVSIM与HUGSIM上取得领先且前向延迟较ChainFlow-VLA降低73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类驾驶日志每个场景仅记录一条轨迹，缺乏其他可行驾驶选择的显式轨迹目标与质量标签，限制了对"生成—精炼多候选轨迹"类端到端规划器的监督；同时以大型视觉语言模型（VLM）为条件做精炼会带来显著在线推理开销。

Method: 1) 对每个训练场景，离线通过对记录轨迹做平滑变形生成替代轨迹，并基于日志交通进行模拟执行评估打分；2) 多样化高质量替代用于监督不同提案，并定义残差目标以进行质量引导去噪，覆盖全分数区间的候选用于训练驾驶质量评分器；3) 采用共享联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），通过掩码视频特征预测进行适配，单次编码观测帧，同时为精炼与评分提供时序上下文。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1 Navtest上，DriveSTA在仅使用NAVSIM数据训练的对比规划器中排名第一；扩展训练数据后，DriveSTA-Scale在Navtest与闭环HUGSIM上于对比方法中达到最优，且匹配评估下平均规划器前向延迟相对ChainFlow-VLA降低73%。

Conclusion: 以离线生成并评分的替代轨迹作为监督信号，可有效提升多候选轨迹规划器的生成与精炼质量，并以轻量共享表征替代VLM条件化，在保持SOTA性能的同时大幅降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Human driving logs record only one trajectory per scene, leaving other feasible driving choices without explicit trajectory targets or quality labels. This limits supervision for end-to-end planners that generate and refine multiple candidate trajectories. Meanwhile, conditioning refinement on a large vision-language model adds substantial online inference cost. We propose DriveSTA, which extends autoregressive proposal generation and proposal-conditioned residual flow refinement with scored trajectory alternatives. For each training scene, we generate alternatives offline through smooth deformations of the recorded trajectory and evaluate their simulated execution against logged traffic. Diverse, high-quality alternatives supervise distinct proposals and define residual targets for quality-guided denoising, while candidates spanning the full score range train a scorer to assess driving quality. A shared joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA), adapted through masked video-feature prediction, encodes observed frames once and provides temporal context for both refinement and scoring. On NAVSIM v1 Navtest, DriveSTA ranks first among the compared planners trained on NAVSIM data alone. With expanded training data, DriveSTA-Scale achieves state-of-the-art results among the compared methods on Navtest and closed-loop HUGSIM, while reducing mean planner forward-pass latency by 73% relative to ChainFlow-VLA in matched evaluations.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond What They Say: Cue Acknowledgment and Robustness in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27389)
*Ali Rahimiakbr,Arvin Baghal Asl,Nima Niroumand,Mohammad Parsa Jafarnejady,Hossein Shahabadi,Amirmohammad Izadi,Mahdieh Soleymani Baghshah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文针对视觉语言模型（VLM）解释可靠性提出关键质疑：回答中“点名”提示线索并不等于抵抗了该线索。作者通过配对比较发现，模型可能更频繁地承认线索、同时更频繁地选中被诱导的错误答案，故“沉默减少”并不代表鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常用解释来判断VLM是基于视觉证据还是误导性提示作答，并默认“显式指出线索”即意味着识别并抵抗了该线索。但作者认为披露（disclosure）与遵循（following）可同时发生，仅以解释作为鲁棒性判据并不充分，因此需要检验“线索承认增加是否伴随抵抗增加”。

Method: 在固定同一问题与同一正确答案的前提下，构造帮助性、误导性与冲突性的视觉/文本线索，收集模型匹配的回答与解释，并对“遵循率”与“承认率”进行配对比较；同时分析解释中提及文本提示却遗漏同时呈现的视觉线索的现象，以及要求模型给出证据和承认线索是否提升抵抗。

Result: 配对比较显示，模型可以在更频繁承认线索的同时更频繁选中被背书的错误答案，即承认率上升并不对应抵抗率上升；解释中存在“提及文本提示而忽略视觉线索”的不完整披露；在测试设置下，要求提供证据与承认线索并未稳定提升抵抗能力；结果随模型与设置而异。

Conclusion: 解释中的线索披露不能作为鲁棒性的替代指标。评估应在同一批样本上同时报告“线索遵循”与“线索承认”，并区分视觉线索与文本线索；本文比较未分离承认的因果效应，故不主张因果关系。

Abstract: Explanations are used to assess whether vision-language models rely on visual evidence or misleading suggestions. A response that explicitly names a cue can still follow it, making disclosure alone an incomplete basis for judging robustness. We ask whether greater cue acknowledgment accompanies greater resistance. We compare matched answers and explanations under helpful, misleading, and conflicting visual and textual cues while holding the underlying question and correct answer fixed. A paired comparison shows that a model can select the endorsed wrong answer more often while acknowledging the cues more often. Reduced silence therefore need not indicate improved resistance. Explanations may also mention a textual suggestion while omitting an accompanying visual cue. Requesting evidence and acknowledgment does not consistently improve resistance in the tested settings. Outcomes vary across models and settings, and these comparisons do not isolate a causal effect of acknowledgment. Evaluations should report cue following and acknowledgment on the same instances, distinguishing visual from textual cues.

</details>


### [24] [Backbone-Specific LoRA Geometry: Orthogonality, Superposition, and Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27201)
*Kazuhiko Kawamoto,Keigo Watanabe,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出以「骨干特定的更新方向」作为训练骨干的签名，从注意力对称性推导跨骨干相关界，并据此设计基于余弦的骨干识别与内积检验，实现无需坐标对齐的适配器复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模型共享同一LoRA更新可简化部署，但组合方法与跨模型迁移各有局限；跨骨干共享更新时能否保持精度、并区分各更新贡献的条件尚不明确。

Method: 将骨干特定的更新方向视为训练骨干的签名；在独立、均匀预训练特征朝向假设下，由注意力对称性与等变LoRA学习推导跨骨干相关界；构建局部梯度流模型解释同骨干同任务独立运行间的正相关现象；提出基于余弦的骨干识别与内积检验，用于已注册适配器复用而无需坐标对齐。

Result: 在CLIP骨干上，共享求和适配器平均精度与独立适配器几乎相同；余弦分数在评估任务上可靠识别源骨干；不同骨干适配器之和中仍可检测出已注册适配器。

Conclusion: 更新方向可作为骨干的可辨识签名，支持无坐标对齐的适配器共享与归属检验，为跨骨干LoRA复用提供理论与实证依据。

Abstract: Sharing one low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update across pretrained models simplifies deployment. Composition methods typically combine updates from a common backbone, while cross-model transfer transforms updates for a target backbone. The conditions for sharing updates across backbones while preserving accuracy and distinguishing individual contributions remain unclear. Our idea is to use backbone-specific update directions as signatures of their training backbones. We derive a cross-backbone correlation bound from attention symmetries and equivariant LoRA learning under independent, uniform pretrained feature orientations. A local gradient-flow model explains positive mean correlation between independent runs on the same backbone and task. These patterns motivate cosine-based backbone identification and inner-product tests for registered-adapter reuse without coordinate alignment. Experiments on CLIP backbones show that shared sums achieve nearly the same mean accuracy as separate adapters. Cosine scores reliably identify source backbones across the evaluated tasks. Registered adapters remain detectable in sums of adapters from different backbones.

</details>


### [25] [GEG-SAM: Gaze Exploration-Guided Segment Anything Model for Weakly Supervised Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27183)
*Ao Wang,Lei Zhang,Lituan Wang,Zhenwei Zhang,Xiangbao Gong,Zhiyi Zhang,Shunyao Wang,Xianglei Yuan,Zizhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GEG-SAM，利用内镜医师注视序列的结构化关系（时间动态+空间拓扑）及注视对齐视觉上下文，生成gaze-conditioned token注入冻结SAM编码器，实现弱监督息肉分割，五个公开数据集mean Dice较最强弱监督基线提升9.30个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内镜医师注视可无干扰地记录病灶导向的视觉探索，可作弱监督信号。但现有方法将注视点视为孤立的空间提示直接与目标关联，忽视了自然注视是连续视觉搜索过程：注视点在时间上相连、空间上有组织、并与视觉内容耦合。仅凭局部视觉内容独立解释每个注视点会丢失轨迹的时间演化与空间组织；而只建模注视点间关系又无法揭示其所关注区域的视觉语义。

Method: GEG-SAM框架：1）通过建模注视探索的时间动态与空间拓扑捕获结构化注视关系；2）将关系表示与注视对齐的视觉上下文关联，得到gaze-conditioned tokens；3）将tokens注入冻结的SAM编码器以调制其预训练图像特征，引导SAM进行病灶导向分割。

Result: 在五个公开息肉分割数据集上实验验证有效性，mean Dice较最强弱监督基线提升9.30个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式联合建模注视的结构化关系与对应视觉线索可有效调制SAM，为弱监督息肉分割提供原则性框架。

Abstract: Endoscopist gaze offers an unobtrusively acquired record of lesion-oriented visual exploration during routine clinical inspection, providing relevant supervision for tasks. However, existing methods treat fixations as isolated spatial prompts directly associated with the target, overlooking the fact that natural gaze records a continuous visual search process in which fixations are temporally connected, spatially organized, and coupled with the visual content. Interpreting each fixation independently based only on its local visual content overlooks the temporal evolution and spatial organization of the gaze trajectory, whereas modeling relations among fixations alone cannot reveal the visual semantics of the regions they attend to. To this end, we propose the Gaze Exploration-Guided Segment Anything Model (GEG-SAM), a principled weakly supervised framework designed to explicitly exploit structured gaze relations and their corresponding visual cues to modulate SAM for polyp segmentation. Specifically, GEG-SAM captures structured gaze relations by modeling the temporal dynamics and spatial topology of gaze exploration. It then associates these relational representations with fixation-aligned visual context to derive gaze-conditioned tokens, which are further injected into the frozen SAM encoder to modulate its pretrained image features. In this way, GEG-SAM effectively exploits the rich information embedded in gaze exploration to guide SAM toward lesion-oriented segmentation. Extensive experiments on five public polyp segmentation datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, improving the mean Dice by 9.30 percentage points over the strongest weakly supervised baseline.

</details>


### [26] [Crafting the Difference, Grounding the Evidence: Discriminative Concept Learning for Zero-Shot Skeleton Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27135)
*Siyu Zhu,Feng Ding,Zixiao Jin,Kaizhi Li,Xin Dong,Haisheng Fu,Yuchen Zou,Guohe Zhang,Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRAFT通过概念精炼与特征接地令牌，缓解LLM描述在零样本骨架动作识别中的混淆线索重复与语义-特征脱节问题，在NTU-60/120固定划分上平均提升ZSL准确率5.4个百分点、GZSL调和均值5.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的文本描述虽能增强零样本骨架动作识别，但存在两个问题：一是易重复易混淆动作共享的线索，缺乏判别性；二是上下文语义与骨架特征之间缺乏显式关联。

Method: 提出CRAFT框架，包含两部分：(1) 概念精炼——采用困难负样本条件生成与对比打分，筛出能区分目标动作与其最相近竞争类别的概念；并引入Unique-QDPP在每个语义槽内选择互补描述、禁止跨槽复用，构建概念库；(2) 特征接地令牌——以该概念库监督可学习的概念令牌，使其从每个骨架中读取运动与上下文线索。

Result: 在固定的NTU-60与NTU-120划分上，CRAFT对覆盖四种识别范式的五个基线均有提升；每个基线-划分组合的ZSL准确率与GZSL调和均值均上升，平均分别提升5.4与5.9个百分点。随机划分实验将增益扩展至三个数据集（含PKU-MMD）。

Conclusion: 通过判别性概念精炼与特征接地的概念令牌设计，可有效利用LLM语义先验提升零样本骨架动作识别性能，且方法对不同范式与数据集具有良好泛化性。

Abstract: LLM-generated descriptions enrich zero-shot skeleton-based action recognition but repeat cues shared by confusable actions. Their contextual semantics also lack explicit links to skeleton features. To address these challenges, we propose CRAFT, which couples Concept Refinement and Feature-grounded Tokens. Hard-negative-conditioned generation and contrastive scoring identify concepts that distinguish each action from its closest rivals. Unique-QDPP selects complementary descriptions within each semantic slot and prevents their reuse across slots. This bank supervises learnable concept tokens that read motion and context cues from each skeleton. On fixed NTU-60 and NTU-120 splits, CRAFT improves five baselines spanning four recognition paradigms. ZSL accuracy and GZSL harmonic mean increase on every baseline-split pair, by 5.4 and 5.9 percentage points on average. Random-split experiments extend these gains to three datasets, including PKU-MMD. Code will be released after acceptance.

</details>


### [27] [RemoteEvolver: Expanding Earth Observation Capabilities through Agent Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27110)
*Shengxiang Xu,Liang Yao,Shuyi Chen,Yishu Wang,Shimin Di,Fan Liu,Bishun Yao,Hanghui Guo,Xinyu Liu,Chengye Liang,Yuyu Luo,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RemoteEvolver——一个面向遥感智能体的外部harness框架，在冻结基座模型参数的前提下，通过执行反馈驱动工具获取与工作流知识演化，实现跨任务的能力扩展与经验复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感智能体多依赖固定工具集，且经验积累仅限于对预定义能力的重新编排，既无法扩展能力边界，也难以将改进迁移到后续任务，因而难以实现持续自我改进。

Method: 将工具与工作流知识作为基座模型外部的可编辑、可持久化资源统一由harness管理；在不更新基座参数的情况下，依据执行反馈按需获取缺失的专用工具，并将单任务中经验证的成功执行经验蒸馏为可跨任务复用的agent skills，二者共同支撑后续任务的规划与执行。

Result: 在Earth-Bench与OpenEarthAgent-Bench上，工具完全可用与部分缺失两种设置下，相较初始状态平均准确率分别提升8.18与23.86个百分点；同设置下平均领先SOTA方法6.77与17.10个百分点。

Conclusion: 以外部harness承载可持久化的工具与技能资源，可在不进行额外后训练的条件下实现遥感智能体的持续能力扩张与经验迁移，为自改进智能体系统提供了一条可复用的工程路径。

Abstract: We are interested in developing a continually self-improving remote sensing agentic system. It should identify and address its capability gaps, retaining the resulting improvements to enhance performance on subsequent tasks. However, existing systems typically rely on fixed toolsets and confine their experience accumulation to re-orchestrating predefined capabilities, constraining both capability expansion and the transfer of improvements across tasks. As the infrastructure for agentic systems, the harness manages tools and workflow knowledge as editable, persistent resources outside the base model. These resources can therefore be updated and reused across tasks without additional post-training. Based on these observations, we propose RemoteEvolver, an external harness for remote sensing agents that drives tool and workflow evolution through execution feedback. With the base model parameters fixed, RemoteEvolver acquires missing specialized tools on demand and distills verified successful execution experience from individual tasks into agent skills that can be reused across tasks. The acquired tools and accumulated skills jointly support planning and execution on subsequent tasks. Experiments on Earth-Bench and OpenEarthAgent-Bench show that RemoteEvolver achieves average accuracy gains of 8.18 and 23.86 percentage points over its initial state when tools are fully available and partially missing. Under the same settings, it also outperforms the state-of-the-art methods by an average of 6.77 and 17.10 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [28] [Learning Video Foresight Reasoning from Unlabeled Videos via On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26954)
*Jingchen Sun,Jiaxing Zhang,Qingqiao Hu,Dingqiang Ye,Yuqi Jia,Yijun Liang,Di Fu,Yunjie Tian,Dongdi Zhao,Changyou Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Future-OPSD框架，用on-policy自蒸馏将过去-未来视频对转化为自监督信号，并通过foresight score引导模型聚焦最有价值的未来信息，在开放式预测与封闭式规划上分别提升至多14.2和15.9分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频前瞻推理方法存在两个局限：1）依赖标注或外部大模型监督，可扩展性差；2）把整段未来视频等同看待，而其中大部分是无关上下文信息。

Method: 采用on-policy自蒸馏框架，把过去-未来视频对作为自然自监督信号，无需额外标注或LLM监督；定义foresight score以获得对比式特权优势（contrastive privilege advantage），引导模型学习最有价值的未来信息。因该分数不可解，优化一个可解的下界替代目标，将真实未来与随机参考未来间的优势与师生蒸馏优势插值结合，从而放大决策相关信息、抑制无关上下文。

Result: 在开放式预测和封闭式规划任务上，Future-OPSD相比基座模型分别提升最多14.2和15.9个点，且不损害通用视频理解能力。

Conclusion: Future-OPSD以自监督方式实现可扩展的视频前瞻推理训练，并通过价值感知的未来信息筛选显著提升预测与规划性能，同时保持通用能力。

Abstract: Video foresight reasoning requires vision-language models to anticipate future events or next actions from currently observed videos. Existing methods fit future captions, rationales, or full visual states, but face two limitations. First, they rely on annotations or external large models for supervision, limiting scalability. We address this with an on-policy self-distillation framework that turns past-future video pairs into a natural self-supervised signal without extra annotations or LLM supervision. Second, they treat the whole future clip as equally valuable, although most of it is irrelevant context information. We address this by defining a foresight score, from which we obtain a contrastive privilege advantage that drives the model to learn the most valuable future information. Since the score is intractable, we optimize a tractable lower-bound surrogate that interpolates this advantage, computed between the true and a random reference future, with a teacher-student distillation advantage; together, they amplify decision-relevant content information and suppress irrelevant context information. We call the resulting framework Future-OPSD. On open-ended prediction and closed-ended planning, Future-OPSD improves base models by up to 14.2 and 15.9 points without compromising general video understanding. Code will be available at https://jingchensun.github.io/future-opsd/

</details>


### [29] [SonoAtlasWorld: Anatomy-Grounded Ultrasound World Models for Wide-Range Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26867)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Boris Mailhe,Hang Yu,Yikang Liu,Xiao Chen,Ankush Mukherjee,Terrence Chen,Shanhui Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SonoAtlasWorld，以"解剖为状态"的超声世界模型：用CT提取的持久3D解剖资产锚定扫描，探针动作决定可见性与声学条件，驱动因果超声生成器，实现81.2 FPS的pose-to-ultrasound生成，并提出AFI指标衡量解剖可控性，可用于离线策略训练与闭环评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人超声策略学习需要可扩展的交互环境与可重复评估，但真实患者上的探索受采集成本与安全约束限制。现有生成式世界模型仅依赖图像历史与探针动作，在大范围扫描和声窗变化下无法保证解剖一致性。

Method: 以解剖作为隐状态：从CT中提取持久3D解剖资产，为每次扫描锚定特定解剖结构；探针位姿决定解剖与声学可见性条件，驱动在临床视频上训练的因果超声生成器。三阶段流程为解剖条件预训练、因果化转换与长程 rollout 对齐；并引入动作跟随指数（AFI），以相对静态前缀基线的归一化重建增益衡量解剖可控性。

Result: 推理优化达81.2 FPS；在40个留出3D体数据上，肝脏与宏观器官AFI分别为0.428与0.262，而受测的动作/几何条件方法最高仅0.117与0.038；长程rollout对齐将40次重复扫描周期末端的亮度漂移降低3.46倍；规模化生成数据使仿真中未见解剖上的关键平面获取成功率从48.2%提升至81.2%。

Conclusion: 解剖锚定的世界模型可提供解剖一致、动作可控且高效的超声生成，既能生成离线策略训练演示，也支持解剖接地的闭环评估，验证了其对机器人超声策略学习的实用价值。

Abstract: Learning robotic ultrasound policies requires interactive environments for scalable data generation and repeatable evaluation, while exploration on real patients is limited by acquisition costs and safety constraints. Generative world models offer an alternative, but image history and probe actions alone do not ensure anatomical consistency across wide-range scans and changing acoustic windows. We introduce SonoAtlasWorld, an ultrasound world model built on anatomy as state. A persistent 3D anatomical asset extracted from computed tomography (CT) anchors each scan to a specific anatomy. Probe actions determine anatomical and acoustic visibility conditions for a causal ultrasound generator trained on clinical videos. Our three-stage pipeline combines anatomy-conditioned pretraining, causal conversion and long-rollout alignment. Inference optimizations achieve pose-to-ultrasound throughput of 81.2 FPS. To evaluate anatomical control beyond image similarity, we introduce the Action-Following Index (AFI), which measures normalized reconstruction gain over a static-prefix baseline. On 40 held-out 3D volumes, SonoAtlasWorld achieves liver and macro-organ AFI of 0.428 and 0.262, compared with at most 0.117 and 0.038 for the tested action- and geometry-conditioned methods. Long-rollout alignment reduces brightness drift by 3.46x at the end of 40 repeated scan cycles. SonoAtlasWorld also generates offline policy-training demonstrations and supports anatomically grounded closed-loop evaluation. Scaling the generated training data improves key-plane acquisition success from 48.2% to 81.2% on unseen anatomies in simulation, demonstrating its utility for robotic ultrasound policy learning.

</details>


### [30] [Scoliosis5K: Multimodal Gait Analysis via Clip-Conditioned RGB-Silhouette Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26849)
*Zizhao Peng,Zihan Wang,Zheng Lv,Yan Wang,Ping Li,Fengwei An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建Scoliosis5K多站点步态数据集，研究在分类器已观察配对彩色序列时，显式二值轮廓对受试者级脊柱侧弯步态分类的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 彩色行走视频同时编码身体边界运动、衣物褶皱、表面外观与录制环境线索，因此需澄清显式二值轮廓在已有RGB视频输入之上是否带来额外判别信息。

Method: Scoliosis5K：4,998段走廊行走序列、3,010个受试者标识，RGB人体裁剪、二值轮廓与2D姿态共享录制标识与帧索引。模型用每个patch相对其片段均值的像素偏差，对预训练RGB编码器内两组低秩query/value更新加权，再将所得视频表示与轮廓Transformer序列、每个窗口单帧原生分辨率RGB的区域核岭回归得分融合。

Result: 988名受试者、5个受试者不交叉折、3个训练种子上：完整系统83.77%准确率、79.91%宏F1；冻结RGB编码器为83.27%与79.56%。固定权重下将轮廓向量置零仅使总体准确率变化0.10个百分点，但正类召回由68.52%升至77.78%，其余两类召回下降。宏AUROC全队列0.950，按采集站点与录制次数分组匹配的受试者对间为0.879。

Conclusion: 二值轮廓对总体准确率的边际贡献有限，主要影响类别间召回分配；研究提示需以受试者级、站点匹配的评价协议审慎评估轮廓输入的增益。

Abstract: Color walking video records body boundary motion alongside clothing folds, surface appearance, and recording-environment cues. To examine what an explicit binary silhouette contributes to participant-level classification of scoliosis-related gait when a classifier already observes the paired color sequence, we construct Scoliosis5K, a multi-site collection of 4,998 corridor-walking sequences across 3,010 participant identifiers in which RGB person crops, binary silhouettes, and two-dimensional poses share each recording identifier and frame index. Our architecture uses each patch's pixel deviation from its clip mean to weight two low-rank query and value updates inside a pretrained RGB encoder, fusing the resulting video representation with a silhouette transformer sequence and regional kernel ridge scores extracted from one native-resolution RGB frame per window. Across 988 participants in five participant-disjoint cross-validation folds and three training seeds, the complete system reaches 83.77% accuracy and 79.91% macro-averaged F1, compared with 83.27% accuracy and 79.56% macro-F1 when the video RGB encoder remains frozen under identical inputs. Zeroing the silhouette vector at fixed weights shifts overall accuracy by only 0.10 percentage points while raising positive-category recall from 68.52% to 77.78% and lowering recall for both remaining categories. Macro-averaged AUROC is 0.950 across the cohort and 0.879 among participant pairs matched on acquisition site and recording-count group.

</details>


### [31] [3D Open-Vocabulary Detection via Visual Alignment and Generative Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26842)
*Thomas E. Huang,Alex Zihao Zhu,Hao Xiang,Tianyi Cai,Jingwei Ji,Yuliang Zou,Jiafan Yu,Xin Huang,Mingxing Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个两部分框架，通过对象级直接蒸馏与MLLM字幕语义增强，实现室外开放词汇3D目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以将2D视觉语言模型(VLM)的丰富语义知识有效迁移到3D场景，室外开放词汇3D检测仍是难题。

Method: 1) 无需人工标签生成类无关3D proposals，利用空间感知VLM提取每个proposal投影2D框的语义嵌入，并监督3D模型逐实例回归该嵌入；2) 用多模态大语言模型(MLLM)作为图像字幕器为各proposal生成丰富文本描述，将3D特征与这些语义对齐，形成"特征蒸馏+字幕语义增强"的复合损失。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset与nuScenes上取得3D开放词汇检测的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 对象级直接特征蒸馏结合MLLM字幕语义增强，可有效提升室外开放词汇3D检测的零样本能力与性能。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D object detection remains a significant challenge for outdoor scenes, with existing methods often struggling to effectively transfer the rich semantic knowledge from powerful 2D Vision-Language Models (VLMs) into a 3D context. We propose a novel, two-part framework for this task. First, we introduce a direct, object-level distillation method. We generate class-agnostic 3D proposals to source regions from the entire scene, without relying on any manual labels. We leverage a spatially-aware VLM to extract a semantic embedding from each proposal's projected 2D bounding box and supervise our 3D model to predict this embedding on a per-instance basis. Second, to enhance zero-shot capability and inject domain-specific information, we employ a powerful Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as an image captioner to generate rich textual captions for each of these proposals. These captions are integrated into the training objective to directly align the 3D features with these rich semantics, creating a composite loss that combines direct feature distillation with caption-based semantic enrichment. Our experiments validate the effectiveness of our approach, establishing a new state of the art in 3D open-vocabulary detection on the Waymo Open Dataset and nuScenes.

</details>


### [32] [Forehead ROI Localization under Real-World Imaging Degradations via Cross-View Pseudo-Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26712)
*Geetanjali Sharma,Gaurav Jaswal,Aditya Nigam,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出置信度与一致性引导的伪标签方法（CCGP），在无标注人脸图像上自适应Grounding DINO实现额头ROI定位，在五个外部数据集中的四个取得最高mIoU，并在模糊、低对比度条件下提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 额头ROI定位对下颌被遮挡时的额头皱纹生物识别至关重要，但在图像质量、姿态和光照变化下定位仍具挑战；同时缺乏标注数据，需在不大改检测器的前提下利用无标注人脸图像提升跨域鲁棒性。

Method: CCGP：基于Grounding DINO的半监督伪标签适配。利用检测置信度与原始视图和水平翻转视图之间的几何一致性双重过滤无标注人脸图像，筛选可靠伪标签用于自训练，且不修改检测器结构与损失函数。评估涵盖五个外部数据集及覆盖七种成像条件的350张图像，并按质量分层分析。

Result: 五个外部数据集中四个取得最高mIoU；RMFRD上由监督Grounding DINO的0.415提升至0.580；质量分层子集中模糊（0.487→0.719）和低对比度（0.615→0.710）增益最大；优于传统自训练（0.580 vs. 0.551 mIoU）。存在轻微域内性能差距（0.861 vs. 0.866 mIoU），且斜角姿态仍有挑战。

Conclusion: 置信度与几何一致性引导的伪标签能在不改变检测器架构的情况下有效提升跨数据集、挑战成像条件下的额头ROI定位性能，代价是极小的域内精度损失；斜角姿态是后续改进方向。

Abstract: Forehead region-of-interest (ROI) localization is important for forehead-creases biometrics, particularly when the lower face is occluded. However, localization remains challenging under varying image quality, pose, and illumination. We propose Confidence and Consistency Guided Pseudo-Labeling (CCGP), which adapts Grounding DINO using unlabeled facial images filtered by detection confidence and geometric consistency between original and horizontally flipped views, without modifying the detector architecture or loss. We evaluate CCGP on five external datasets and 350 images spanning seven imaging conditions. CCGP achieves the highest mean intersection over union (mIoU) on four of five external datasets, improving from 0.415 to 0.580 on RMFRD compared with supervised Grounding DINO. On the quality-stratified subset, the largest gains occur under blur (0.487 to 0.719) and low contrast (0.615 to 0.710). CCGP also outperforms conventional self-training on RMFRD (0.580 versus 0.551 mIoU). Despite a slight in-domain performance gap (0.861 vs. 0.866 mIoU) and challenges with off-angle poses, CCGP improves cross-dataset localization under challenging imaging conditions.

</details>


### [33] [Solar-Conditioned Flow Matching for Multitemporal Aerial Visible-to-Infrared Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27501)
*Wenwen Fang,Bingwei Hui,Xin Chen,Zhongzhen Sun,Zhenfeng Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Solar-Flow，将航拍可见光到红外（V2IR）翻译由静态映射升级为多时相生成，用太阳高度、热相位钟与飞行高度作为物理条件注入预训练流匹配生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2IR方法在狭窄采集窗口内学习静态映射，但热状态随昼夜演化，单一可见光图像可能对应多种合理红外外观，因此V2IR本质上是多时相任务。

Method: 设计三个可计算物理因子：太阳高度、热相位钟（TPC，以日出为锚点、在24小时连续轴上累计加热与冷却时长，使无训练样本的时段热状态仍有定义）、飞行高度；通过自适应层归一化将条件注入预训练流匹配生成器的每个Transformer块；并构建覆盖14个时段组的成对航拍V2IR数据集。

Result: 在新数据集上实现多时相生成：单张可见光图像可翻译为一天中多个时刻的合理红外图像，包括训练中未出现的时段；条件注入设计在保持翻译保真度的同时支持多时相生成。

Conclusion: 以物理可解释的太阳条件驱动流匹配生成，可将航拍V2IR翻译从单一时段静态映射拓展为跨昼夜的连续多时相生成。

Abstract: Aerial visible-to-infrared (V2IR) image translation provides scalable thermal data across the full diurnal cycle. However, existing methods predominantly learn a static mapping within narrow acquisition windows. As the thermal state evolves throughout the day, a single visible image may correspond to multiple plausible IR appearances, making V2IR translation an inherently multitemporal task. This letter proposes solar-conditioned latent flow matching (Solar-Flow), which upgrades aerial V2IR translation from static mapping to multitemporal generation. First, we design three computable physical factors, namely the solar elevation, the thermal phase clock (TPC), and the flight altitude. The TPC counts the elapsed heating and cooling hours on a continuous 24-hour axis anchored at sunrise, so the thermal state stays well defined even at hours without training samples. Second, we inject the condition into every Transformer block of a pretrained flow matching generator through adaptive layer normalization. This design preserves translation fidelity and enables multitemporal generation. Finally, we construct a paired aerial V2IR dataset covering 14 hour groups. Solar-Flow achieves multitemporal generation on this dataset, where a single visible image is translated into plausible IR images at multiple times of day, even at hours that were never seen in training.

</details>


### [34] [PanoUAV-Track: Full-Spherical UAV Visual Tracking in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27383)
*Bingyu Li,Lichang Yang,Zhiyuan Zhao,Junyu Gao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个面向移动无人机平台的全景（等距柱状投影）跟踪基准 PanoUAV-Track，并揭示透视到球面的泛化鸿沟与纬度畸变、接缝不连续导致的跟踪退化，提出几何感知跟踪框架 PUTrack。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV跟踪基准多基于视场受限的透视相机；虽有全向相机可实现全景观测，但移动UAV平台上的全景跟踪基本未被探索。与地面全景跟踪不同，UAV全向相机需在方位角与俯仰角两维上观测目标，在ERP投影下带来独特几何挑战。

Method: 采集约40小时真实8K全景UAV视频，经时间片段抽取、目标标注并融合仿真序列构建PanoUAV-Track（1,895条序列、507,961帧、2,872个标注目标，涵盖昼夜真实场景与仿真，支持视觉与语言目标指定）；并据此提出PanoUAV-Tracker（PUTrack），采用纬度自适应搜索与接缝感知周期采样。

Result: 大规模评测显示明显的透视到球面泛化鸿沟，并定位出与纬度相关畸变及ERP接缝不连续相关的跟踪性能下降；PUTrack提升零样本与域内跟踪性能，尤其改善跨接缝目标。

Conclusion: 移动UAV全景跟踪是一个尚未被充分研究且具有独特几何挑战的任务；所建基准与几何感知设计可有效缓解ERP畸变与接缝问题，为该方向提供数据与方法基础。

Abstract: Existing UAV tracking benchmarks predominantly rely on perspective cameras with limited fields of view. Omnidirectional cameras enable full-spherical observation, yet tracking from moving UAV platforms remains largely unexplored. Unlike ground-level panoramic tracking, UAV-mounted omnidirectional cameras observe targets across both azimuth and elevation, introducing geometric challenges under equirectangular projection (ERP). To investigate this setting, we capture approximately \textbf{40 hours of real-world 8K panoramic UAV video} and construct \textbf{PanoUAV-Track} through temporal clip extraction, target annotation, and the integration of simulated sequences. The resulting benchmark comprises 1,895 sequences, 507,961 frames, and 2,872 annotated objects, covering real-world daytime and nighttime scenes alongside simulation, and supporting both visual and language-based target specification. Through extensive evaluations, we reveal a clear \textbf{perspective-to-spherical} generalization gap and identify tracking degradation associated with latitude-dependent distortion and ERP seam discontinuity. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{PanoUAV-Tracker (PUTrack)}, a geometry-aware tracking framework combining latitude-adaptive search and seam-aware periodic sampling. PUTrack improves zero-shot and in-domain tracking, particularly for seam-crossing targets. Code and benchmark resources will be available\footnote{\textcolor{tabpink}{https://libingyu01.github.io/PanoUAV-Track/}}.

</details>


### [35] [VMSA: Video Memory Skill Agent for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27381)
*Changin Choi,Jungwon Park,Jungmin Ko,Jimyeong Kim,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VMSA框架：将结构化视频记忆（片段字幕记忆、实体事件记忆、实体感知段落记忆）与从成功轨迹中归纳的"记忆技能"相结合，支持长视频问答中的迭代证据检索、推理与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频（数小时至数天）问答需检索并整合跨时段的证据。现有agentic方法依赖外部记忆迭代检索，但智能体难以确定搜索什么、访问哪个记忆源、如何在远距离时间区间间连接证据，检索与推理仍是瓶颈。

Method: 联合设计结构化视频记忆与记忆技能。记忆含三部分：Segment Caption Memory与Entity Event Memory作为可检索证据源；Entity-Aware Episode Memory提供既宽泛又细致的视频上下文，用于引导检索与推理。并从成功agent轨迹中归纳记忆技能，增量式精炼/扩展紧凑技能库，指导迭代式证据检索、推理与验证。

Result: 在七个长视频问答基准上评测：三个周级自我中心基准全部取得最高准确率，Ego-R1 Bench上比最强基线高至多9.4%；另四个基准达到最佳或具竞争力表现。消融实验验证三个记忆组件与记忆技能各自的贡献，记忆技能还能改善时间证据定位。

Conclusion: 结构化多源记忆与可演化记忆技能协同，可有效提升长视频问答的检索、跨时段推理与证据定位能力。

Abstract: Long-video question answering requires retrieving and integrating relevant evidence from videos spanning hours or even days. Recent agentic approaches organize video information into external memories and iteratively retrieve evidence to answer questions. However, effective retrieval and reasoning remain challenging, as agents must determine what information to search for, which memory sources to access, and how to connect evidence across distant temporal intervals. To address these challenges, we propose Video Memory Skill Agent (VMSA), a framework that jointly designs structured video memories and memory skills for long-video understanding. VMSA constructs three complementary memory components. Segment Caption Memory and Entity Event Memory serve as searchable evidence sources, while Entity-Aware Episode Memory provides broad yet detailed video context to guide evidence retrieval and reasoning. To further support effective memory utilization, VMSA induces memory skills from successful agent trajectories, incrementally refining or extending a compact skill library to guide iterative evidence retrieval, reasoning, and verification.
