Across seven long-video question-answering benchmarks, VMSA achieves the highest accuracy on all three week-long egocentric benchmarks, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 9.4% on Ego-R1 Bench, and the best or competitive performance on four additional benchmarks. Ablation studies further demonstrate the contributions of all three memory components and memory skills, with memory skills also improving temporal evidence localization.

</details>


### [36] [SIR-Agent: Skill-Augmented Image Restoration Agent with Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27285)
*Yijian Wang,Qingsen Yan,Weiyu Zhou,Mingde Zhang,Axi Niu,Haokui Zhang,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIR-Agent，一种通过可扩展技能库与递归自我改进（RSI）维护图像修复（IR）知识、避免微调MLLM参数导致灾难性遗忘的技能增强型IR智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IR智能体依赖微调MLLM参数以适应新任务，导致IR知识灾难性遗忘，且难以持续扩展。

Method: 核心为链反馈驱动的技能修订（CFSR）：分析修复链，识别并修改现有技能中的薄弱操作，依据修订技能与已知任务的兼容性决定替换原技能或独立追加；并引入RSI驱动的技能元学习机制，识别CFSR的低效操作并自主优化其修订策略，实现技能库的高效演化。

Result: 在14个IR基准（6种单一退化、8种混合退化场景）上取得优越的IR性能；在7个未见任务上的评估验证了对新IR任务的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将IR知识外置为技能库并结合RSI演化，可在不微调MLLM参数的前提下兼顾性能、抗遗忘与跨任务泛化。

Abstract: Agents for Image Restoration (IR) aim to leverage Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to autonomously perceive image degradation and invoke IR tools. However, existing IR agents typically rely on fine-tuning MLLM parameters to adapt to new IR tasks, leading to catastrophic forgetting of IR knowledge. To address this limitation, we propose a skill-augmented IR agent, dubbed SIR-Agent, that maintains IR knowledge in a scalable skill library and uses Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) to facilitate efficient skill-library evolution. Specifically, we design a Chain Feedback-driven Skill Revision (CFSR) to evolve the skill library. The CFSR analyzes restoration chains to identify and revise weak operations in existing skills. The compatibility of a revised skill with known tasks decides if it replaces the original skill or is appended independently. Furthermore, to advance CFSR's skill-library evolution, we present an RSI-driven skill meta-learning mechanism, which identifies CFSR's inefficient operations and autonomously refines its revision strategy. Extensive experiments across 14 IR benchmarks, covering 6 single-degradation and 8 mixed-degradation scenarios, demonstrate SIR-Agent's superior IR performance. Further evaluation on 7 unseen tasks validates SIR-Agent's generalization to new IR tasks.

</details>


### [37] [Model the Annotation, Not the Noise: Semantic Annotation-Channel Prompt Learning against Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26702)
*Zibin Geng,Yitong Bao,Jia Li,Xuefeng Jiang,Sheng Sun,Yuwei Wang,Min Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对提示学习在少样本网络与检索场景中对标签噪声脆弱的问题，提出语义感知标注通道提示学习框架 SACP，从数据生成视角解耦潜在干净标签预测与实例相关标注生成，并用广义EM与后验感知记忆保持不确定性，在合成及真实噪声基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效提示学习已成为适配预训练视觉语言模型的重要手段，但在少样本网络与检索场景下对标签噪声高度敏感；现有鲁棒方法依赖硬样本筛选或确定性标签纠正，既丢弃了有价值的语义歧义信息，又忽视了观测标注的实际生成机制。

Method: 将含噪提示学习重构为数据生成问题：构建SACP框架，把潜在干净标签预测与实例相关的标注生成过程显式解耦；融合冻结VLM语义先验与轻量视觉残差，构建结构化“标注通道”（annotation channel），并通过带软后验的广义期望最大化（generalized EM）进行优化；此外引入后验感知记忆模块，在测试时推理阶段保留标签不确定性。

Result: 在多种合成噪声基准与真实含噪Food101N数据集上，SACP在不同噪声模式下均优于对比方法、达到SOTA，尤其在严重非对称噪声下优势明显。

Conclusion: SACP表明以生成式建模替代启发式噪声抑制，可在提示学习中有效保留语义歧义与标签不确定性，为噪声鲁棒的视觉语言模型适配提供新范式。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient prompt learning has become a cornerstone for adapting pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs), yet it remains highly vulnerable to label noise in few-shot Web and retrieval scenarios. Existing noise robust methods typically rely on hard sample selection or deterministic label correction, which discard valuable semantic ambiguity and overlook how observed annotations are actually generated. To bridge this gap, we reformulate noisy prompt learning through the perspective of data generation, proposing SACP, a Semantic-aware Annotation Channel-based Prompt learning framework. Instead of suppressing noise heuristically, SACP decouples latent clean-label prediction from instance-dependent annotation generation. By integrating frozen VLM semantic priors with lightweight visual residuals, SACP constructs a structured annotation channel optimized via generalized expectation-maximization with soft posteriors. Furthermore, a posterior-aware memory is introduced to preserve label uncertainty during test-time inference. Extensive experiments across different synthetic benchmarks and the real-world noisy Food101N dataset demonstrate that SACP achieves state-of-the-art performance against its counterparts in different label noise patterns, especially under severe asymmetric noise.

</details>


### [38] [PhysForce: Physics-aware Force Control for Interactive Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27164)
*Xuancheng Xu,Xiaofeng Wang,Yue Ma,Zheng Zhu,Bing-Kun Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PhysForce 两阶段框架（监督微调+强化学习），通过隐空间力编码、物理归因网络与物理感知强化学习，提升交互式视频世界模型对施加力的响应与物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型以运动轨迹表示力，会引入不当运动先验导致过度依赖位移模式；模型内部表示难以准确定位力诱导的状态变化，难以将力与其物理响应关联；且力标注数据有限，难以在未见场景中生成物理一致、时序稳定的预测。

Method: 两阶段框架，包含三个互补组件：(1) 监督微调阶段，Latent Force Intervention Encoder 将力的位置、方向、幅度映射到视频隐空间，且不暴露未来运动；(2) Physics Attribution Network 将力干预与隐状态的局部化变化相关联，强化物理响应学习；(3) 强化学习阶段，Physics-Aware Reinforcement Learning 在分布外视频上使用自监督奖励，提升物理一致性与泛化能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，PhysForce 在力响应性、物理一致性和泛化能力上均有提升，验证了其对交互式视频世界模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 以隐空间直接编码力并结合物理归因与自监督强化学习，可有效缓解运动先验依赖与标注稀缺问题，是构建可控交互式视频世界模型的可行路径。

Abstract: Interactive video world models aim to predict how visual environments evolve in response to user interventions, including physically consistent state changes induced by applied forces. Existing methods often represent forces as motion trajectories, which may introduce inappropriate motion priors, causing the model to over-rely on encoded displacement patterns. More fundamentally, the model's internal representations do not reliably localize force-induced state changes, making it difficult to associate applied forces with their resulting physical responses. Also, limited force-annotated data make it difficult to produce physically consistent and temporally stable predictions in unseen scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose PhysForce, a two-stage framework that integrates supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning through three complementary components. During supervised fine-tuning, (1) the Latent Force Intervention Encoder maps force location, direction, and magnitude into the video latent space without exposing future motion; and (2) the Physics Attribution Network associates force interventions with localized changes in the latent state, strengthening the learning of physical responses to applied forces. During reinforcement learning, (3) Physics-Aware Reinforcement Learning uses self-supervised rewards on out-of-distribution videos to improve physical consistency and generalization. Extensive experiments show that PhysForce improves force responsiveness, physical consistency, and generalization, demonstrating its effectiveness for interactive video world models.

</details>


### [39] [EgoSurgHands: An Egocentric 3D Hand Pose Dataset & Benchmark for Open Surgery Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27101)
*Ankit Pal,Guillaume Kugener,Sofia Volpi,Joshua Schwartz,Alex Idarraga,Pranav Rajpurkar,Gabriel A. Brat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EgoSurgHands，首个真实采集的开放手术训练场景第一视角关节式3D手部姿态估计基准数据集，并评测八种方法，揭示外科域间存在显著gap。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科技能评估、AR引导与机器人示范学习均依赖术者手部3D跟踪，但现有外科数据集均不提供真实的第一视角关节式3D手部姿态标注。

Method: 用多传感器眼镜采集：55段手术过程录制、41,909条训练手部实例，13段留出录制、9,476条人工验证评测样本，覆盖13个任务、3种手套颜色、4名受训者。每帧含内参、手部关键点及置信度、时间戳、眼动注视，以及SLAM成功时的外参。评测标签由传感器融合伪真值、逐帧人工交叉复核、外科医生裁定争议样本及独立多视角三角化参考共同构成。设置过程不相交划分与留一佩戴者（leave-one-wearer-out）协议；对八种方法做零样本评测，并提出58K参数残差校正器JRN。

Result: 第一视角预训练在所有留出佩戴者上均带来更强迁移：WildHands 与 JointTransformer 的 PA-MPJPE 分别比非第一视角的 HaMeR 低 18% 与 40%；JointTransformer 最优，误差 14.95 mm。MediaPipe Hands 仅在 24.1% 的评测样本中检测到手。JRN 在冻结主干上于留出佩戴者达到 10.96–14.85 mm PA-MPJPE。

Conclusion: 外科场景与通用第一视角数据间仍存在显著域间gap；该基准与代码已公开，为外科手部3D姿态估计与下游应用提供标准化评测平台。

Abstract: Surgical skill assessment, AR guidance, and robot learning from demonstration all benefit from 3D tracking of the surgeon's hands, yet no surgical dataset offers real egocentric articulated 3D hand pose. We introduce EgoSurgHands, a benchmark for real-captured egocentric hand pose estimation in open surgery training on synthetic pads. Captured with multi-sensor glasses, it contains 41,909 training hand-instance rows across 55 procedure recordings and 9,476 human-validated evaluation rows across 13 held-out recordings, spanning 13 tasks, 3 glove colors, and 4 trainees from first-year medical student to third-year resident. Each frame carries a multi-sensor stack: intrinsics, hand landmarks with per-hand confidence, timestamps, and eye gaze, plus SLAM extrinsics where SLAM succeeded. Evaluation labels combine sensor-fused pseudo-ground truth, human cross-review of every frame, surgeon adjudication of flagged cases, and an independent multi-view triangulated reference. Alongside the within-corpus, procedure-disjoint split, a leave-one-wearer-out protocol tested unseen trainees. Across eight methods evaluated zero-shot, from MANO-based reconstructors to the two-hand IntagHand and the keypoint-only MediaPipe Hands, egocentric pretraining was associated with stronger transfer on every held-out wearer. WildHands and JointTransformer had 18\% and 40\% lower PA-MPJPE, respectively, than the non-egocentric HaMeR. JointTransformer was the best performing model with an error of 14.95 mm. A substantial surgical domain gap remains, and the widely deployed MediaPipe Hands detected a hand in only 24.1\% of evaluation rows. As a baseline, a 58K-parameter residual corrector (JRN) on frozen backbones reached 10.96-14.85 mm PA-MPJPE on held-out wearers for the two ego-pretrained backbones. We release the dataset and benchmark code at https://egosurghands.github.io

</details>


### [40] [V2VFusion: Text-Controlled Video-to-Video Diffusion for Degradation-Aware Video Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27063)
*Jiajun Chen,Han Xu,Yunfei Huang,Zhuoya Zhang,Guangcan Liu,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: V2VFusion 是首个统一的 video-to-video 融合框架，将视频融合重构为潜空间扩散中的视频条件生成问题，实现退化多源输入到时间连贯高质量融合视频的直接生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法逐帧融合，缺乏跨序列的统一时空建模，破坏时间连贯性；面对退化时忽略时间动态，并以单一网络承载多种退化，导致退化抑制有限且不均衡。

Method: 1) 将视频融合重构为潜空间扩散中的 video-to-video 条件生成，整体建模全序列时空依赖；2) 提出文本引导的退化条件 ControlNet，桥接高层文本退化描述与低层视觉先验，提供可解释、可控的结构保持融合引导；3) 设计状态调制的退化感知分层混合专家（MoE）模块，将专家选择解耦为扩散状态感知过滤与退化感知路由，抑制不可靠路由并促进专家专门化，缓解多退化间的表征冲突。

Result: 在红外–可见光、多曝光、多聚焦视频融合任务及复杂退化上验证，V2VFusion 在融合质量与时间稳定性上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过统一时空建模的扩散式 video-to-video 融合框架，结合文本条件控制与分层 MoE 的退化感知路由，可同时提升融合质量、序列一致性与复杂退化鲁棒性。

Abstract: Video fusion integrates complementary information in multiple source video streams into a single sequence for complete scene perception. Existing methods perform frame-wise fusion and lack unified spatio-temporal modeling across sequences, damaging temporal coherence across sequences. When dealing with degradations, they also ignore temporal dynamics and overload a single network with diverse degradations, leading to limited and imbalanced degradation suppression. To address these issues, we propose V2VFusion, the first unified video-to-video fusion framework that directly generates temporally coherent, high-quality fused videos from degraded multi-source inputs. By reformulating video fusion as a video-to-video conditional generation problem in latent diffusion space, our approach holistically models spatio-temporal dependencies across entire sequences and intrinsically ensures sequence-level consistency. On this basis, a text-guided degradation-conditioned ControlNet bridges high-level textual degradation descriptions with low-level visual priors, enabling interpretable and controllable guidance for structure-preserving fusion beyond purely data-driven conditioning. To handle heterogeneous and compound degradations, a state-modulated degradation-aware hierarchical mixture-of-experts module decouples expert selection into diffusion-state-aware filtering and degradation-aware routing. It prevents unreliable routing and fosters expert specialization to mitigate representation conflicts among diverse degradations. Experiments on infrared-visible, multi-exposure, and multi-focus video fusion tasks and complex degradations validate that V2VFusion surpasses existing methods in fusion quality and temporal stability. The code is available at https://github.com/ChenDoubleJ/V2VFusion.

</details>


### [41] [S2D-Splat: Static-First Gaussian Reconstruction of Dynamic Driving Scenes with Layer-Wise Feature Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27003)
*Jinheng Li,Tianchen Deng,Bufan Zhang,Qu Chen,Jianwu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S2D-Splat：一种静态优先的前馈高斯重建框架，通过分层特征引导在相机与几何估计前分离静态/动态特征，降低动态物体的干扰，并在Waymo及零样本nuScenes、MM-AU上取得多数指标领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景中运动物体带来跨帧不一致性，干扰相机估计与静态几何恢复。现有前馈高斯方法虽显式分离静态与动态基元，但仍从同时编码两者的共享特征中估计相机参数与几何，使静态重建易受动态成分干扰。

Method: 提出分层特征引导的静态优先框架：（1）在相机与几何估计之前先分离静态与动态特征，静态分支独立恢复相机参数与静态3D高斯；（2）逐层缓存中间静态特征，作为动态分支对应层的固定条件，形成非对称信息流，为动态高斯重建提供稳定几何上下文；（3）实例级运动解码器查询融合后的静动态表示以估计物体轨迹，支持运动对齐的高斯融合与时间插值。

Result: 在Waymo上多数评估指标优于现有方法；在nuScenes与MM-AU事故数据集的零样本评测中表现良好，可泛化至高度动态的碰撞前驾驶场景。

Conclusion: 先分离、后估计的静态优先设计配合非对称分层特征引导，能有效抑制动态干扰并保持静态表示稳定，同时为动态重建提供可靠几何上下文，具备良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Moving objects introduce cross-frame inconsistencies that complicate camera estimation and static geometry recovery in driving scenes. Recent feed-forward Gaussian methods explicitly separate static and dynamic primitives, but often estimate camera parameters and geometry from shared features that jointly encode both components, leaving static reconstruction susceptible to dynamic interference. To address this challenge, we propose S2D-Splat, a feed-forward framework for static-first Gaussian reconstruction with layer-wise feature guidance. Our framework separates static and dynamic features before camera and geometry estimation, enabling the static branch to recover camera parameters and static 3D Gaussians with reduced dynamic interference. Intermediate static features are cached at each layer and reused as fixed conditioning at corresponding layers of the dynamic branch. This asymmetric information flow preserves the established static representations while providing stable geometric context for reconstructing dynamic 3D Gaussians. An instance-level motion decoder further queries the fused static and dynamic representations to estimate object trajectories, supporting motion-aligned Gaussian fusion and temporal interpolation. Experiments on Waymo and zero-shot evaluations on nuScenes and the MM-AU accident dataset demonstrate that S2D-Splat outperforms existing approaches on most evaluated metrics and generalizes to highly dynamic, pre-collision driving scenarios.

</details>


### [42] [SparseHPS: A Unified Model for Human Pose and Shape across Depth Cameras and LiDARs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26730)
*Ilya Zaychuk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SparseHPS 是一个传感器无关的人体点云姿态与形状恢复网络，用一套权重统一处理不同密度、不同设备（深度相机/LiDAR）的单帧与流式输入，并在 LiDAR 与 Kinect 基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法各自针对单一传感器定制：不同设备的点密度相差十余倍，公开数据集标注使用互不兼容的人体模型，LiDAR 方法还需长序列补偿扫描稀疏，缺乏统一的跨传感器方案。

Method: 以 LitePT 主干在稀疏体素上编码点云；用可学习查询表示每个关节并由共享解码头解码；通过体素投票定位人体全局平移；用前七帧 token 的因果记忆提供时序上下文；在十个混合渲染点云与真实深度相机、LiDAR 采集的数据集上训练，并统一到同一人体模型（SMPL-X）。

Result: 在 SLOPER4D 与 FreeMotion LiDAR 基准上，相较需 32 帧的最强现有方法，根相对关节误差分别降低 22% 和 11%；在 HuMMan 未见受试者的真实 Azure Kinect 数据上关节误差为 34.7 mm，且仅用当前帧即可获得大部分精度。

Conclusion: 单一传感器无关网络可同时覆盖单帧与流式、深度相机与 LiDAR 场景，突破设备点密度差异与标注不统一的瓶颈，时序信息只是边际增益而非必需条件。

Abstract: Recovering 3D human pose and shape from point clouds is central to motion capture, robotics and self-driving vehicles, because depth cameras and LiDARs sense a person directly in metric 3D and work under any lighting. However, current approaches are each tailored to one sensor type: point density differs by more than ten times between devices, public datasets are labeled with incompatible body models, and LiDAR approaches need long input sequences to offset sparse scans. In this work, we present SparseHPS, a novel sensor-agnostic network that recovers SMPL-X pose, shape and global translation from a human point cloud with one set of weights, for single frames and streams alike. Specifically, SparseHPS encodes the cloud with a LitePT backbone over sparse voxels, represents every joint by a learnable query decoded with one common head, localizes the body by voting over voxels, and draws temporal context from a causal memory of tokens from the seven preceding frames. A single model is trained on ten datasets that mix rendered clouds with real depth-camera and LiDAR captures, all converted to one body model. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art results on the SLOPER4D and FreeMotion LiDAR benchmarks, where SparseHPS lowers the root-relative joint error by 22% and 11%, respectively, compared with the strongest prior method, which needs 32 frames. On real Azure Kinect captures of held-out HuMMan subjects, SparseHPS attains a joint error of 34.7 mm. Moreover, the current frame alone already yields the bulk of this accuracy.

</details>


### [43] [SAVIOR: Sample-efficient Adaptation of Vision-Language Models for OCR Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27319)
*Akshata A Bhat,Sharath Naganna,Saiful Haq,Prashant Khatri,Krishna Chaitanya Reddy Tamataam,Neha Arun,Niyati Chhaya,Pushpak Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向金融文档的样本高效数据策划方法 SAVIOR 与结构感知评测指标 PaIRS，微调 Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct 后在困难版式场景实现 0.93 词召回，配合文档问答 F1 达 0.89，优于 GPT-4o、Mistral-OCR、PaddleOCR-VL、DeepSeek-OCR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OCR 流水线与视觉语言模型在标准基准上准确率高，但在金融工作流中至关重要的困难版式（竖排/旋转文本、嵌入 logo 的供应商名称、监管细则小字、退化扫描件、复杂多栏布局）上系统性失效；这些模式在公开数据集中占比低，却构成大量真实失败案例。

Method: 提出 SAVIOR 样本高效数据策划方法，定向采集/构造上述高影响失败场景数据以适配视觉语言模型；并提出 PaIRS 结构感知评测指标，通过比较 token 间成对空间关系来衡量版面保真度；以 SAVIOR-Train 微调 Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct，并将版面感知 OCR 输出作为文档问答上下文。

Result: 微调后词召回率 0.93；将 OCR 输出用于文档问答时 F1 达 0.89，超过 GPT-4o、Mistral-OCR、PaddleOCR-VL、DeepSeek-OCR 等开源与闭源模型。

Conclusion: 针对高影响失败样本的定向数据策划可在样本高效前提下显著提升金融 OCR 鲁棒性，结构感知指标 PaIRS 有助于评估版面保真度，且高质量版面感知 OCR 能直接下游增益文档问答性能。

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) pipelines and vision-language models exhibit high accuracy on standard OCR and document-understanding benchmarks, yet they systematically underperform on document patterns that are operationally critical in financial workflows. These patterns include vertical or rotated text, logo-embedded vendor names, fine-print regulatory clauses, degraded scans, and complex multi-column layouts. While underrepresented in public datasets, they constitute a substantial portion of real-world failure cases. We introduce SAVIOR, a sample-efficient data curation methodology that targets such high-impact failure scenarios to adapt vision-language models for robust financial OCR. We further propose PaIRS, a structure-aware evaluation metric that measures layout fidelity by comparing pairwise spatial relationships between tokens. Fine-tuning Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct with SAVIOR-Train achieves a word recall of 0.93. When the resulting layout-aware OCR output is used as context for document question answering, it attains an F1 score of 0.89, outperforming both open- and closed-source models such as GPT-4o, Mistral-OCR, PaddleOCR-VL, and DeepSeek-OCR.

</details>


### [44] [What Does the Visual Encoder Buy? Visual Information Gain in Encoder-Free Multimodal Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26917)
*Zhixuan Ge,Anqi Li,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉信息增益（VIG）指标，将图文数据上的下一词损失分解为全局信息与布局信息，发现视觉编码器（如SigLIP 2）相对无编码器解码器的净优势几乎全部来自布局信息（尤其是读图内文字），而非全局图像统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有scaling研究仅用图文数据的文本下一词损失来比较无编码器与有编码器视觉语言模型，但该损失可在完全不用图像的情况下下降，且相同损失的两组训练在"从图像中获得什么"上可能不同，因此缺少一个能真正衡量"图像带来多少信息、带来何种信息"的评估指标。

Method: 定义视觉信息增益VIG：将图像替换为均匀图像后损失的上升量；并将其精确分解为全局信息（由图像统计量携带）与布局信息（打乱图块后即被破坏）。在158M–1.04B参数、以线性嵌入读取像素块、每10亿token评测至40B的纯解码器上实验，并评测19个公开检查点，以及在同一解码器前接入冻结SigLIP 2编码器的对比设置。

Result: 图像使解码器损失降低3%–46%（依图像类型而定）；全局信息在首个10亿token后即出现；布局信息起点接近零、随训练缓慢增长，且在6类图像中的5类上不随模型规模增长，更大批量能以更少布局信息达到相同损失。接入冻结SigLIP 2编码器后，仅当图像排布完整时损失才更低；在打乱图像上，有/无编码器的损失在6类中的5类上相差不超过0.04 nats。生产级无编码器模型（Gemma 4 12B、NEO、Mono-InternVL）确实习得布局信息：在相同文档上为0.53–0.76 nats，近期有编码器模型为0.65–0.82 nats，而作者的解码器仅0.04–0.07 nats，且其公开训练阶段显示该值持续上升。

Conclusion: 结论：两类模型从头训练时表现持平的报告与本文结果一致；预训练编码器所报告的优势应归因于布局信息，而非全局图像统计。VIG为评估视觉语言模型"是否真正利用图像"提供了可分解、可诊断的指标。

Abstract: Recent scaling studies ask whether vision-language models still need a visual encoder by comparing encoder-free and encoder-based models on the next-token loss of the text in image-text data. This loss can fall without any use of the image, and two runs with the same loss can differ in what they take from it. Visual information gain (VIG) is the loss increase when the image is replaced by a uniform one. It splits exactly into global information, carried by image statistics, and layout information, which shuffling the patches within the image destroys. We apply it to decoders with 158M to 1.04B parameters that read pixel patches through a linear embedding, evaluated every billion tokens up to 40B, and to 19 public checkpoints. In our decoders the image lowers the loss by 3% to 46%, depending on the image type. Global information is present after the first billion tokens. Layout information starts near zero, grows slowly with training and does not grow with model size on five of six image types, and a larger batch reaches the same loss with less of it. With a frozen SigLIP 2 encoder in front of the same decoder the loss is lower only when the arrangement of the image is intact. On shuffled images the losses with and without the encoder agree to within 0.04 nats on five of six image types, so the net advantage of the encoder in the loss is layout information, above all the ability to read text in images. Public encoder-free models at production scale (Gemma 4 12B, NEO, Mono-InternVL) do acquire layout information. On the same documents they have 0.53 to 0.76 nats of it, against 0.65 to 0.82 for recent encoder-based models and 0.04 to 0.07 for our decoders, and their released training stages show it rising. These results agree with the reported parity of the two families when both are trained from scratch and place the reported advantage of a pretrained encoder in layout information.

</details>


### [45] [Taking Apart and Putting Together: Tracking Objects Through Physical Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27398)
*Davide Gardenal,Mingxuan Liu,Flavio Vella,Quentin Guimard,Elisa Ricci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出新任务TTU（跟踪物体在物理装配/拆卸过程中的演化），并给出免训练框架TIPO与新数据集VISTA，实现SOTA的跟踪与状态图精度，且速度较已有方法快6–15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频目标跟踪与基础模型（如SAM 2/3）仅能在外观变化下维持tracklet，无法在物体发生物理组装/拆卸、实体新生时更新语义身份；已有工作只处理拆卸，且依赖仅在跟踪失败时触发的昂贵搜索。

Method: 提出TIPO（Tracking Instances via Proposals and Overlaps），免训练框架，采用generate-evaluate-judge流程：从不连通的掩码分量生成拆分与合并提议，用内部与外部重叠度对照mask-decoder先验打分，再对最优提议查询MLLM做视觉验证与语义标注，持续维护状态图。

Result: 构建VISTA数据集（50段序列、超26,000帧、124个装配与132个拆卸事件及状态图标注）；TIPO在VISTA上取得SOTA的跟踪与状态图精度，运行速度比先前方法快6×–15×。

Conclusion: 以提议生成+重叠度评估+MLLM验证的免训练范式可有效支撑装配/拆卸场景下的持续跟踪与语义更新，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: We introduce Transformation Tracking and Understanding (TTU), extending video object tracking to environments where objects undergo physical assemblies and disassemblies. TTU requires continuously tracking objects through transformations, identifying new entities, maintaining a state graph of their relationships, and assigning semantic labels to transformations. While foundation models (e.g., SAM 2/3) maintain tracklets through appearance changes, they cannot update semantic identities as objects physically evolve. Prior work addresses only disassemblies, relying on expensive searches triggered only when tracking fails. We propose Tracking Instances via Proposals and Overlaps (TIPO), a training-free framework that continuously infers transformations via a generate-evaluate-judge pipeline. TIPO generates split and merge proposals from disjoint mask components, scores them against mask-decoder priors using inner and external overlap, and queries an MLLM on the top proposal for visual verification and semantic labeling. To benchmark TTU, we curate Video Instances, States, and Transformation Annotations (VISTA), a dataset including state graphs with 124 assembly and 132 disassembly events across 50 sequences and over 26,000 frames. TIPO achieves state-of-the-art tracking and state-graph accuracy on VISTA, while operating $6\times$ to $15\times$ faster than prior methods. Project page: https://davidegardenal.github.io/tipo-tracking

</details>


### [46] [Prune to Expand: Training Prompt Enhancers with Less to Imitate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27229)
*Tianhe Wu,Qilin Sun,Kede Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现"生成增益大的caption对"并不比"增益小的caption对"带来更好的提示增强模型，指出稠密caption中与用户意图弱相关的冗余细节构成冗余监督，并提出纯文本方法Prune to Expand (P2E)通过逐例有界删除冗余句子来精炼监督信号，在相同训练配置下提升人类偏好对齐与视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Prompt Enhancement (PE) 的监督微调数据构造方式，是从稠密caption反推出短的用户式prompt，并隐含假设"能改善图像生成的caption就是有效监督"。但作者发现这一假设不成立：在生成增益显著与增益微弱的caption对上训练的PE模型表现相当。作者认为稠密caption要求PE模型复现与用户意图弱相关的图像特定细节，形成了冗余监督，因此需要精炼监督信号而非单纯追求生成增益。

Method: 提出Prune to Expand (P2E)，一种纯文本（text-only）方法：在逐样本删除上限（per-example deletion bound）约束下，选择性地删除稠密caption中的冗余句子，同时保留显式用户需求与有信息量的视觉结构，从而构造更优的SFT监督对。

Result: 在与传统稠密caption SFT完全相同的训练配置下进行大量实验，P2E在多种图像类别和多种视觉生成器上，均提升了与人类偏好的一致性以及生成图像的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 监督数据的"生成增益"不等于"监督有效性"；减少稠密caption中的冗余细节可显著改善PE模型。P2E作为一种简单、仅需文本的数据精炼方法，可作为稠密caption监督的替代方案，具有跨类别、跨生成器的泛化性。

Abstract: Prompt enhancement (PE) refines concise user prompts into detailed descriptions to improve the compositional aesthetics of generated images while preserving user intent. A common practice constructs supervised fine-tuning (SFT) pairs by deriving short, user-like prompts from dense captions, implicitly assuming that captions that improve image generation also provide effective supervision. In this paper, however, we uncover a counterintuitive finding: PE models trained on caption pairs with substantial generation gains perform comparably to those trained on pairs with only marginal gains. We argue that dense captions can impose redundant supervision by requiring PE models to reproduce image-specific details weakly related to user intent. Motivated by this discrepancy, we introduce Prune to Expand (P2E), a simple text-only method that refines dense-caption supervision by selectively removing redundant sentences under a per-example deletion bound while preserving explicit user requirements and informative visual structure. Extensive experiments under identical training configurations demonstrate that P2E improves alignment with human preferences and visual quality over conventional dense-caption SFT across diverse image categories and visual generators.

</details>


### [47] [EAST: Event-Anchored Surface Transport for Event-Based Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27224)
*Ke Luo,Wei Zhai,Yuliang Wu,Pengfei Yu,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Event-Anchored Surface Transport (EAST)，将事件约束的符号距离场（SDF）与高斯渲染耦合，通过三角化候选锚定零水平集、事件时序测得的法向流约束射线-表面交点深度，并以冻结初始参考场与实时场的描述子差异对高斯中心施加有界修正，从而在保持显式渲染效率的同时恢复连续表面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率与大动态范围，高斯重建渲染高效，但仅靠视觉外观的亮度变化监督会与深度误差相互补偿（外观歧义），导致连续表面难以恢复。

Method: 1) 将事件约束的 SDF 与高斯渲染联合建模；2) 用三角化支持的候选点锚定 SDF 零集；3) 由事件时序估计的法向流约束射线-表面相交深度；4) 以实时场与冻结初始参考场的描述子差异对高斯中心做有界修正，该修正初始为零但可将图像梯度回传至实时场。

Result: 在 PAEv3D 与 NeRF Synthetic 上相较高斯基线提升了表面精度与参考视角渲染质量，并可从所学场中高效提取网格。

Conclusion: 以事件几何（三角化候选与法向流）作为锚定约束、以有界描述子传输连接隐式场与高斯基元，可有效缓解外观-深度歧义，兼顾表面几何精度与显式渲染效率。

Abstract: Event-supervised Gaussian reconstruction combines the high temporal resolution and wide dynamic range of event cameras with efficient explicit rendering. However, recovering a continuous surface remains difficult: changes in rendered appearance can compensate for depth errors while preserving the predicted brightness changes. We introduce Event-Anchored Surface Transport (EAST), which couples an event-constrained signed distance field to Gaussian rendering. Triangulation-supported candidates anchor the zero set, while normal flow measured from event timing constrains ray-surface intersection depths. Gaussian centers receive bounded corrections from the descriptor difference between the live field and a frozen initial reference. The correction is zero at initialization, yet transmits image gradients to the live field. Experiments on PAEv3D and NeRF Synthetic show improved surface accuracy and reference-view rendering over Gaussian baselines, together with efficient mesh extraction from the learned field.

</details>


### [48] [Tokenize Moments, Not Frames: Tokenizer-Aware Budget Allocation for Training-Free Video LLM Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26933)
*Zirui Zhao,Kai Liu,Linghe Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法ESSENCE：在编码前按"时间片段"而非"帧"进行token化，用闭式规则设定token网格数与降维维度，并释放后层视觉token，在高TTFT匹配下普遍优于现有加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频LLM用数千视觉token表示视频，免训练的剪枝/合并加速器在长视频上掉精度。作者通过分析帧相似性与16个开源模型的视觉tokenizer发现，损失发生在token选择之前：在token预算约束下，长视频帧采样间隔大于帧间保持相似约2s的窗口，Qwen-VL类双帧3D patch会把两个不相关场景混合进单个视觉token，形成"双重曝光"，且发生在任何剪枝/合并之前。

Method: ESSENCE为免训练方法：以"片段（片段内帧保持相似）"为token化单位而非单帧；编码前用基于采样间隔和tokenizer类别的闭式规则确定token网格数量与需降维的维度；随后在LLM较后层释放视觉token。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL、Qwen2.5-VL、InternVL3.5上与五个近期加速器对比，在平均TTFT匹配条件下，60次比较中58次精度更高；用80%token达到完整Qwen模型平均精度且TTFT快1.6倍；在GLM-4.1V上最高超过完整模型3.4个点。

Conclusion: 长视频token损失的根源在token选择之前的3D patch双重曝光问题；按时段token化可有效规避，ESSENCE在多种模型上实现精度-效率更优的免训练加速。

Abstract: Video large language models (LLMs) represent each video with thousands of visual tokens, and training-free accelerators that prune or merge these tokens lose accuracy on long videos. Analyzing frame similarity and the vision tokenizers of 16 open models, we trace this loss to a step before token selection. Under a token budget, the frames of a long video are sampled farther apart than the roughly 2 s over which frames remain similar. The two-frame 3D patches of models such as Qwen-VL then blend two unrelated scenes into each visual token, a double exposure that arises before any pruning or merging. Fixed-size tokenizers such as InternVL's avoid this blend but respond differently to the same reduction. We propose ESSENCE, a training-free method that tokenizes moments, segments within which frames remain similar, rather than frames. Before encoding, a closed-form rule uses the sampling interval and the tokenizer class to set the number of token grids and the dimension to reduce; visual tokens are then released from later LLM layers. Against five recent accelerators on Qwen3-VL, Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3.5, ESSENCE achieves higher accuracy in 58 of 60 comparisons at matched mean time to first token (TTFT). With 80% of the tokens, it matches the average accuracy of the full Qwen models at $1.6\times$ faster TTFT; on GLM-4.1V, it exceeds the full model by up to 3.4 points. Code is available at: https://github.com/zhkandsrz/ESSENCE.

</details>


### [49] [Perceive Once, Escalate on Demand: Handing Off Visual KV Memory from Small to Large VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26902)
*Qiang Zhou,Jianing Deng,Siyan Dong,Jingxian Chen,Han-Yi Lin,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Baton 将小模型的 KV cache 通过无标签逐层线性映射迁移给大模型，替代级联中昂贵的重编码历史，在 RoboMME 等任务上以更少算力达到接近重看的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频具身智能体无法在每步都运行大 VLM，级联方案只在不确定时升级到大模型；但每次升级都迫使大模型重编码小模型已感知的历史，这是主要成本来源。作者追问大模型究竟需要"忠实的自身缓存"还是"可用的记忆"。

Method: 关键发现：能最精确复现大模型自身 KV cache 的映射式方案并不优于小模型单独使用。Baton 转而从大模型自身的答案中无标签地蒸馏一个逐层线性映射，把视觉记忆从小模型 KV cache 投射到大模型隐空间；大模型只需编码当前帧。作者通过对映射的干预实验定位增益来源。

Result: 在 RoboMME 上优于小模型单独使用、剪枝/截断式重看以及文本记忆；作为级联升级通道，与重看在约 1 分以内持平，算力降低 28–47%，64 帧时每次升级省 8 倍。增益随大模型规模（8B→32B）增长，并在四个家族八个模型对上与"大模型剩余空间"相关，零样本迁移至 OpenEQA。干预表明增益集中于某一方向，由大模型中间三分之一层承载。

Conclusion: 大模型需要的是可用记忆而非自缓存复刻；无标签逐层线性映射是高效、可迁移的跨模型记忆传递方案，可作为级联视觉语言系统的低成本升级通道。

Abstract: An embodied agent cannot afford a large vision-language model (VLM) at every step of a long video, so a cascade runs a small VLM continuously and escalates only uncertain decisions to a large one. Each escalation, however, forces the large model to re-encode a history the small model has already perceived, and this dominates its cost. We present Baton, which hands the large model the small model's visual memory, its key-value (KV) cache over the history. Baton rests on one finding: the large model needs a usable memory, not a faithful copy of its own. A map trained to reproduce the large model's cache matches it most closely, yet brings no gain over the small model alone. Baton instead distills a per-layer linear map, without labels, from the large model's own answers, with the large model encoding only the current frame. Interventions on the map trace the gain to one direction, carried by the middle third of the large model's layers. On RoboMME, Baton beats the small model alone, a pruned or shortened re-watch, and a text memory; as the escalation channel of a cascade it matches re-watching to within about one point at 28-47% less compute, and 8x less per escalation at 64 frames. Its gain grows with the large model, from 8B to 32B, tracks the large model's headroom across eight model pairs from four families, and transfers zero-shot to OpenEQA.

</details>


### [50] [AppCon-Bench: Perception and Misperception of Material Appearance in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27453)
*Santiago Jimenez-Navarro,Ana Serrano,Belen Masia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 AppCon-Bench 基准，用五项任务在受控的几何与光照变化下评估视觉语言模型（VLM）的材料外观恒常性，并与人类观察者对比，揭示二者在材料感知上的相似性与系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类具备显著的材料外观恒常性，能在光照、形状等外在线索变化下识别物体表面的本征物理属性；而 VLM 虽在语义视觉任务上表现强劲，其对细微中层感知属性（如材料外观恒常性）的能力尚不清楚，缺乏系统评测手段。

Method: 构建 AppCon-Bench 基准，包含五项任务，在受控的几何与光照变化条件下考察材料外观恒常性；对代表性 VLM 与人类观察者进行评测，并分析二者在跨观察条件与跨实验语境下材料判断的一致性。

Result: 分析发现人类与 VLM 的材料外观感知既存在相似之处，也存在系统性差异；并指出仅看总体准确率不足以揭示感知层面的成功与失败模式。

Conclusion: 应超越单一准确率指标，细粒度评估感知成败，以定位当前 VLM 的改进方向；AppCon-Bench 为材料外观恒常性研究提供了可复用的评测框架。

Abstract: Identifying the same material across changes in illumination or geometry is a critical ability for robust vision systems. Humans exhibit remarkable material appearance constancy, being able to recognize the intrinsic physical properties of an object's surface material across variations in extrinsic, confounding factors such as the lighting or the shape of such object. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong performance across a wide range of semantic vision tasks, yet their ability to interpret subtle mid-level perceptual attributes remains less understood. We introduce AppCon-Bench, a benchmark comprising five tasks designed to assess material appearance constancy under controlled variations in geometry and illumination, and evaluate representative VLMs alongside human observers, investigating their ability to maintain consistent material judgments across viewing conditions and experimental contexts. After thorough analysis, we identify several key findings that reveal both similarities and systematic differences between human and VLM material appearance perception, highlighting the value of evaluating perceptual successes and failures beyond overall accuracy and informing areas of improvement for current VLMs.

</details>


### [51] [Projector Time Map Calibration for Event-based Structured Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27412)
*Buu-Hoang-Lam Le,Kota Mizutani,Shohei Nobuhara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有基于事件相机的结构光方法依赖"投影仪平行光栅扫描+传感器逐像素独立打时间戳"两个错误假设；本文改用直接标识投影仪像素的时间编码获得对应关系，无需先验标定，并从同一段记录中自标定投影仪时间映射及系统几何与时钟偏移，仅需数秒前导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的基于事件的结构光方法从事件时间戳推导稠密的投影仪-相机对应关系，但其隐含的两个前提——投影仪以平行光栅扫描、传感器对每个像素独立打时间戳——均不成立，因而导致对应关系精度与标定依赖问题。

Method: 1）用时间编码直接标识投影仪像素，从而直接获得对应关系，无需预先标定；2）从同一段记录中估计投影仪时间映射（projector time map）；3）通过数秒前导段中的时空自标定，恢复系统几何结构以及各时钟之间的偏移（offset），无需参考物、也无需第二台相机。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果；定性结果是：方法在不依赖上述两个假设下可获得投影仪-相机对应关系，并能仅凭同一记录完成投影仪时间映射标定与相机-投影仪时钟偏移估计。

Conclusion: 该工作指出现有事件结构光标定的假设缺陷，提出免标定、基于时间编码的对应关系获取与时空自标定框架，简化了硬件与流程（无需参考物与附加相机），为事件相机结构光系统的实用化提供方案。

Abstract: Existing event-based structured light obtains a dense projector-camera correspondence from the timestamp of an event. It assumes that the projector scans a parallel raster and that the sensor timestamps each pixel independently, and we show that neither assumption holds. We obtain the correspondence from a temporal code that identifies the projector pixel directly and therefore needs no prior calibration, we calibrate the projector time map from the same recording, and we recover the geometry of the system and the offsets between its clocks by a spatio-temporal self-calibration in a preamble of a few seconds that needs no reference object and no second camera.

</details>


### [52] [Borrow the Style, Follow the Prompt: Progressive Reference-Guided Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27360)
*Yu Lin,Zi-Jun Wang,Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProStyle：通过跨内容监督学习"内容抑制的风格先验"，并以弱条件注入辅助层，配合残差修正互补通路，实现参考图风格迁移与文本内容控制的解耦；同时发布ReConStyle数据集与评测体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考引导的文本到图像风格化中，参考图中的主体与背景会干扰生成内容所描绘的对象；而直接抑制这些无用信息又会丢失可迁移的纹理与材质线索，形成"内容控制"与"风格保真"之间的张力。

Method: 1) 构造同风格、不同主体与背景的成对数据，通过跨内容监督学习内容抑制的风格先验；2) 将该先验以弱条件方式注入辅助层以强化风格表达；3) 在风格通路冻结的前提下，用完整参考图的patch特征学习残差修正的互补通路；4) 两条通路在统一目标下联合精修；5) 构建ReConStyle数据集（134K训练对、两种测试设置）及目标内容保真度、参考物体重叠度、风格相似度、文本对齐的物体级指标。

Result: 在ReConStyle-Full上优于所有对比的SD1.5基线，四项自动指标与三项人工指标的平均排名为1.86；用户研究中文本对齐与综合适用性选择率分别达84.36%和50.90%；在外部Style-Rank基准上取得最高CLIP-Text与ImageReward分数。

Conclusion: 内容抑制的风格先验加弱条件注入与残差互补通路，可在保留可迁移纹理/材质线索的同时有效抑制参考主体与背景干扰，实现更好的内容可控性与风格保真平衡。

Abstract: Reference-guided text-to-image stylization transfers visual appearance to content specified by text, but the reference subjects and backgrounds can interfere with what the generated image depicts. Suppressing this unwanted information can also discard transferable texture and material cues, creating a tension between content control and faithful preservation of the reference style. Accordingly, we propose ProStyle, which learns a content-suppressed style prior through cross-content supervision on same-style pairs with different subjects and backgrounds and injects it with weak conditioning at auxiliary layers to strengthen style expression. Building on this prior, a complementary pathway learns residual corrections from full reference patch features with the content-suppressed style pathway frozen, before both pathways undergo joint refinement under the same objective. For cross-content learning and evaluation, we introduce ReConStyle, which provides 134K training pairs, two test settings, and object-level measures of target-content fidelity and reference-object overlap alongside style similarity and text alignment. ProStyle outperforms all compared SD1.5-based baselines and achieves the best average rank of 1.86 across four automatic and three human-evaluation criteria on ReConStyle-Full. In the user study, it substantially outperforms the baselines in text alignment and combined suitability, with selection rates of 84.36% and 50.90%, respectively. On the external Style-Rank benchmark, it also achieves the highest CLIP-Text and ImageReward scores.

</details>


### [53] [CoGITAtion: Collaborative Gaze-based Interpretable Task-adaptive Representation for Privacy-sensitive Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27340)
*Mayar Elfares,Yasmine Elfares,Andreas Bulling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoGITAtion：面向纵向分布眼动数据的隐私保护、任务自适应、可解释推理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼动分析蕴含丰富的行���信息，适用于隐私敏感场景，但实际部署受制于数据异构、稀缺、分布式三大挑战。

Method: 将变长眼动记录在本地转化为紧凑、可解释的眼动转移图与描述子（特征计算在下游）；任务自适应模型通过任务门控与可用性门控兼容异构及缺失特征块，避免不必要的私有计算；公开可用性门控剪除由公开已知任务与所有者可用性决定的加密计算分支且不改变预测。

Result: 共享组件可捕获跨任务泛化模式，任务特定组件在不增大模型体量的前提下提供额外容量；公开可用性门控有效减少加密计算量且预测不变。

Conclusion: CoGITAtion 实现眼动模型复用、高效隐私保护部署，并在所有者可用性不完整时保持稳健推理，同时保留可解释的眼动表示。

Abstract: Gaze-based analysis provides rich behavioural information for privacy-sensitive applications, but deployment is challenged by heterogeneous, scarce, and distributed data. We present CoGITAtion (Collaborative Gaze-based Interpretable Task-Adaptive Representation), a framework for privacy-preserving inference over vertically distributed gaze data. CoGITAtion transforms variable-length gaze recordings into compact, interpretable gaze-transition graphs and descriptors computed locally by each data owner. Task-adaptive models accommodate heterogeneous and missing feature blocks through task and availability gating, avoiding unnecessary private computation. Results show that shared components capture patterns that generalise across tasks, while task-specific components provide capacity without increasing model size. Public availability gating further reduces encrypted computation by removing branches determined by publicly known task and owner availability without altering predictions. Overall, CoGITAtion enables gaze model reuse, efficient privacy-preserving deployment, and robust inference under incomplete owner availability while retaining interpretable gaze representations.

</details>


### [54] [Zing-1.0: A Playable World System Built on Controllable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27336)
*Wenbo Zhang,Guoli Zhang,Xuewen Yu,Xueliang Yang,Jiangqiao Yan,Teng Xia,Xinmiao Wu,Zeyang Tian,Haoze Sun,Jinghui Niu,Hongli Ning,Zhiyuan Liu,Xinna Liu,Weimin Liu,Jialin Liu,Tianpeng Li,Kejun Li,Jun Li,Jiawen Li,Yirong He,Haoyuan Guo,Bosheng Gong,Junhao Gao,Xiaoxiao Fu,Xuezhi Chen,Shengdong Chen,Mingyang Chen,Diying Chen,Yinglun Chai,Yiwen Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Zing-1.0 是一个基于“可控渲染”构建的可玩世界系统：WorldKit 让编码智能体构建可执行的代理世界，MiniMax-H3 改造成块因果流式渲染器将代理视频合成为玩家视角，交互运行时实现低延迟游戏闭环，单机部署达 23.82 FPS@832×480。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成/世界模型缺乏可执行的世界规则与交互闭环，且缺少与实现无关的系统规范；需要把“可执行世界”与“流式视频渲染”解耦，使行为由引擎确定、外观由生成模型合成，从而兼顾可控性、一致性与实时性。

Method: (1) 提出 Controllable Rendering 范式，并形式化定义世界构建、世界执行、观测合成与交互运行时的接口，给出首个与实现无关的 Playable World System 规范；(2) AI 原生引擎 WorldKit：面向编码智能体的结构化创作工具与可复用角色/相机/控制组件，分离可执行行为与最终外观，支持可扩展交互与精确几何运动控制；(3) 流式渲染器：将 MiniMax-H3 改造为块因果自回归生成 + 增量解码，代理视频约束结构与运动，文本与独立场景/主体参考控制外观，多任务训练并蒸馏至少步去噪；(4) 交互基础设施：Interaction Runtime 将玩家动作路由至 WorldKit，支持游戏内提示与参考更新，采用跨块联合执行与压缩 K/V 稀疏注意力降低推理开销。

Result: 流式渲染器在 OpenVE 上 3.90、LongV2VBench 上 3.44，领先对比的流式编辑器；StreamEval 上指令编辑 57.86、参考编辑 54.06，两条赛道均超过已公开基线。服务系统在单张 H200 运行 DiT、两张 H100 分别负责编解码的条件下，832×480 稳态吞吐 23.82 FPS。

Conclusion: 该工作以“可执行代理世界 + 流式视频合成”的分层架构首次给出可玩世界系统的实现无关规范，并验证了在交互场景下同时实现可控性、编辑质量与实时吞吐的可行性。

Abstract: We present Zing-1.0, a Playable World System built on Controllable Rendering. An executable engine resolves player actions under the world's rules and produces proxy video from the updated state. A streaming video model synthesizes the player's view from this proxy, and the runtime delivers it to inform subsequent actions. (1) Principle and system. We formulate Controllable Rendering and define the interfaces among world construction, world execution, observation synthesis, and the interaction runtime. To our knowledge, this is the first implementation-independent specification of a Playable World System. (2) AI-native engine. WorldKit provides structured authoring tools and reusable character, camera, and control components for coding agents to construct executable proxy worlds. It supports extensible interactions and precise geometric motion control while separating executable behavior from final visual appearance. (3) Streaming renderer. We adapt MiniMax-H3 for block-causal autoregressive generation and incremental decoding. Proxy video constrains structure and motion, while text and separate scene and subject references specify appearance. We train the generator on diverse tasks and distill it to generate each block in a few denoising steps. The renderer leads the compared streaming editors on OpenVE (3.90) and LongV2VBench (3.44). On StreamEval, Zing-1.0 scores 57.86 on instruction editing and 54.06 on reference editing, exceeding the published baselines on both tracks. (4) Interaction infrastructure. We build an Interaction Runtime that routes player actions to WorldKit while supporting in-play prompt and reference updates. Cross-block joint execution and sparse attention with compressed K/V reduce inference overhead. With the DiT on a single H200 and encoding and decoding on one H100 each, the serving system achieves a steady-state throughput of 23.82 FPS at $832\times480$.

</details>


### [55] [Can Reasoning and Image Description Enhance Yes/No Visual Mathematical Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27302)
*Guangcheng Song,Weichuan Wang,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究显式推理与文本图像描述能否提升视觉语言模型在二元是/否视觉数学问题上的表现，通过构建配对评估集并在不同提示与输入条件下测试开源VLM，发现CoT提示有效、文本描述不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在通用视觉任务上表现优异，但在理解数学图像方面仍受限制，作者希望探究显式推理与文本图像描述这两类手段能否改善其在视觉数学判断题上的理解能力。

Method: 构建了一个配对评估集（paired evaluation set），在多种提示与输入条件下对主流开源VLM进行基准测试；采用Chain-of-Thought提示，并用文本描述替换或增强视觉输入，以隔离和评估数学理解能力；指标上关注配对一致准确率。

Result: 显式推理（CoT）能提升性能，尤其在配对一致准确率上；文本图像描述并不能稳定带来收益，甚至可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 推理对于稳定可靠的视觉数学判断至关重要，而基于描述的中介表示需要更细致的设计。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on general visual tasks, yet their ability to understand mathematical images remains limited. This paper investigates whether explicit reasoning and textual image descriptions can enhance VLMs' performance on binary yes/no visual mathematical questions. To achieve this, we curated a paired evaluation set to benchmark prominent open-source VLMs under varying prompt and input conditions. In particular, we leverage Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting and use textual descriptions, either replacing or augmenting visual inputs, to isolate and evaluate mathematical comprehension. Our results show that explicit reasoning improves performance, especially in paired consistency accuracy, while textual image descriptions do not consistently help and may degrade performance. These findings suggest that reasoning is important for stable visual mathematical judgement, whereas description-based intermediates require more careful design.

</details>


### [56] [MODULE: A Mutual-Promoting Deep Unfolding Framework Towards Degradation-Robust Multi-modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27269)
*Yunfei Deng,Han Xu,Jiayi Ma,Guangcan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出理论启发的互促深度展开框架（MODULE），将退化鲁棒的图像融合重构为联合优化问题，并展开为多阶段网络，实现恢复与融合的双向信息交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图像融合对场景表征至关重要，但真实退化会损害其效果，因此需要退化鲁棒的融合范式。现有方法存在级联次优解（分阶段处理导致误差累积）和黑箱不可解释两大缺陷。

Method: 将退化鲁棒融合建模为联合优化问题，利用退化模型与模态生成机制显式建模单任务先验与跨任务依赖；把复杂多任务优化分解为任务相关的迭代子问题，建立恢复与融合间的双向互惠信息流；再将迭代过程展开为多阶段深度网络，每个模块显式对应特定数学运算。融合的跨模态先验正则化病态恢复问题，净化后的模态特征持续精炼融合输出。

Result: 大量实验表明该范式在取得最先进性能的同时具备更优的可解释性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过模型驱动与数据驱动相结合，该框架以透明架构同时兼具二者优点，为退化鲁棒多模态融合提供了兼具性能与可解释性的新思路。

Abstract: Multi-modal image fusion is crucial for comprehensive scene representation, yet real-world degradations compromise its efficacy, necessitating degradation-robust fusion paradigms. Existing degradation-robust multi-modal image fusion methods are often hindered by cascaded sub-optimal solution or black-box opacity. To address these challenges, we propose a theory-inspired mutual-promoting deep unfolding framework. It reformulates degradation-robust image fusion as a joint optimization problem, utilizing the degradation model and modality generation mechanism to explicitly model both single-task priors and cross-task dependencies. By decomposing the complex multi-task optimization problem into task-related iterative subproblems, the framework establishes a bidirectional reciprocal information flow between restoration and fusion. This process is further unfolded into a multi-stage deep neural network, where each component explicitly corresponds to a specific mathematical operation. In our framework, the fused cross-modal prior actively regularizes the ill-posed restoration, while the purified modality features continuously refine the fusion output. It ensures a transparent architecture that combines the merits of both model-based and data-driven methodologies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our paradigm achieves state-of-the-art performance while providing superior interpretability. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Meskend/MODULE.

</details>


### [57] [PP-CFG: Polarity-Preserving Guidance for Controllable Few-Step Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27266)
*Junyi He,Xu Peng,Chengming Xu,Sixiao Zheng,Donghao Luo,Chao Wen,Peng Tang,Xijie Huang,Chengjie Wang,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PP-CFG 将正/负文本条件在共享视觉主干内以相反符号组合注意力响应，并通过 Scale-Time Polarity Mixer 使提示影响随引导尺度与去噪步动态变化，从而在少步扩散蒸馏中保留可调 CFG 与运行时负提示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步扩散蒸馏虽降低采样成本，但常削弱可调的无分类器引导（CFG）与运行时负提示能力，现有少步学生模型难以保持连续尺度输入与可选负提示。

Method: 提出 PP-CFG 架构：正负文本条件分别保持，在共享视觉主干内以相反符号融合注意力响应；Scale-Time Polarity Mixer 依据引导尺度与去噪时间步对每个条件进行调制；学生模型保留连续尺度输入和可选择的负提示，每步仅一次视觉主干前向。在 Wan2.1（视频，DMD）与 Qwen-Image（LCM 与 DMD）上验证。

Result: Wan2.1 上四步达到 81.46 VBench，且在多种引导尺度下师生距离低于步蒸馏 DMD；在 NegElement-1K（1000 组配对图像/视频提示基准）上目标移除率高于对应教师模型；生成质量具竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法在注意力架构、生成模态与蒸馏目标上均具通用性，可在少步采样下恢复可调 CFG 与负提示能力而不显著牺牲质量。

Abstract: Few-step diffusion distillation reduces sampling cost but often weakens adjustable classifier-free guidance (CFG) and runtime negative prompting. We introduce PP-CFG, an architecture that keeps positive and negative text conditions separate and combines their attention responses with opposite signs within a shared visual backbone. Its Scale-Time Polarity Mixer refines each condition using the guidance scale and denoising timestep, allowing prompt influence to vary throughout generation. The student retains a continuous scale input and selectable negative prompts with one visual backbone pass per step. We evaluate PP-CFG on Wan2.1 for video generation with distribution matching distillation (DMD), and on Qwen-Image with both latent consistency model (LCM) distillation and DMD. PP-CFG achieves 81.46 VBench in four steps and lower video teacher-student distances across guidance scales than step-distilled DMD. On NegElement-1K, our benchmark of 1,000 matched image and video prompt cases, it achieves higher target-removal rates than the corresponding teachers. These experiments support the method's generality across attention architectures, generation modalities, and distillation objectives while retaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [58] [EAGLE: Entity-Aware Grounding with Layered Evidence for High-Resolution Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27264)
*Zhongjian Zou,Quan Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EAGLE是一个插件式框架，通过筛选视觉接地注意力头并结合实体感知接地与分层证据构建，提升MLLM对高分辨率图像的细粒度理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在高分辨率图像细粒度理解上存在两个问题：不同注意力头对目标实体及其属性的接地可靠性差异大；局部细节与整体场景上下文割裂。

Method: 先用细粒度数据集筛选出具有视觉接地能力的注意力头，再进行实体感知接地以提取目标特定区域；随后构建分层视觉证据，并借助问题引导的路由选择最优视图组合，输入冻结的MLLM进行增强。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B与InternVL3-8B两个骨干上均获得一致性提升，并在V*、HRBench-4K、HRBench-8K上以相同骨干取得SOTA。

Conclusion: 注意力头筛选与分层证据组织相结合是提升高分辨率图像理解的有效途径，且该框架具备跨骨干的通用性与即插即用特性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) still struggle with fine-grained understanding of high-resolution images: attention heads vary in their reliability for grounding target entities and their attributes, while localized details are often disconnected from the broader scene context. We propose EAGLE (Entity-Aware Grounding with Layered Evidence for High-Resolution Image Understanding), a plug-in framework that jointly selects grounding signals and organizes hierarchical visual evidence. EAGLE employs a fine-grained dataset to screen visually-grounded attention heads, followed by entity-aware grounding to extract target-specific regions. It then integrates hierarchical evidence construction and question-guided routing to select optimal view combinations, feeding them into the frozen MLLM to enhance fine-grained visual understanding. Experiments with Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3-8B demonstrate that EAGLE consistently improves performance across both backbones and achieves SOTA results on V*, HRBench-4K, and HRBench-8K compared with existing methods using the same backbone.

</details>


### [59] [FedQCal: Quadratic Programming with Dual-Space Calibration for One-Shot Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27170)
*Shunsei Koshibuchi,Hiroshi Kera,Kazuhiko Kawamoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FedQCal，用小型服务器标注集训练的参考模型定义对齐约束，通过最小范数二次规划（QP）求解聚合系数并避免系数过大，从而在一次上传后完成异构客户端模型的有效合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮联邦学习（one-shot FL）必须在一次上传后合并异构客户端模型，而各客户端本地类别分布不同，难以判断哪些客户端更新应被保留、以何种权重保留。

Method: 以服务器小规模标注数据训练参考模型，给出聚合的“对齐要求”；构建最小范数二次规划，在满足参考对齐的前提下最小化系数范数，避免不必要的大聚合系数；用同一批服务器样本上的分类损失经两个对偶变量选择合并方案，使对齐阈值无需预先指定。理论上证明所选系数在其诱导阈值处仍为精确 QP 解，并给出扰动界说明系数范数如何限制投影误差的放大。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100 与 SVHN 上，多数设置下优于单轮 FedAvg；消融实验支持参考引导加权与系数校准的有效性，且每类仅需一张标注服务器图像即可生效。

Conclusion: 参考模型引导 + 最小范数 QP 的对偶式阈值选择，可在免预设对齐阈值的条件下实现稳健的单轮异构模型聚合，且对标注数据需求极低。

Abstract: One-shot federated learning must combine heterogeneous client models after a single upload. Different local class distributions make it difficult to decide which client updates to retain. We propose FedQCal, which uses a reference model trained on a small labeled server set to define alignment requirements for aggregation. The key idea is to retain sufficient reference alignment while avoiding unnecessarily large aggregation coefficients. A minimum-norm quadratic program (QP) enforces these requirements. Classification loss on the same server examples selects a merge through two dual variables, so the alignment thresholds need not be prescribed in advance. We prove that the selected coefficients remain exact QP solutions at their induced thresholds. A perturbation bound explains how coefficient norm limits the amplification of projection errors. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and SVHN show gains over one-round FedAvg in most settings. Ablations support reference-guided weighting and coefficient calibration, even with one labeled server image per class.

</details>


### [60] [Real-Anchored Complementary Evidence for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27161)
*Wenpeng Mu,Tanfeng Sun,Zhenyu Shao,Qiang Xu,Xinghao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅用真实图像训练、以真实图像规律为共同参照的检测框架RACE，融合全局与局部互补证据，六个基准平均AUROC达96.59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型持续演化，基于生成样本训练的检测器容易依赖生成器特有的线索，跨生成器泛化能力不足；而真实照片共享源于物理成像与采集过程的规律性，可作为超越单一生成器的共同参照。

Method: RACE仅用真实训练图像：全局路径融合冻结与可学习表示以度量与真实参照的接近度，并引入受控扰动响应以补充表示敏感性；局部路径以中间特征与带符号扰动响应为上下文重建完整痕迹描述子，并将检测证据限制在静态末端残差重建误差上；最后用真实图像统计对齐全局与局部得分进行融合，并用独立真实集校准在测试生成器间固定不变的判决阈值。

Result: 六个基准上平均AUROC为96.59%，在五个域外基准上达97.18%，均优于对比方法；同时对图像扰动和真实图像来源变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 真实图像的规律性可作为跨多样生成器与成像条件的可迁移检测基础。

Abstract: Evolving generative models challenge detector generalization, as methods trained on generated examples may rely on generator-specific cues. By contrast, authentic photographs share regularities rooted in physical image formation and acquisition, providing a common reference beyond individual generators. Building on these regularities, we propose Real-Anchored Complementary Evidence (RACE), which learns complementary global and local detection evidence using only authentic training images. First, the global pathway combines frozen and learned representations to assess proximity to authentic references. Next, it incorporates responses to controlled perturbations, complementing static compatibility with representation sensitivity. The local pathway then reconstructs complete trace descriptors, using intermediate features and signed perturbation responses as context while restricting detection evidence to static terminal-residual reconstruction errors. Finally, authentic-image statistics align the global and local scores for fusion, and an independent authentic set calibrates a decision threshold held fixed across test generators. Across six benchmarks, RACE achieves the highest average AUROC of 96.59% among the compared methods, reaching 97.18% on five out-of-domain benchmarks. Together with robustness to image perturbations and variation in authentic-image sources, these results support authentic-image regularities as a transferable basis for detection across diverse generators and image conditions.

</details>


### [61] [Analyzing Multi-Layer Visual Representations of Large Vision-Language Models for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27128)
*Satoshi Hashimoto,Hitoshi Nishimura,Mori Kurokawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文分析大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）的多层视觉表征，比较逐层特征与多层融合在异常检测中的有效性，并给出面向视觉中心任务的表征选择经验性洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM通常通过projector将视觉编码器特征接入语言模型，深层特征具高层语义并与文本对齐，而浅层与中间层保留局部外观、细粒度形状等视觉中心信息。因此需依据下游任务性质选择层表征与融合方式，而现有工作缺乏针对异常检测的系统性分析。

Method: 以异常检测为下游任务，在多种监督级别与多种分类器下，系统比较逐层特征与多层融合方案，并从多个视角考察多层视觉表征对下游性能的影响。

Result: 通过分析不同层表征与融合方式带来的性能差异，量化各层表征对异常检测的贡献，揭示深层语义特征与浅中层细粒度特征在不同监督设置下的相对作用。

Conclusion: 多层视觉表征的层选择与融合策略应匹配任务特性；论文为视觉中心任务中的表征选择提供经验性指导与洞见。

Abstract: In this paper, we analyze multi-layer visual representations in large vision-language models (LVLMs) and examine the effectiveness of layer-wise features and multi-layer fusion for anomaly detection. Representative LVLMs connect visual encoder features to a language model through a projector. In this design, deep visual encoder features carry high-level semantics and alignment with text representations, whereas shallow and intermediate layers retain information relevant to vision-centric tasks, such as local appearance and fine-grained shape. It is therefore important to choose layer representations and fusion methods according to the nature of the downstream task. Focusing on anomaly detection, we compare layer-wise features and multi-layer fusion across multiple supervision levels and a variety of classifiers, and examine from several perspectives how multi-layer visual representations affect downstream performance. By analyzing the resulting performance differences, we further discuss the contribution of each layer representation to anomaly detection and provide empirical insights into representation selection for vision-centric tasks.

</details>


### [62] [UDA-Restoration: Bridging Unsupervised Domain Adaptation and Multi-Degradation Denoising for Enhanced AV Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27097)
*Zainab Bibi,Mohsin Kamal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出扩散引导的无监督域适应图像恢复方法 UDA-Restoration（Noise-DA 范式），结合多源退化学习与扩散引导适应，在去噪与增强上优于自监督/无监督基线并接近最优有监督性能，可提升下游语义分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶视觉感知在恶劣天气、传感器噪声、运动伪影和光照变化下图像质量下降，导致感知性能受损；而真实退化场景缺乏成对标注数据，需借助无监督域适应实现跨合成与真实退化的恢复。

Method: 遵循 Noise-Space Domain Adaptation（Noise-DA）范式：以多源退化学习构建退化分布，结合扩散引导的域适应，在噪声空间中跨越合成与真实退化进行图像增强与去噪。

Result: 在雪、雾、传感器畸变等未见退化场景下保持鲁棒；定量结果显示 Noise-DA 优于所对比的自监督与无监督基线，并与最佳有监督恢复性能相当；语义分割实验证明图像恢复质量提升直接改善下游感知任务。

Conclusion: 基于扩散的无监督域适应是自动驾驶图像恢复中可扩展且有效的方案，能同时提升恢复质量与下游感知性能；后续可进一步拓展退化覆盖范围与实际部署验证。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles rely heavily on vision-based perception systems for safe navigation and decision-making. Adverse weather conditions, sensor noise, moving artifacts and illumination changes, however, often degrade the image quality and hence reduce perception performance. This paper introduces a diffusion-guided unsupervised domain adaptation algorithm for autonomous driving environments, namely UDA-Restoration, which is designed to enhance and denoise images. The proposed method follows the paradigm of Noise-Space Domain Adaptation (Noise-DA), which is based on multi-source degradation learning and diffusion-guided adaptation that spans across synthetic and real-world degradations. The framework has been extensively tested and shown to restore image quality and to be robust to unseen degradation scenarios, such as snow, haze, sensor distortions, and more. The quantitative results indicate that Noise-DA outperforms the evaluated self-supervised and unsupervised baselines while matching the best supervised restoration performance. In addition, semantic segmentation experiments show that the performance of downstream perception tasks is directly improved by better image restoration. The outcomes show the promise of unsupervised domain adaptation based on diffusion as a powerful and scalable method for image restoration tasks in autonomous driving systems under various environments.

</details>


### [63] [Learning 3D MRI Representations with Generative Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27057)
*Sujeet Lahane,Sapna Mishra,Akash Suryavanshi,Tapan Gandhi,Bharat Biswal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 R2RG，一种基于 3D CNN 并用 GAN 增强重建任务的表示学习方法，在有限数据下实现了高效且可泛化的 3D 医学影像表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D MR 影像的表示学习面临特征稀疏、输入维度高、数据集小等困难；现有 Transformer 与扩散模型在数据量较小时表现不佳，常见做法是将 3D 体数据切片为 2D 求解，导致空间三维信息丢失。

Method: R2RG 利用 3D CNN 的架构先验（architectural bias），结合数据高效的自编码 GAN 以及复合损失函数，以提升重建保真度并稳定训练，从而以重建式方式学习表示。

Result: 在三个标准数据集及一个本地采集数据集上的实验表明，R2RG 表现优越，并可泛化至异构与更小规模的数据集；在帕金森病与阿尔茨海默病分类任务上，其学到的表示优于对比学习类方法。

Conclusion: 在数据可用性有限的条件下，重建式表示学习是 3D 医学影像的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Learning representations of visual images is a challenging task. It requires extracting abstract patterns from high-dimensional data without explicit supervision. This becomes pronounced with 3D MR imaging due to sparse features, higher dimensional inputs, and smaller datasets. Many state-of-the-art representation learning algorithms successfully rely on transformers and diffusion-based architectures. However, these methods struggle with smaller datasets. In effect, solution methods relax the problem by slicing 3D volumes and solving the problem for 2D medical imaging, resulting in a loss of spatial 3D information. We present R2RG, a representation learning method based on Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), with Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) honing the reconstruction task. Our key insights lie in exploiting architectural biases in 3D CNNs, combined with data-efficient auto-encoding GANs and a composite loss function to enhance reconstruction fidelity and stabilize training. Our experiments on three standard datasets and a locally collected dataset show that R2RG not only achieves superior performance but also generalizes to heterogeneous and smaller datasets. Furthermore, representations learned with R2RG outperform those learned with contrastive-based representation learning methods on Parkinson's and Alzheimer's disease classification. Our work demonstrates that reconstruction-based representation learning is a promising direction for 3D medical imaging under limited data availability.

</details>


### [64] [ViewDreamer: A Robot World Model beyond Fixed Camera Setups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26981)
*Ye Mao,Jinhui Xiong,Egor Larionov,Shiyang Lu,Krystian Mikolajczyk,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViewDreamer 是一种支持可变相机集合的单一世界模型，通过解耦单视角生成与跨视角交互（仅经由共享 slot token 交互）实现多视角一致的视频生成，并在机器人世界-动作模型中带来性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型多逐视角独立生成或将所有视角按固定布局拼接，而机器人示教数据由同步相机采集，其数量、位置与顺序随本体变化而不同，固定布局方案难以适配。

Method: 每个视角沿用预训练单视角模型的生成路径，视角间仅通过共享 slot token 交互；采用逐步增加视角数的课程学习稳定多视角训练；提出 Cross-View Slot Agreement，利用视觉基础模型将跨视角几何与语义相似区域分组为共享 slot。

Result: 在覆盖 1–4 相机的 RoboView-Bench 上取得所评估模型中最优生成质量，且跨视角一致性优于独立逐视角生成；增益可迁移至 WAM 与 AC-WM，其中 WAM 在 LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0、RoboDojo 及真实 Franka 机器人上优于 FastWAM-Joint 基线，两种变体均提升了对多种相机设置与场景的域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过共享 slot token 的可变视角建模与课程训练，可在不依赖固定相机布局的前提下统一处理多变相机配置，并有效增强下游动作建模与域外泛化。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly important for robot learning, yet most generate each camera view separately or tile all views in a fixed layout. Robot demonstrations, however, are captured by synchronized cameras whose number, placement, and order vary across embodiments. We present ViewDreamer, a single world model that handles variable sets of cameras by disentangling per-view generation from cross-view interaction. Each view follows the generation path of a pretrained single-view model, and views interact only through shared slot tokens. We stabilize multi-view training with a curriculum that gradually increases the number of views. Cross-View Slot Agreement uses visual foundation models to group geometrically and semantically similar regions across views into shared slots. On RoboView-Bench, which covers one to four cameras, ViewDreamer achieves the best generation quality among the models evaluated and improves cross-view consistency over independent per-view generation. These gains carry over to our world-action model (WAM) and action-conditioned world model (AC-WM). Our WAM outperforms the FastWAM-Joint baseline on LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin 2.0, and RoboDojo, and on a real Franka robot. Both variants improve out-of-domain generalization to various camera settings and scenes.

</details>


### [65] [Which Dwarf Is This? Doppeldwarf-306 and Near-Identical Instance Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27490)
*Turhan Can Kargin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Doppeldwarf-306数据集：306座波兰弗罗茨瓦夫青铜小矮人雕像的1,691张开放许可照片，用于细粒度实例检索。冻结的DINOv2特征无需训练即可达93.1% top-1，最佳13个骨干达94.7%；并分析地理位置先验与局部特征匹配的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例检索基准多聚焦地标建筑，而视觉上高度相似的同系列小型雕塑构成互补性挑战；同时缺乏对"照片检索手机导览"这类实际场景中地理位置先验价值的严谨评估。

Method: 构建Doppeldwarf-306数据集（306座雕像、1,691张图，记录摄影师以支持留一摄影师协议）。采用无需训练的最近邻检索，评估13个来自7种预训练范式的骨干；模拟"仅检索附近雕像"的过滤策略与"位置作为加权"策略，并用置换检验验证布局效应；在扣除查询摄影师条件下比较学习式局部特征匹配；采用双候选比较降低未知雕像误接受率。

Result: DINOv2留一查询top-1为93.1%，最佳骨干94.7%；候选数每翻倍约降0.8点。按位置过滤在模拟中虽有增益，但不及等量随机子集，说明随机子采样高估其价值（置换检验p=0.005）；将位置作为权重（25 m处模拟用户）使DINOv2从93.4%升至98.7%。扣除摄影师后，局部特征匹配将DINOv2由81.8%提升至85.5%，亦改善DINOv3。双候选比较在90%已知接受率下将未知雕像误接受率从38.3%降至19.6%。

Conclusion: 相似系列小雕塑构成有意义的细粒度实例检索基准；冻结视觉特征已具强基线，且随候选库增长退化平缓。地理位置宜作软权重而非硬过滤，局部特征匹配可补偿摄影师线索缺失，双候选比较可有效抑制开集误接受。

Abstract: Most established instance-retrieval benchmarks emphasise landmarks; small sculptures from visually similar series offer a complementary challenge. We introduce Doppeldwarf-306: 1,691 openly licensed photographs of 306 bronze dwarf statues in Wrocław, Poland, many made in themed series that share one style, with the photographer of each image recorded. With no training, nearest-neighbour search over frozen DINOv2 features names the right statue first for 93.1% of leave-one-out queries, and the best of thirteen backbones from seven pretraining paradigms reaches 94.7%. Accuracy falls slowly as candidates are added: for DINOv2, by about 0.8 points each time their number doubles. A natural design for a phone guide compares a photograph only with statues near the user. In simulation this helps, but less than an equal number of randomly chosen statues does, so random subsampling overstates its value; a permutation test ties the penalty to the real layout (p = 0.005). Used as a weight instead of a filter, location raises DINOv2 from 93.4% to 98.7% for a simulated user 25 m from the statue. With the query's photographer withheld, learned local-feature matching raises DINOv2 accuracy from 81.8% to 85.5% and also improves DINOv3. Comparing the two best candidates cuts DINOv2's false acceptance of unknown statues from 38.3% to 19.6% at 90% known acceptance.

</details>


### [66] [PathOPEN: A pathologist-curated histopathology dataset for visual question answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27469)
*Awais Naeem,Zhongling Xu,George Huang,Amy Coffey,Nkechi Okonkwo,Mario Saab-Chalhoub,John Lee Sang,Dina Kokh,Muhammad Zulfiqar,Ankita Shukla,Hairong Wang,Chandra Krishnan,Ying Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PathOPEN 是一个面向组织病理学视觉问答（VQA）的数据集，包含 157 例病例、229 张原始图像、三种题型（开放式、五选一、是/否）、6,870 张增强图像及四份用于平衡子集的清单，支持子组评估与偏好样本构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织病理学领域缺乏兼具多题型、丰富元数据与偏好标注的 VQA 基准，难以支撑子组评估和偏好对齐研究，作者据此构建 PathOPEN。

Method: 由两名临床病理学家选取 157 例、229 张原始图像，撰写开放式、五选一与是/否题目，并为 309 道开放式题中的 201 道同时提供正误答案；每例标注器官、病理类别与区域解剖，每图标注放大倍数；生成 6,870 张增强图像与四份按各元数据字段平衡子集的清单；由五名病理学家与两名视觉语言模型评审按评分细则打分，并以六个模型在三种题型下进行零样本作答。

Result: 数据集完成元数据标注、图像增强与四个平衡子集清单构建，并经多评审打分与六模型零样本评测，具备子组分析条件，正误答案对可直接生成偏好样本。

Conclusion: PathOPEN 提供了带细粒度元数据、多题型及配对答案的组织病理 VQA 资源，可用于子组评估与偏好学习，但其规模较小（157 例），普适性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: PathOPEN is a histopathology dataset for visual question answering. Two clinical pathologists selected 157 cases with 229 available original images and wrote open-ended, five-option multiple-choice and close-ended (yes/no) questions. They supplied both a correct and a wrong answer for 201 of the 309 open-ended questions. Each case records the organ, pathology category and regional anatomy, and each image records its magnification. The dataset includes 6,870 augmented images and four manifests that select subsets to improve balance by individual metadata field. Five additional pathologists and two vision-language model judges rated items with scoring rubrics, and six models answered questions zero-shot across the three formats. The metadata support subgroup evaluation, and the paired answers can be used to construct preference examples.

</details>


### [67] [Pose-based Co-speech Gesture Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27415)
*Frances Yung,Ninlawat Phuangschoke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文对现有伴随语音手势检测方法在三个数据集（MULTISIMO、GESRes、CABBS）上采用统一交叉验证划分与帧/手势跨段/窗口三级评价进行基准测试，揭示原有性能难以复现且受手势频率显著影响，并提出CTR-GCN+BiLSTM模型在CABBS上超越此前最佳的MPSL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随语音手势检测是手势标注与人类交际行为研究的基础步骤，但该任务研究不足；现有方法所用数据集、实验设置与评价指标各不相同，性能难以横向比较，领域缺乏明确的最优基线。

Method: 构建统一评测框架：在MULTISIMO、GESRes、CABBS三个数据集上使用相同的交叉验证折，并在帧级、手势跨段级、窗口级三个粒度进行评价，对现有检测器进行基准对比；同时提出CTR-GCN + BiLSTM的检测模型。

Result: 先前报告的性能在统一评测设置下不能稳定复现；性能随数据集差异显著，并与手势频率密切相关，表明任务远未解决。所提CTR-GCN + BiLSTM模型在CABBS上优于此前最佳方法MPSL。

Conclusion: 该工作为伴随语音手势检测提供了更一致的比较基础，明确了现有方法的可复现性问题与关键挑战，并为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Co-speech gesture detection identifies the spans in which a speaker gestures (e.g., beat or semantic gestures) in video. As a first step in gesture annotation, it is crucial for studying human communicative behaviour. Despite the availability of automatic tools and models, co-speech gesture detection remains underexplored, and its current state of the art is unclear: existing methods have been evaluated using different datasets, experimental setups, and metrics, making their performance difficult to compare. To address this gap, we benchmark existing detectors using the same cross-validation folds across three datasets (MULTISIMO, GESRes, and CABBS), with evaluation at the frame, gesture-span, and window levels. Our results show that previously reported performance does not consistently transfer to this common evaluation setting. Performance varies substantially across datasets, particularly with gesture frequency, indicating that the task remains far from solved. We further propose a CTR-GCN + BiLSTM model that outperforms the previous best method, MPSL, on CABBS. Our results provide a more consistent basis for comparing existing approaches and highlight key challenges for future research on co-speech gesture detection.

</details>


### [68] [ReSA: Preserving Reference-Specified Foreground in In-Context Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27341)
*Dezhao Su,Bingxu Hou,Jingdi Lei,Lin Qian,Likai Wang,Yunyun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReSA，一个以共享参考核统一查询适配与掩码选择的参考引导框架，用于上下文分割，在十个基准上取得最高平均 mIoU（1-shot 62.6%，5-shot 71.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文分割需要将参考指定的前景准确迁移到查询图像，关键在于既恢复目标区域又抑制无关预测，而现有方法在预测细化与掩码生成之间缺乏一致的参考信息利用。

Method: 以共享参考核贯穿查询适配与掩码选择：利用前景/背景参考原型约束适配过程中的预测变化，并通过参考加权重建引导前景选择；在多个适配预测间进行选择，并使边界细化与所选前景阈值对齐；同时给出连接适配期参考响应变化与软重建的理论界。视觉编码器冻结，仅做轻量测试时适配，无需离线任务训练。

Result: 消融实验显示前景保留改善、假阳性区域被有效抑制；定性结果掩码更完整、孤立伪影更少；在十个物体、部件与跨域分割基准上平均 mIoU 最高，1-shot 62.6%、5-shot 71.8%。

Conclusion: 共享参考引导可在细化与掩码形成阶段一致地利用参考信息，无需离线训练即可实现更强的上下文分割性能。

Abstract: In-context segmentation uses annotated references to specify the target and its extent in a query image. Accurately transferring this reference-specified foreground requires recovering target regions while suppressing irrelevant predictions. We propose ReSA, a reference-guided framework that unifies query adaptation and mask selection through a shared reference kernel. ReSA leverages foreground and background reference prototypes to constrain prediction changes during adaptation and guide foreground selection through reference-weighted reconstruction. This shared guidance promotes consistent use of reference information across prediction refinement and mask formation. ReSA further selects among adapted predictions and aligns boundary refinement with the selected foreground threshold. We establish a theoretical bound connecting reference-response changes during adaptation and soft reconstruction. With a frozen visual encoder and lightweight test-time adaptation, ReSA requires no offline task training. Ablation studies demonstrate improved foreground retention and effective suppression of false-positive regions, while qualitative results show more complete masks with fewer isolated artifacts. Across ten object, part, and cross-domain segmentation benchmarks, ReSA achieves the highest average mIoU among the evaluated methods, reaching 62.6% in 1-shot and 71.8% in 5-shot.

</details>


### [69] [BioCL-POS: Biological Completer-aware Learning with Predictive Observation Selection - A Wild Ginseng Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27316)
*Yulu Wang,Tingting Yao,Taolue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BioCL-POS 外部选择器，在冻结的点云补全模型前选择观测子集，从而在不重训练的情况下提升缺失区域几何恢复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 根茎形态学分析依赖完整三维结构，但断裂会遮挡关键结构特征；因此需要点云补全提供几何参考。作者质疑"输入观测点越多补全越好"的直觉，探索通过选择观测子集来提升原始缺失区域的恢复质量。

Method: 采用外部选择器 BioCL-POS：对每个候选输入，共享空间位置到其补全结果最近点的位移与距离被用作空间响应，以估计恢复质量，并用真实补全结果的测量进行监督；推理时仅从观测几何中选出一个输入，补全器只运行一次，且补全器保持冻结。

Result: 在人参和树木数据的 5%、10%、20% 缺失率上，使用四个冻结补全器均优于全输入补全以及五种替代选择器，获得更低的平均重建误差、更高的平均 F-score 与覆盖率；30% 缺失率的人参实验亦做了考察。

Conclusion: 表明通过选择观测——而非尽可能多地输入观测点——可在不重训练补全器的情况下改善几何恢复，为后续形态学分析提供更准确的几何参考。

Abstract: Growers and traders rely on root morphology to assess wild ginseng, yet breakage can obscure important structural traits. We study point cloud completion to provide geometric references for morphological analysis. Does providing more observed points always improve 3D completion? We test whether selected subsets improve recovery of the original missing region while keeping the pretrained completer fixed. We introduce BioCL-POS, an external selector that predicts how the completer responds to candidate inputs. For each candidate, we predict the displacement and distance from shared spatial locations to the nearest points in its completion. We use these spatial responses to estimate recovery quality and supervise them with measurements from actual completions. At inference, we select an input from the observed geometry and run the completer once. We evaluate four frozen completers on controlled gaps in ginseng and tree data at 5%,10%, and 20% omission, and examine 30% omission on ginseng. At 5--20% omission, we outperform full-input completion and all five alternative selectors on both datasets with all four completers. We achieve lower mean reconstruction error and higher mean F-score and coverage. We thus show that selecting observations can improve geometric recovery without retraining the completer, providing more accurate geometric references for subsequent morphological analysis.

</details>


### [70] [A Vision-Based Framework for Manual Assembly Verification and Adaptive Operator Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27293)
*Naveen Joseph Delphin,Leonardo Venturoso,Isabella Soraruf,Marco Todescato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于单目俯视RGB相机的模块化装配过程监测框架，集成目标检测、手部运动跟踪、区域感知交互检测、有限状态校验、任务姿态分类与可配置视觉引导，并在气动活塞装配/拆卸任务中开展8人三条件实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工装配过程难以监测，因为需要同时理解操作者动作、零部件状态与任务进度，现有手段缺乏在单相机条件下统一感知、流程校验与反馈的能力。

Method: 构建模块化框架：单目俯视RGB相机采集；目标检测、手部运动跟踪、区域感知交互检测、有限状态机流程校验、任务特定姿态分类、可配置视觉引导。实验为气动活塞装配与拆卸，8名被试，三种辅助条件（完整、中等、无引导），采用混合效应模型分析运动质量复合指标、完成时间、失败阶段数及学习轨迹。

Result: 混合效应分析显示辅助条件与运动质量复合指标相关，中等与无引导条件下的估计得分低于完整引导；但条件×试次交互不显著，学习轨迹差异仅为描述性。完成时间与失败阶段数未发现条件间差异。

Conclusion: 研究验证了在单相机工作流中集成视觉感知、流程校验与可配置操作反馈的可行性；依据操作者熟练度自适应选择辅助强度仍为后续设计目标。

Abstract: Manual assembly remains difficult to monitor because operator actions, components, and task progress must be interpreted together. This paper presents a modular framework based on a single overhead red-green-blue (RGB) camera, combining object detection, hand-motion tracking, region-aware interaction detection, finite-state verification, task-specific pose classification, and configurable visual guidance. The system was evaluated during pneumatic-piston assembly and disassembly with eight participants under three assistance conditions: full, mid, and no guidance. Mixed-effects analysis identified an association between assistance condition and a composite measure of motion quality, with lower estimated scores under mid and no guidance relative to full guidance. However, the condition-by-trial interaction was not statistically significant, and the estimated differences in learning trajectories remain descriptive. No condition differences were detected in completion time or failed-stage counts. The study demonstrates the integration of visual perception, procedural verification, and configurable operator feedback in a single-camera workflow. Selecting assistance according to operator expertise remains a design objective for future investigation.

</details>


### [71] [InterBand-EMG: A Cross-Band Foundation Model for Generalizable Hand-Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27289)
*Zongheng Guo,Tao Chen,Dalong Du,Yijian Yang,Heng Liu,Lun Shu,Chen Luo,Junhao Tu,Lei Shu,Mingzhou Wu,Yifan Liu,Yu Zhang,Zhenqiang Liu,Shilei Wen,Jie Zhou,Manuela Ferrario*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InterBand-EMG自监督表征学习方法，结合频带量化、跨频带重建与结构化掩码码本预测，实现跨用户、跨动作泛化的sEMG手势姿态估计，并发布八通道SynapMyo数据集（499用户、1227.74手小时、68类动作）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面肌电（sEMG）可支撑连续手势姿态估计，但现有表征难以跨用户、跨动作泛化；同时缺少大规模、多用户、多动作类别的公开数据资源。

Method: 提出InterBand-EMG：采用频带特定量化（band-specific quantization），并联合跨频带重建（cross-band reconstruction）与结构化掩码码本预测（structured masked code prediction）进行自监督预训练，学习可迁移的EMG表征；同时构建八通道EMG-姿态数据集SynapMyo。

Result: 在emg2pose与SynapMyo两个数据集、全部三种泛化设定下，关节角度精度均优于现有SOTA基线。SynapMyo含1,227.74手小时数据，覆盖499名用户、68类动作。

Conclusion: InterBand-EMG验证了多频带自监督学习对sEMG跨用户/跨动作泛化的有效性；源码与SynapMyo数据集将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) enables continuous hand-pose estimation, but learning representations that generalize across users and movements remains challenging. We introduce InterBand-EMG, which combines band-specific quantization with cross-band reconstruction and structured masked code prediction to learn transferable EMG representations for hand-pose tracking. We also introduce SynapMyo, an eight-channel EMG-to-pose dataset comprising 1,227.74 hand-hours from 499 users across 68 movement categories. Experiments on emg2pose and SynapMyo show that InterBand-EMG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in joint-angle accuracy across all three generalization settings on both datasets. The source code and the SynapMyo dataset will be publicly released upon acceptance of this paper.

</details>


### [72] [SecretAgent: A Multi-Agent Framework for Privacy Protection against Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26936)
*Minda Zhao,Yue Zhang,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SecretAgent：首个面向视觉语言模型（VLM）的通用多智能体图像隐私保护框架，在对抗扰动中兼顾隐私防护与视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 可从共享图像中推断位置、PII、年龄性别乃至医学图像中的隐私信息；现有对抗扰动方法虽能防御黑盒 VLM，但忽视图像质量，会引入可见伪影。
