Method: 提出 SecretAgent 多智能体框架：LLM 智能体迭代定位泄露隐私的视觉证据，并自适应生成不可见扰动，同时受"外观审查智能体"约束，拒绝任一存在可见伪影的候选。实例化为两变体：SecretAgent-Gradient 通过六个代理编码器优化扰动以提升跨模型迁移性；SecretAgent-Tool 为无梯度方法，组合程序化噪声。

Result: 在四个隐私应用（地理定位、PII、人脸、医学）、五个数据集、六个 VLM 上评测：SecretAgent 图像质量保持显著优于现有方法，各应用均达 SOTA 隐私保护，且保护可迁移至其他 VLM；SecretAgent-Tool 在其中三个数据集上取得最佳感知保真度。

Conclusion: 通过引入外观审查约束与多智能体迭代机制，SecretAgent 首次实现隐私保护与视觉保真度的双重优化，并具备良好的跨模型迁移泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can infer private information from shared images, including locations, personally identifiable information (PII), and personal attributes such as age and sex, even from medical images. Adversarial perturbations can protect such information against black-box VLMs, but existing methods overlook image quality and introduce visible artifacts into the images they protect. We present SecretAgent, the first general multi-agent framework that protects image privacy against VLMs while preserving visual fidelity. LLM agents iteratively locate the visual evidence that reveals private information and adapt an invisible perturbation to the target VLM's answers and to an appearance reviewer agent, which rejects any candidate with visible artifacts. We instantiate SecretAgent with two complementary variants: SecretAgent-Gradient optimizes the perturbation through six surrogate encoders for cross-model transferability, and the gradient-free SecretAgent-Tool composes procedural noise and achieves the best perceptual fidelity on three of the five datasets. Experiments on four privacy applications (geolocation, PII, facial, and medical) across five datasets and six VLMs show that SecretAgent preserves image quality substantially better than existing methods while achieving state-of-the-art privacy protection in every application, and that its protection transfers to other VLMs.

</details>


### [73] [TouchBench: Can Vision-Language Models Feel Invisible Physical Properties?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26883)
*Ziyao Zeng,Ziyi Kou,Chuan Qin,Mia Huang,Roger Boldu,Zhiwen Fan,Daniel Rakita,Eric Whitmire,Li Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出第一人称视-语-触基准TouchBench区分视觉可答题(Tier A)与视觉隐藏属性题(Tier B)，并给出无需触觉编码器的"触觉即提示"(TaP)方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM物理理解评测多依赖图像即可作答的问题，而操作任务中关键属性（如不透明容器液位、物体软硬度）须靠触觉才能获知，缺少相应的评估基准与方法。

Method: 构建TouchBench：头戴视频与同步137通道全手触觉传感配对，5名受试者、11项任务、900段片段，900个绝对题与4102个相对题；提出Tactile-as-Prompt(TaP)，将34个可解释触觉统计量以文本形式输入VLM，并用留一用户微调。

Result: 四个SOTA VLM在Tier A相对题平均66.4%，Tier B仅49.8%（近随机50%）。TaP使Tier B零样本相对题达62.9%，留一用户微调后73.1%，优于纯视频(67.4%)与学习式触觉token编码器(67.3%)，且泛化到未见物体与用户；在20小时真实语料上接触类问题准确率由67.1%升至75.5%。

Conclusion: 纯视觉物理推理存在明显瓶颈，语言可作为触觉感知的高效接口，且无需专门触觉编码器即可显著提升隐藏属性推理能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used for embodied perception and reasoning, yet their physical understanding is largely evaluated on questions answerable from images. Many properties critical to manipulation, such as an opaque bottle's fill level or an object's compliance, may only be revealed through touch. We introduce TouchBench, an egocentric vision-language-tactile benchmark that distinguishes visually answerable questions (Tier A) from those involving properties hidden from the camera (Tier B). It pairs head-mounted video with synchronized 137-channel whole-hand tactile sensing, comprising 900 clips from five participants across 11 tasks, with 900 absolute and 4,102 relative questions. Four state-of-the-art VLMs average 66.4% accuracy on Tier A relative questions but only 49.8% on Tier B, near chance (50%). To bridge this gap, we propose Tactile-as-Prompt (TaP), which presents 34 interpretable tactile statistics as text, enabling VLMs to reason with touch without a learned tactile encoder. TaP raises zero-shot Tier B relative accuracy to 62.9% and reaches 73.1% after leave-one-user-out fine-tuning, outperforming video alone (67.4%) and a learned tactile-token encoder (67.3%). The gains extend to unseen objects and users. On a separate 20-hour in-the-wild corpus, tactile text improves fine-tuned contact-question accuracy from 67.1% to 75.5%. These results highlight the limits of vision-only physical reasoning and establish language as an effective interface for tactile perception.

</details>


### [74] [Tracktail: Multi-View 3D Point Tracking without Depth Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26779)
*Katie L Rupp,Jonathan Wong,Sharmishtaa Seshamani,Ana Mafalda Vicente,Lili Karashchuk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tracktail：无深度输入的多视角3D查询点跟踪架构，通过每相机视频编码+每查询解码器实现高效推理，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点跟踪方法依赖估计深度，且在不同数据集尺度下泛化困难、推理效率低。作者希望无需深度输入、在多变尺度下高效且鲁棒地跟踪多视角3D查询点。

Method: 将任务分解为每相机的视频特征编码与每查询解码器；跨相机注意力仅在解码器中跨查询进行，提升效率（10视角100查询点下7 FPS、<4 GB内存）；引入由相机几何计算的每相机场景尺度，对不同数据集尺度归一化输入与预测。训练数据为合成Multi-View Kubric及新提出的Multi-View Point Odyssey基准（499帧人体与动物合成渲染）。

Result: 在Panoptic Studio、DexYCB、Multi-View Kubric基准上取得SOTA，优于依赖估计深度的方法；相比现有方法在更长时序范围上保持准确跟踪。

Conclusion: Tracktail证明无需深度输入的架构设计可实现高效、跨尺度鲁棒且长程稳定的多视角3D点跟踪，代码、数据与权重已开源。

Abstract: We introduce Tracktail, a novel architecture designed to efficiently track query points in 3D from multiple calibrated views without any depth input. Our architecture separates the task as a per-camera video encoding of features and a per-query decoder. Notably, camera cross-attention is only performed across queries in the decoder, allowing for efficient inference (7 FPS and less than 4 GB memory for 10 views and 100 query points). We further introduce a per-camera scene scale computed from camera geometry, which allows us to normalize inputs and predictions appropriately across a vast variety of dataset scales. We train on a synthetic Multi-View Kubric dataset as well as a novel Multi-View Point Odyssey benchmark dataset, which consists of synthetic renders of humans and animals across 499 frames. Tracktail achieves state-of-the-art results on the Panoptic Studio, DexYCB, and Multi-View Kubric benchmarks, outperforming methods that rely on estimated depth. Tracktail also demonstrates a strong ability to maintain accurate tracking over longer ranges compared to existing methods. Our code, data, and weights are publicly available: https://ai-hhmi.github.io/tracktail/

</details>


### [75] [Decomposing images in a PRISM: A Single-Source Multi-View Framework for Image Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26726)
*Eduardo Campos,Willam Cardoso,Diego Furtado,Solange Oliveira,Alneu Lopes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRISM框架，通过显式结构图将单一图像源转化为具有不同归纳偏好的多视图表示，并提供衡量各视图贡献的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图学习证明互补视图可提升预测性能，但许多数据集仅提供单一原始信息源，因此需要从不具多视图的数据中构造多个有用视图。

Method: 将图像通过显式结构图变换为具有不同归纳偏置的表示；给出PRISM-HD手工设计实例，用表示特定的描述符汇总各构造视图；并在三个艺术类和四个非艺术类图像数据集上进行评估，辅以固定特征预算分析与无参数融合规则。

Result: 固定特征预算下，结构视图的增益不能仅由特征维度解释；PRISM-HD在全部七个数据集上优于WND-CHARM；与Fisher Vector等权组合在艺术类数据集上比更优的单一表示提升5–8个百分点；经固定无参数融合规则与微调深度网络结合后，在四个数据集上提升性能，其余三个统计上不变。

Conclusion: 由单源图像构造的结构视图能提供与学习到的深度表示互补的预测信号，是一种有效的多视图构建途径。

Abstract: Multi-view learning has shown that combining complementary views can improve predictive performance, yet many datasets provide a single raw source of information. How can multiple useful views be constructed from one source? We propose PRISM (Plural Representations of Images via Structural Maps), a framework that transforms an image into representations with distinct inductive biases through explicit structural maps, together with a protocol for measuring what each view contributes. We evaluate PRISM-HD, a hand-designed instantiation of PRISM in which the constructed views are summarized through representation-specific descriptors, on three artistic and four non-artistic image datasets. Analyses at a fixed feature budget show that the gains of the structural views are not explained by feature dimensionality alone. PRISM-HD by itself outperforms WND-CHARM on all seven datasets. Moreover, PRISM-HD and Fisher Vector provide complementary predictive signals: their equal-weight combination improves over the better individual representation by 5--8 percentage points on the artistic datasets. When combined with fine-tuned deep networks through a fixed parameter-free fusion rule, the PRISM-based representation improves performance on four of seven datasets and leaves it statistically unchanged on the remaining three, indicating that the constructed views provide predictive signals that are complementary to learned deep representations.

</details>


### [76] [When Visually Rich Documents Meet Multi-Vector Retrieval for Region-level Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27542)
*Xu Yuan,Liangbo Ning,Yujuan Ding,Qing Li,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MV-VisRAG：以语义完整区域为单位做文档表示与视觉增强的区域级 Vision-RAG 框架，利用多向量表示+late-interaction 检索并筛选区域构建紧凑视觉上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉丰富文档（VRD）的文本、视觉、版面信息在 Text-RAG 中难以充分利用；现有 Vision-RAG 直接基于文档图像检索与生成，但采用粗粒度检索表示与整页视觉上下文，易掩盖局部证据，并带来显著冗余与计算开销。

Method: 1) 内容自适应区域化模块（Content-Adaptive Regionalization）构建语义完整区域；2) 基于 VLM 的检索器经多模态重建预训练与检索微调，将区域编码为局部区域向量并生成页级全局向量；3) 以紧凑多向量表示进行 late-interaction 检索，由匹配分数估计区域贡献度，据此选取相关区域并重排为紧凑视觉上下文用于生成。

Result: 在五个基准上，检索与生成性能相对对应基线均有提升；区域级增强显著减少视觉 token 数量与计算成本。

Conclusion: 区域级（而非整页）的表示与视觉增强是提升 Vision-RAG 效果与效率的有效途径；代码将开源。

Abstract: Visually rich documents (VRDs) convey knowledge through textual, visual, and layout information that text-centric Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Text-RAG) struggles to fully exploit. Recent Vision-RAG approaches enable retrieval and generation directly from document images, retaining this multimodal information. However, earlier Vision-RAG methods commonly adopt coarse retrieval representations and full-page visual contexts, which can obscure local evidence and introduce substantial redundancy and computational overhead. To address these challenges, we propose MV-VisRAG, a region-level Vision-RAG framework that uses semantically complete regions for both document representation and visual augmentation. Specifically, MV-VisRAG first constructs these regions through a ContentAdaptive Regionalization module. It then encodes them into local region vectors and a page-level global vector using a VLM-based retriever trained through multimodal reconstruction pretraining and retrieval fine-tuning. With this compact multi-vector representation, MV-VisRAG performs late-interaction retrieval and estimates region contributions from the resulting matching scores. These contributions guide the selection of relevant regions, which are then rearranged into a compact visual context for generation. Experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate retrieval and generation gains over corresponding baselines, while region-level augmentation substantially reduces visual tokens and computational cost. Our code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/xandery-geek/MV-VisRAG.

</details>


### [77] [PGS-PCQA: Perceptually Gated Synthetic Supervision for No-Reference Point Cloud Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27512)
*Qinyuan Fan,Meng Zhang,Clemens Gühmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PGS-PCQA：用合成失真库的组内严重度排序作额外监督，并以感知门软化小感知差异对的排序目标，显著提升跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NR-PCQA方法在单一数据集内表现良好但跨数据集泛化差，作者将其归因于训练数据失真覆盖不足：面对未见失真类型时，模型的严重度排序接近随机甚至反转。

Method: 从源参考点云构建合成失真库，对合成样本无需主观分数，仅用同类型内的严重度排序作为监督，与源数据集MOS联合训练；因更高严重度不必然意味着更低感知质量，引入感知门利用渲染视图的感知差异软化小差异对的排序目标；合成监督仅用于训练，无需目标域数据。

Result: 在SJTU-PCQA单次训练：WPC上SRCC 0.709、BASICS上0.751（DSP-PCQA为0.595、0.723）；参考不交叉验证下SRCC达SJTU-PCQA 0.955、WPC 0.934、BASICS 0.906、LS-PCQA Part I 0.791；缩减合成库的排除实验显示其贡献为全库相对无库SRCC增益的58–65%。

Conclusion: 合成失真排序监督配合感知门是提升NR-PCQA跨数据集泛化的有效手段，且不依赖目标域数据与额外主观标注。

Abstract: Existing no-reference point cloud quality assessment (NR-PCQA) methods perform well within individual datasets but generalize poorly across datasets. We identify insufficient distortion coverage in training data as a key cause: on unseen distortion types, models rank severity levels near chance or even in reverse. We propose perceptually gated synthetic supervision for no-reference point cloud quality assessment (PGS-PCQA), an NR-PCQA model trained with synthetic distortion ranking. It builds a synthetic distortion bank from source reference point clouds and uses within-type severity ranking as supervision in addition to the source mean opinion scores (MOS), requiring no subjective scores for the synthetic samples. Since higher severity does not always mean lower perceived quality, a perceptual gate softens the ranking targets of pairs with small perceptual differences in rendered views. The synthetic supervision is used only during training and needs no target-domain data. In a single training run on SJTU-PCQA, PGS-PCQA achieves Spearman rank correlation coefficients (SRCCs) of 0.709 on WPC and 0.751 on BASICS. The corresponding published DSP-PCQA scores are 0.595 and 0.723. Under reference-disjoint cross-validation, PGS-PCQA achieves SRCCs of 0.955 on SJTU-PCQA, 0.934 on WPC, 0.906 on BASICS, and 0.791 on LS-PCQA Part I. In the exclusion experiment, the reported scores for the reduced synthetic bank represent 58-65% of the full-bank SRCC gain relative to training without the bank. Code is available at https://github.com/Fan-QY/PGS-PCQA.

</details>


### [78] [Self-Supervised Continuous-Time Depth Learning and Ego-Motion Estimation from Asynchronous Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27509)
*Sadiq O. Macaulay,Dipon Kumar Ghosh,Hadfield Simon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督方法，为每个事件在连续时间上赋予独立深度，同时从同一观测中恢复相机运动；监督信号来自几何一致性（运动补偿的事件对齐），再经可微渲染生成目标时刻的稠密深度图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机以微秒级精度逐像素记录亮度变化，但可用来监督的稠密深度获取代价高且仅在孤立时刻存在；将事件流压缩为帧便于学习，却牺牲了事件感知赖以立足的时间精度。因此需要一种在连续时间上直接监督事件深度的自监督方案。

Method: 将每个事件按其预测深度反投影到3D，并用该事件触发时刻的相机运动进行位移，使同一边缘产生的事件应在空间中重合，任何错位即暴露深度或运动误差，以此作为对齐目标；结合局部时空上下文与时间骨干网络，最终通过可微渲染把逐事件估计转化为每个窗口内目标时刻的稠密深度图。

Result: 在MVSEC上，多数序列与深度范围内优于既有仅用事件的自监督方法，夜间场景提升幅度最大；相机旋转估计精度优于此前的无监督事件方法。消融实验分别验证了局部时空上下文、时间骨干网络与对齐目标的作用。

Conclusion: 运动补偿的事件对齐可作为连续时间深度与自运动估计的一种有原理依据的监督来源。

Abstract: Event cameras register brightness changes pixel by pixel with microsecond precision, whereas the dense depth that could supervise them is costly to acquire and exists only at isolated instants. Condensing the stream into frames eases learning, but at the expense of the very timing that sets event sensing apart. We propose a self-supervised approach in which each event is assigned its own depth in continuous time, and the camera motion is recovered from the same observations. Supervision arises from geometry: lifted into 3D by its predicted depth and displaced by the camera motion at the instant it fired, an event should coincide with others produced by the same edge, so any misalignment exposes an error in depth or motion. Differentiable rendering then turns these per-event estimates into dense depth maps at target times within each window, faithful to the events behind them. On MVSEC, the approach outperforms prior event-only self-supervised methods in most sequences and depth ranges, with the widest margins at night, and recovers camera rotation more accurately than an earlier unsupervised event-based method. Ablations isolate the roles of local spatiotemporal context, the temporal backbone, and the alignment objective. These results position motion-compensated event alignment as a principled source of supervision for continuous-time depth and ego-motion estimation.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond Prompt Imitation: Capability-Aware Video Generation with Smaller Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27252)
*Ziqi Ding,Yuxin Cao,Kai Ye,Yaobo Yang,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"oracle control ladder"评测框架，用目标片段的渐进信息（稠密描述→首帧→深度视频→两者组合）作为经验上限参考，量化各推理时方法对保真度差距的闭合比例，并以项目反应模型预测最优控制层级、指导预算受限的选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时方法仅用偏好分数比较小开源视频生成器与专有模型，无法说明方法究竟弥合了多少与目标视频的差距，缺乏可量化、可归因的"剩余空间"参照。

Method: 构建oracle控制阶梯：向固定学生模型逐级注入来自目标片段的更丰富信息，在每一级读取各保真度指标的闭合率；在954个片段上评测外观与运动两类差距，跨四位教师模型及第二个学生模型验证；进一步用项目反应模型建模"哪一级控制能闭合哪个片段"，并作为预算受限选择策略的先验。

Result: 稠密描述几乎不能复现外观、基本不能复现运动；首帧闭合约一半外观差距与十分之一运动差距；深度视频闭合约四分之三运动差距但不影响颜色；两者合用可在四位教师下闭合各保真度差距的64%–94%；第二学生模型在可接受的层级上结论一致；项目反应模型对保真度准则的留出AUC为0.76–0.95。

Conclusion: 控制免费时固定稠密控制层级难以被超越；控制计入成本后，该策略在同等成本下位于固定层级前沿之上，且对抗可使critic驱动搜索失效的critic仍保持稳健。

Abstract: Inference-time methods that lift a small open video generator toward a proprietary one are compared through preference scores, which never say how much of the gap to a target video they close. We introduce the oracle control ladder, which hands a fixed student progressively richer information from a target clip and reads the closure of each fidelity metric at each rung as an empirical headroom reference for the methods that must synthesize that information. On 954 clips of the principal student, a dense caption reproduces little of the appearance and almost none of the motion of a target, a first frame closes half of the appearance gap and a tenth of the motion gap, a depth video closes three quarters of the motion gap and leaves color untouched, and the two together close 64 to 94% of every fidelity gap across four teachers, while a second student agrees on the rungs that it accepts. An item response model forecasts which rung closes which clip with held-out AUC of 0.76 to 0.95 on the fidelity criteria and serves as the prior of a budgeted selection policy. When controls are free, a fixed dense-control rung is hard to improve upon, and once controls are priced the policy lies above the fixed-rung frontier at matched cost and survives critics that ruin critic-driven search.

</details>


### [80] [Ego2Ego: Generative Visual Perspective Taking Across Collaborators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27459)
*Chuhan Jiao,John Gideon,Anshul Gupta,Guy Rosman,Andreas Bulling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"生成式视觉视角采样"(generative visual perspective taking)任务：给定自我视角视频、场景点云与协作者相机轨迹，生成协作者视角的连续第一人称视频；提出Ego2Ego几何模块增强视频扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将视角采样简化为状态估计、几何重建或离散问答；现有跨视角视频生成方法仅从输入视频推断，或依赖额外观测目标人物的外部相机，无法应对大视角偏移、动态遮挡与稀疏视觉重叠。

Method: Ego2Ego为与骨干无关的几何模块集合：向各视角渲染致密、时序一致场景坐标图作为共享空间参照；通过相对几何注意力在3D射线空间对齐跨视角token；利用自我视角视觉记忆补全当前视野外区域；接入预训练视频扩散模型。

Result: 在CoMind数据集、三种视频扩散骨干上，Ego2Ego一致提升视觉质量、Level-2场景布局精度与协作者运动建模，各项指标均优于现有跨视角视频生成基线。

Conclusion: 将视觉视角采样推进至人机协作：可推断人类协作者所见所为，并将单人录制转化为同步多视角视频用于机器人学习。

Abstract: Visual perspective taking, the ability to infer what another person sees from their position, is fundamental to human collaboration. However, prior works reduced perspective taking to state estimation, geometric reconstruction, or discrete question answering, whereas existing cross-view video generation methods either inferred the target view from the input video alone or required an external camera that observes the target person. We introduce generative visual perspective taking: Given an ego person's egocentric video, a scene point cloud, and a collaborator's camera trajectory, the task is to generate the continuous egocentric video from the collaborator's perspective. This task is challenging given extreme viewpoint shifts, dynamic occlusions, and sparse visual overlap. We address these challenges with Ego2Ego -- a set of backbone-agnostic geometric modules that provide a pretrained video diffusion model with the 3D scene geometry shared by both interactants. Ego2Ego renders dense, temporally consistent scene coordinate maps into every view as a shared spatial reference, aligns cross-view tokens in 3D ray space through relative geometric attention, and recovers regions outside the ego person's current view from the ego person's visual memory. Experiments on the CoMind dataset across three video diffusion backbones show that Ego2Ego consistently improves visual quality, Level-2 scene layout accuracy, and collaborator motion, outperforming state-of-the-art cross-view video generation baselines on every backbone. By generating the collaborator's egocentric video more accurately, Ego2Ego brings visual perspective taking to human-robot collaboration, where it can help infer what a human collaborator sees and does, and can turn single-person recordings into synchronised multi-perspective videos for robot learning.

</details>


### [81] [PaMuk-Bench: A Parallel Multilingual Multitask Benchmark for Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27521)
*Silviu-Florin Gheorghe,Radu-Stefan Popa,Adriana Valentina Costache,Adrian-Gabriel Chifu,Alessia Amelio,Paul Irofti,Anders Skaarup Johansen,Lallouani Bouchakour,Mohamed Lichouri,Khaled Lounnas,Neelu Madan,Yashvir Malik,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PaMuk-Bench：一个并行多语言多任务视觉-语言基准，含1万余条英文提示及六种语言平行翻译与配套生成/检索图像，覆盖五个跨模态任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在英语等高资源语言上表现优异，但在缺乏标注数据的低资源语言上性能显著下降，缺少可跨语言、跨任务对比评测的平行数据资源。

Method: 构建1万余条精选英文提示，经半自动流程翻译为阿拉伯语、丹麦语、法语、印地语、意大利语、罗马尼亚语，形成平行翻译，并配套生成与检索图像；基准覆盖文本到图像生成、图像描述、基于内容的图像检索、图像生成难度估计、图像检索难度估计五类任务；在此基线上对比零样本与微调的多语言预训练VLM。

Result: 实验显示零样本英文及多语言预训练VLM在多数任务与架构上一致劣于微调版本，验证基准具有区分度与可评测性。

Conclusion: PaMuk-Bench 是可用于跨任务、跨语言开发与评测VLM的有价值资源，有助于显著缩小多种VLM架构与任务上的语言鸿沟；基准已公开。

Abstract: While vision-language models (VLMs) currently obtain notable results for highly-studied languages, e.g. English, they exhibit significant performance drops in certain low-resource languages, especially due to the lack of annotated datasets. To this end, we propose PaMuk-Bench, a novel parallel multilingual multitask benchmark for vision-language tasks. Our benchmark includes a set of over 10K curated English prompts that are translated into six languages: Arabic, Danish, French, Hindi, Italian, Romanian. Parallel translations, which are obtained via a semi-automatic process, along with accompanying generated and retrieved images enable the comparative development and evaluation of VLMs across a wide range of tasks: text-to-image generation, image captioning, content-based image retrieval, image generation difficulty estimation, image retrieval difficulty estimation. We empirically show that zero-shot English and multilingual pre-trained VLMs compare consistently worse than their fine-tuned counterparts, confirming that PaMuk-Bench is a valuable resource that can drastically reduce the language gap across a variety of VLM architectures and tasks. PaMuk-Bench is available at https://github.com/ghesil/pamuk_bench.

</details>


### [82] [FlowScope: 3D Scene Flow Pretraining Improves Surgical Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26865)
*John J. Han,Adam Schmidt,Jingpei Lu,Zooey He,Muhammad Abdullah Jamal,Jie Ying Wu,Omid Mohareri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowScope通过预测场景流（metric 3D scene flow）对无标注临床立体视频做自监督预训练，仅预训练ACT主干、推理时丢弃flow head，即可在真实手术机器人上将穿针拾取成功率从54%提升至90%、首次递针交接从4%提升至52%，且无额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人模仿学习受限于示教数据采集成本高；大量机器人辅助手术以立体视频形式留存，但缺乏动作标签，策略无法直接学习。需要一种能从无标注临床视频中预训练手术策略的方法。

Method: 以off-the-shelf立体深度与光流模型计算标签，让策略网络预测场景流（场景点的度量3D运动）；在1,149例临床手术（1.35M片段）上预训练Action Chunking Transformer（ACT）主干，预训练后丢弃flow head，部署策略为标准ACT，无附加推理成本。设计多种消融预训练信号（运动学、2D轨迹、单独深度、无尺度3D流，各与运动学配对）及流匹配动作头验证。

Result: 真实手术机器人系统上（每项n=50次试验）：穿针拾取成功率54%→90%；首次递针交接成功率4%→52%（完整任务2%→14%）；换成生成式flow-matching动作头后增益仍保持。消融显示度量3D运动是所测最强预训练信号，其余信号增益更小或无增益。表征分析表明场景流预训练更准确编码度量针深度；运动学预训练增加对机器人状态的依赖；针深度探针的表现与真机成功率排序一致，而离线验证误差不一致。

Conclusion: 无标注临床立体视频中的度量3D场景流是有效且"零推理成本"的手术策略预训练信号；针深度线性探针可作为与真机表现更一致的表征评估指标，优于离线验证误差。

Abstract: Imitation learning for surgical robots is limited by the cost of collecting demonstrations. Meanwhile, many robot-assisted procedures are recorded as stereo video, yet policies cannot learn from them directly; clinical video carries no action labels. We present FlowScope, which pretrains a surgical policy network on unlabeled clinical video by forecasting scene flow, the metric 3D motion of scene points, with labels computed by off-the-shelf stereo depth and optical flow models. We pretrain the trunk of an Action Chunking Transformer (ACT) on a dataset of 1,149 clinical procedures (1.35M clips) and discard the flow head afterward, so the deployed policy is a standard ACT with no added inference cost. On a real surgical robotic system, FlowScope raises needle pickup success from 54% to 90% and first-handover success on needle regrasp from 4% to 52% (full task: 2% to 14%) over training from scratch (n=50 trials each), and retains its gains with a generative flow-matching action head. Ablations indicate that metric 3D motion is the strongest pretraining signal among those tested; kinematics, 2D tracks, depth alone, and scale-free 3D flow, each paired with kinematics, yield smaller or no gains. Representation analyses suggest that scene-flow pretraining encodes metric needle depth more accurately, that kinematics pretraining increases reliance on the robot state, and that needle-depth probes follow the ordering of real-robot success where offline validation error does not.

</details>


### [83] [CADENCE:Temporally Coherent Whole-Body Human Mesh Recovery that Preserves Expressive Hand Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26783)
*Keniel Peart,Jagmohan Chauhan,Indu P. Bodala,Shelly Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CADENCE全身SMPL-X单目视频系统，用逐帧估计器加学习式零相位滤波器混合的离线时间精化器，在抑制躯干抖动的同时保留快速手指动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身SMPL-X逐帧估计在视频上产生抖动，通用时间平滑虽能去抖但会压平承载语义的快速手指动作；现有仅评测躯干的基准无法衡量这一权衡。

Method: 共享C-RADIOv4编码器的逐帧估计器，附加复用同一编码器的高分辨率手部裁剪通路；随后用离线时间精化器处理SMPL-X旋转序列，通过沿运动学树的注意力为每个关节每帧选择学习到的零相位滤波器混合。

Result: 3DPW上加速度误差由17.4降至5.4 mm/s²（通用平滑为6.2–7.7），PA-MPJPE由41.9降至40.2 mm；SignAvatars/How2Sign上相比其他平滑器手部抖动最低，手速峰值幅度0.98（全SMPL-X重训SmoothNet仅0.35）；EHF上全身网格误差25.7 mm、手部关节误差8.3 mm均为最优。

Conclusion: 关节自适应、零相位的滤波混合能在保持手语与手势细节的前提下有效去抖，兼顾平滑性与动作保真度。

Abstract: Whole-body SMPL-X estimators recover body, hand, and face meshes from single images, but applied frame by frame to video they produce jittery motion. Smoothing the output removes the jitter at a cost that body-only benchmarks do not measure: a temporal filter strong enough to steady the torso also flattens the fast finger strokes that carry meaning in sign language and gesture. We present CADENCE, a whole-body SMPL-X system for monocular video designed around this trade-off. A per-frame estimator built on a shared C-RADIOv4 encoder, with a high-resolution hand pathway that reuses the same encoder on hand crops, produces each frame's estimate. An offline temporal refiner then filters the SMPL-X rotation sequence: attention along the kinematic tree selects, for every joint and every frame, a mixture of learned zero-phase filters, so the torso is smoothed while fast finger strokes pass through. On 3DPW, the refiner reduces acceleration error from 17.4 to 5.4 mm/s2 while lowering PA-MPJPE from 41.9 to 40.2 mm; generic smoothers applied to the same predictions reach 6.2 to 7.7 mm/s2. On SignAvatars/How2Sign sign-language sequences, the refiner has the lowest hand jitter among the compared smoothers while keeping a hand-speed peak amplitude of 0.98, against 0.35 for a SmoothNet retrained on the full SMPL-X vector. The per-frame estimator also obtains the lowest full-body mesh error (25.7 mm) and hand joint error (8.3 mm) among the compared methods on EHF.

</details>


### [84] [ConRef: Auditing Reference Reliability in 2D/3D Registration Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26758)
*Jianfeng Yue,Feng Li,Tianyu Song,Julius Watrinet,Ulrich Eck,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConRef（Consensus Reference）多视角框架，用于审计X-ray/CT 2D/3D配准基准中的参考位姿。通过联合多张X-ray观测估计额外参考位姿、量化其不确定度并识别异常X-ray，在BeadPhant数据上与独立珠基准偏差0.08–0.12 mm，而在Femur数据上与原参考差异达2.06 mm，并标记DeepFluoro 22张潜在异常图像，Femur F5上参考选择甚至改变亚毫米成功的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准推动了高精度2D/3D配准方法的发展，但当配准误差接近基准参考位姿自身的不确定度时，测得的性能可能受限于参考标注误差而非算法误差，因此需要系统审计现有基准参考位姿的可靠性。

Method: 提出ConRef多视角一致性框架：联合利用多幅X-ray观测，在已有参考之外估计一个附加参考位姿，同时对不确定度进行量化，并识别潜在的X-ray异常（outlier）。采用独立解剖学证据及新采集的基于珠标记参考位姿的X-ray/CBCT体模数据集（BeadPhant）进行验证。

Result: 在BeadPhant上ConRef与独立珠基准参考相差0.08–0.12 mm；在Femur数据集上与原参考差异达2.06 mm；在DeepFluoro上标记出22张潜在异常X-ray；在Femur F5上，参考位姿的选择改变了同一批配准结果的亚毫米成功判定结论。

Conclusion: 参考位姿差异会影响高精度2D/3D配准方法的评估。ConRef通过提供经独立验证的附加参考并识别潜在不一致，可对现有基准进行系统审计，从而支撑更可靠的配准误差评价。

Abstract: Purpose: Accurate 2D/3D registration is crucial for image-guided orthopedic interventions, enabling the alignment of preoperative 3D anatomy with intraoperative X-ray observations for surgical navigation. Public benchmarks have facilitated the development and evaluation of increasingly accurate registration methods. However, as registration errors approach the uncertainty of benchmark reference poses, measured performance may be limited by reference inaccuracies rather than algorithmic errors alone. Methods: We propose Consensus Reference (ConRef), a multi-view framework for auditing reference poses in existing X-ray/computed tomography (CT) registration benchmarks. By jointly exploiting multiple X-ray observations, ConRef estimates an additional reference pose, quantifies its uncertainty, and identifies potential X-ray outliers. We validate the framework using independent anatomical evidence and a newly acquired X-ray/cone-beam CT (CBCT) phantom dataset with bead-based reference poses. Results: On our BeadPhant dataset, ConRef differed from independent bead-based references by 0.08-0.12 mm, while discrepancies with original references reached 2.06 mm on the Femur dataset. It also flagged 22 DeepFluoro X-rays as potential outliers. On Femur F5, reference choice changed the submillimeter success conclusions of the same registrations. Conclusion: Our findings demonstrate that reference discrepancies can influence the evaluation of high-accuracy 2D/3D registration methods. By providing an additional, independently validated reference and identifying potential inconsistencies, ConRef enables systematic auditing of existing benchmarks and supports more reliable evaluation of registration error.

</details>


### [85] [DepthMSA: Combinatorial Training and Budget-Aware Depth Allocation for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27198)
*Pan Wang,Jie Peng,Zhimin Li,Jingxian Chen,Hui Ji,Huanrui Yang,Pengfei Zhou,Tianlong Chen,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DepthMSA，一个预算感知的多模态情感分析框架：通过组合式anytime训练让融合头覆盖所有跨模态深度组合，推理时用免训练、成本感知的分配器在样本级FLOPs预算下选择最优深度配置，实现模态异质编码深度下的有效融合与高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSA模型依赖大规模预训练编码器，却对所有模态一律以全深度处理，忽视其计算成本与预测贡献的不匹配；核心问题是：如何将有限计算在各模态间分配以保持预测性能。

Method: 1) 揭示模态编码器计算成本与加深处理所带来预测收益之间的显著错配，说明不同模态需要不同编码深度；2) 提出DepthMSA，在单一模型中支持灵活的模态深度；3) 引入combinatorial anytime training，用一次全深度编码器前向传播，在全部跨模态深度组合上监督融合头；4) 推理阶段采用免训练、成本感知的分配器，在样本特定的FLOPs预算下选择验证集校准误差最低的配置；5) 提供可单独训练的skip-enabled变体。

Result: 在CMU-MOSEI上，DepthMSA在精度与全深度模型相差1个百分点以内的情况下，实测FLOPs降低45%，skip-enabled变体降低82%；当CLIP视觉编码器主导计算时，分配策略转向削减视觉深度，在精度不变下FLOPs减少56%；与逐预算训练控制器的ADMN相比，skip-enabled变体以3.2倍更少FLOPs和更低延迟达到其精度，且无需任何逐预算训练。

Conclusion: 按模态分配异质编码深度并以成本感知方式动态选择配置，可在几乎不损失性能的前提下大幅降低MSA计算开销，且优于需逐预算训练的既有自适应层分配方法。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) models rely on large pretrained encoders, yet typically process every modality to its full depth, regardless of its computational cost or predictive contribution. This raises a fundamental question: how should limited computation be allocated across modalities to preserve predictive performance? We reveal a substantial mismatch between the computational cost of modality encoders and the predictive benefit of processing them more deeply, suggesting that different modalities require different encoding depths. However, enabling heterogeneous depths introduces a key challenge: representations extracted at different depths must still be effectively fused. To address this, we propose $\textbf{DepthMSA}$, a budget-aware framework that supports flexible modality depths within a single MSA model. Specifically, we introduce combinatorial anytime training, which supervises the fusion head across all cross-modal depth combinations using a single full-depth encoder pass. At inference, a training-free, cost-aware allocator selects the configuration with the lowest validation-calibrated error under a sample-specific FLOPs budget. On CMU-MOSEI, DepthMSA maintains accuracy within one percentage point of the full-depth model while reducing measured FLOPs by 45\%, or by 82\% with a separately trained skip-enabled variant. When a CLIP visual encoder dominates computation, the allocation shifts toward reducing visual depth, achieving 56\% fewer FLOPs at unchanged accuracy. Compared with ADMN, an adaptive layer-allocation method that trains a controller for each budget, the skip-enabled variant reaches its accuracy with 3.2$\times$ fewer FLOPs and lower latency, without any per-budget training. Our project is available at https://pwang322.github.io/DepthMSA/.

</details>


### [86] [SCAP: Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27187)
*Xiao Lin,Yaoxu Lyu,Ronghao Dang,Zhili Liu,Kaijing Ma,Yang Zhou,Dingning Liu,Runzhe Teng,Jun Cen,Shanghang Zhang,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCAP动作专家预训练阶段：在VLM输入端随机掩码视觉与文本token，从而在预训练中保留视觉–语言联合建模通路、降低视觉捷径依赖、提升对语言指令的依赖，在四种VLA架构上稳定涨点，真机三任务平均成功率76.67%（较全输入预训练+25个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单模态（VA或LA）动作专家预训练虽能抑制视觉捷径竞争，但会破坏后续联合预测所需的视觉–语言交互。作者用诊断模型表明：一旦单模态预训练关闭了原本活跃的视觉–语言联合建模通路，即便随后提供完整视觉与语言输入、且适配无限久，该通路也无法恢复。

Method: 构建统一诊断模型，使VA或LA预训练可关闭初始活跃的联合视觉–语言建模通路，以研究通路不可恢复性。基于此提出Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining（SCAP）：在全输入训练之前增设动作专家预训练阶段，进入VLM前对视觉token与文本token随机掩码，形成随机上下文。

Result: 在该诊断模型中，SCAP可在预训练及随后的全输入训练中保持联合视觉–语言建模通路；相比直接全输入训练，降低对视觉捷径的依赖、提高对语言指令的依赖。在四种不同VLA架构上一致提升性能；真机实验在三个指令条件操作任务上平均成功率76.67%，比全输入预训练高25个百分点。

Conclusion: 随机掩码上下文可作为动作专家预训练的有效手段，避免单模态预训练对多模态通路的破坏性副作用，兼顾抑制视觉捷径与保留视觉–语言联合建模能力，具备跨架构通用性与真机有效性。

Abstract: Single-modality action-expert pretraining controls competition from visual shortcuts before vision--language--action (VLA) adaptation. Yet single-modality pretraining can impair the vision--language interactions needed for subsequent joint prediction. We construct a diagnostic model in which VA or LA pretraining can disable an initially active pathway for joint vision--language modeling. In this model, subsequent training with complete visual and language inputs cannot restore the pathway, even with unlimited adaptation. We therefore introduce Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining (SCAP), a new action-expert pretraining stage that precedes full-input training. During this stage, visual and text tokens are randomly masked before entering the VLM. In this unified diagnostic model, SCAP can preserve the joint vision--language modeling pathway during pretraining and subsequent full-input training. Compared with direct full-input training, it can also reduce reliance on visual shortcuts and increase reliance on language instructions. SCAP consistently improves performance across four different VLA architectures. In real-robot experiments, it achieves 76.67% average success across three instruction-conditioned manipulation tasks, outperforming full-input pretraining by 25 percentage points.

</details>


### [87] [How Does SAM-3 Adapt? Capacity, Update Geometry, and Domain Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27142)
*Allan Kazakov,Abdurrahman Javat,Duygu Cakir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较LoRA、HiRA、KRAdapter、QuanTA与全量微调在SAM-3跨域分割适配中的表现：紧凑PEFT常可匹配全量微调，但其优势不能由秩或参数量解释，而源于方法特定的更新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM-3等可提示分割基础模型被日益用于专业领域，但超出原始训练分布时应如何微调仍不清楚，尤其是参数高效微调（PEFT）与全量微调（Full Fine-Tuning）之间的取舍缺乏机制性解释。

Method: 在五个分割数据集（医学影像、遥感、密集材料分割）上对比五种适配方案：LoRA、HiRA、KRAdapter、QuanTA与全量微调；以DSC和IoU评估；通过SVD分析更新谱，并考察LoveDA城市/乡村迁移下的分布偏移响应。

Result: 紧凑PEFT在DSC和IoU上常匹配或接近全量微调，且该表现无法由名义秩或可训练参数量解释；SVD显示全量微调更新具有谱可压缩性，但成功PEFT并非复现相同的更新方向，而是将变化集中于不同分量与子空间；域迁移中，域内表现相近的方法在分布偏移下响应不同。

Conclusion: SAM-3适配在实践中常呈容量饱和；有竞争力的微调来自方法特定的更新路径，而非对全量微调的单一低秩近似，因此适配方法选择应结合更新谱与分布偏移鲁棒性而非仅看参数效率。

Abstract: Promptable segmentation foundation models are increasingly used in specialized domains, but it remains unclear how SAM-3 should be fine-tuned when adapting it beyond its original training distribution. We study this question by comparing five adaptation families --LoRA, HiRA, KRAdapter, QuanTA, and Full Fine-Tuning-- across five segmentation datasets spanning medical imaging, remote sensing, and dense material segmentation. Across these datasets, compact parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods often match or approach Full Fine-Tuning in DSC and IoU, suggesting that strong in-domain adaptation does not always require updating the full model. However, this performance cannot be explained by nominal adapter rank or trainable parameter count alone. To investigate why different methods perform similarly, we analyze their update spectra and their behavior under LoveDA Urban/Rural transfer. Our SVD analysis shows that Full Fine-Tuning updates are spectrally compressible. However, successful PEFT methods do not simply recover the same update directions; instead, they often concentrate changes in different components and subspaces. The domain-transfer study further shows that methods with similar in-domain performance can respond differently under distribution shift. Together, these results suggest that SAM-3 adaptation is often capacity-saturated in practice, while competitive fine-tuning arises through method-specific update paths rather than a single low-rank approximation to Full Fine-Tuning.

</details>


### [88] [PIU-CR: Physics-Informed Deep Unfolding with SAR-Optical Image Fusion for Cloud Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27093)
*Gengque Fan,Han Xu,Junxi Li,Hao Zhang,Guangcan Liu,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理信息深度展开网络PIU-CR，将云去除建模为嵌入大气散射模型的优化问题，通过SAR引导的结构与光谱正则实现可解释的跨模态融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学-SAR融合去云方法多为黑盒数据驱动，跨模态交互可解释性差；引入物理先验的方法公式过于简化，无法忠实刻画物理成像过程，且网络与优化变量耦合松散。

Method: 将去云构建为物理信息约束的优化问题：数据保真项嵌入大气散射模型以刻画云退化机制；正则项建立显式跨模态交互机制，包括SAR引导的结构正则与光谱正则（结构特征对齐与光谱一致性约束）。随后将优化问题展开为含结构与光谱双分支的多阶段网络，每阶段对应一次显式优化迭代。

Result: 实验表明所提方法优于现有最先进方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 物理驱动的优化建模结合深度展开，可在保持跨模态交互可解释性的同时实现有效且高性能的光学-SAR融合去云。

Abstract: Multi-modal cloud removal exploits complementary optical and synthetic aperture radar (SAR) images to restore cloud-degraded remote sensing images. Most existing methods rely on black-box, data-driven architectures with limited interpretability of cross-modal interactions. Although some attempts introduce physical priors into networks, their oversimplified formulations fail to faithfully characterize the underlying physical imaging process and the networks remain loosely coupled with optimization variables. To address these issues, we propose a physics-informed deep unfolding network for cloud removal via optical-SAR image fusion, termed as PIU-CR. We first formulate cloud removal as a physics-informed optimization problem. Specifically, we fundamentally ground the optimization in the physical imaging mechanism of cloud degradation by embedding the atmospheric scattering model into the data fidelity term. Then, the regularization terms establish an explicit cross-modal interaction mechanism, overcoming the single-modal information scarcity and the obscure, uninterpretable interactions in black-box models. They are dedicated to SAR-guided structure and spectral regularization, jointly introducing cross-modal structural feature alignment and coordinated spectral consistency constraints. The resulting optimization problem is then unfolded into a multi-stage neural network with dedicated structure and spectral branches. Each stage corresponds to an explicit optimization iteration step, enabling interpretable and effective reconstruction process. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Fgq188/PIU_CR.

</details>


### [89] [Hindsight Forcing: Learning Better History from the Future in Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27074)
*Haonan An,Yihang Tao,Yu Guo,Renlong Chen,Jiasheng Zhang,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Hindsight Forcing，通过潜空间直通桥（latent straight-through bridge）将前向生成与反向信用分配解耦，使历史分块能直接从未来损失接收梯度，无需额外参数或架构修改即可缓解自回归视频扩散中的视觉漂移与结构崩塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步自回归视频扩散支持实时流式生成，但每个生成分块都作为后续生成的上下文。以往 self-rollout 方法为避免 BPTT 的内存爆炸，通常截断跨块梯度或分离（detach）历史潜变量，导致早期去噪计算无法通过生成的历史获得下游反馈——不知道哪些帧更利于后续生成，从而在长序列 rollout 中出现视觉漂移和结构崩塌。

Method: Hindsight Forcing：前向传播中每个分块完成完整多步 rollout 以更新 KV cache；反向传播中，通过直通桥把来自未来损失（future losses）的梯度回传至每个历史分块中单个选定的去噪步，从而在共享参数上同时训练 context writer 与 historical denoiser，而无需保留完整轨迹计算图。方法不需要额外模型参数、架构改动或独立的轨迹回放（trajectory replay）。

Result: 在 VBench 上的大量实验表明，Hindsight Forcing 在整体视觉质量与动态运动方面均优于现有基线；消融研究验证了对历史去噪进行监督所带来的收益。

Conclusion: 通过解耦前向生成与反向信用分配，Hindsight Forcing 以极低开销实现了对历史分块的"后见"监督，有效提升长程自回归视频生成的稳定性与质量，且具备良好的工程可部署性。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video diffusion enables real-time streaming generation, with each generated chunk serving as historical context for subsequent generation. To avoid the memory explosion of backpropagation through time, previous self-rollout methods typically truncate cross-chunk gradients or detach historical latents. As a result, earlier denoising computations receive no downstream feedback through the generated history on how to synthesize frames that facilitate subsequent generation, leading to visual drift and structural collapse as rollouts extend. To address this limitation, we propose Hindsight Forcing, which allows historical chunks to learn directly from future outcomes by decoupling forward generation from backward credit assignment via a latent straight-through bridge. In the forward pass, each chunk completes a full multi-step rollout to update the KV cache. In the backward pass, the bridge routes gradients from future losses back to a single selected denoising step per historical chunk, training both the context writer and historical denoiser on shared parameters without retaining the full trajectory graph. Remarkably, Hindsight Forcing requires no additional model parameters, architecture modifications, or separate trajectory replay. Extensive experiments on VBench demonstrate that Hindsight Forcing outperforms existing baselines in overall visual quality and dynamic motion, while ablation studies validate the benefit of supervising historical denoising. Code is available at https://github.com/haonanAN309/Hindsight-Forcing.

</details>


### [90] [$R^2$: Recognizing and Reducing Redundant Visual Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27070)
*Yilin Zhao,Jinyu Wang,Wenming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 R² 框架，识别并削减视觉-语言模型中冗余的视觉工具调用：用工具效用分类分析(CAT)揭示基准分数掩盖工具使用能力，用视觉反馈依赖(VFD)度量单次调用的必要性，并以 RA-GRPO 在保持准确率下减少 12.39% 平均工具调用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明视觉工具能提升多模态推理，但工具收益在不同样本间差异巨大；仅凭基准准确率无法判断工具交互是有益还是冗余，因此需要一个能识别并削减冗余工具调用的框架。

Method: 1) CAT（工具效用分类分析）：在样本级别按“是否受益于工具”对样本分类，量化工具收益分布；2) VFD（视觉反馈依赖）指标：在真实反馈与替代视觉反馈下比较同一后续推理的似然，无需外部评估器或轨迹重生成即可评估单次调用；3) RA-GRPO：将 VFD 依赖信号与类别条件下的基础调用成本结合，在正确轨迹间分配相对效率优势，补充结果型监督。

Result: 在七个基准上，受益于工具的样本至多占各基准的 12.44%，多数样本收益很小甚至受损；相比 Vanilla GRPO，RA-GRPO 在准确率相当的情况下平均工具调用次数减少 12.39%，按工具效用类别的宏平均准确率提升 3.35 个百分点。

Conclusion: 基准总分可能掩盖模型的真实工具使用能力；显式建模单次调用的反馈依赖并施加冗余感知的奖励，可在不牺牲准确率的前提下显著降低工具调用开销，并改善跨类别均衡表现。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that visual tools can improve multimodal reasoning. However, the effects of tools vary substantially across samples, and benchmark accuracy alone does not reveal whether tool interactions are beneficial or redundant. We present $R^2$, a framework that recognizes and reduces redundant visual tool use in vision-language models. We first introduce a sample-level Category Analysis of Tool Utility (CAT), which categorizes samples according to whether a model can benefit from tools. This analysis reveals that benchmark scores may obscure a model's tool-use ability. Across seven benchmarks, samples benefiting from tools account for at most 12.44% of each benchmark, while the majority gain little from tool interaction or are even harmed by it. To assess individual calls, we propose the Visual Feedback Dependency (VFD) metric, which compares the likelihood of the same subsequent reasoning under actual and alternative visual feedback without an external evaluator or trajectory regeneration. Redundancy-Aware GRPO (RA-GRPO) combines this dependency signal with a category-conditioned base call cost to assign relative efficiency advantages among correct trajectories, supplementing outcome-based supervision. Across the seven benchmarks, RA-GRPO reduces the mean number of tool calls by 12.39% relative to Vanilla GRPO at comparable benchmark accuracy. Evaluated by tool-utility category, RA-GRPO improves category macro-average accuracy over Vanilla GRPO by 3.35 percentage points.

</details>


### [91] [When Perception Collides with Priors: Diagnosing Modal Polarization in Video Understanding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26972)
*Yao Tong,Jiaxin Qi,Yan Cui,Jinli Ou,Yuhua Zheng,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了多模态大模型在视频理解中的"模态极化"失效模式，并提出TheaterBench基准加以评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在真实视频数据上训练取得较强理解能力，但当视频感知与内部先验或常识严重冲突时，模型无法有效整合模态，出现失效，亟需系统刻画与评测。

Method: 提出"模态极化"概念，界定两种极端：认知惯性（固守先验、无视矛盾视觉证据）与感知惯性（盲信视频感知、忽视逻辑不一致）；构建TheaterBench基准，采用违反物理规律或常识的反直觉场景进行测试。

Result: 大量实验表明当前SOTA模型存在严重的模态极化现象；多种去幻觉方法仅是在感知惯性与认知惯性之间重新平衡权衡，并未根本解决模态冲突。

Conclusion: 该工作为评估与提升未来视频理解模型的感知—认知平衡奠定了基础性一步。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong video comprehension, largely benefiting from extensive training on realistic video data. However, when video perception severely conflicts with internal priors or common sense, we identify a critical failure pattern termed Modal Polarization, where models fail to integrate modalities and instead bifurcate into two distinct extremes: Cognitive Inertia, where models rigidly adhere to priors despite contradictory visual evidence, and Perceptual Inertia, where they blindly trust video perception while ignoring logical inconsistencies. To investigate this, we introduce TheaterBench, a benchmark designed to expose Modal Polarization using counter-intuitive scenarios that violate physical laws or common sense - situations rarely encountered in standard training distributions. Extensive experiments reveal that current state-of-the-art (SOTA) models exhibit severe Modal Polarization, and many de-hallucination methods merely rebalance the trade-off between Perceptual and Cognitive Inertia rather than fundamentally resolving the conflict. This work serves as a foundational step for evaluating and enhancing the perceptual-cognitive equilibrium of future video understanding models.

</details>


### [92] [One state, many questions: Opsis gives a frontier model typed eyes for pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27102)
*Zhixuan Ge,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Opsis，在冻结病理编码器上以"类型化问句接口"取代"阅读描述/查看缩略图"，让前沿语言模型通过提问获取切片答案：Opsis-S为10M参数解码器（冻结Virchow2 token），Opsis-V嵌入Qwen3-VL-8B。四款前沿模型在乳腺问句上达0.78–0.79，泛癌问句较"阅读"高8.6分，单解码器可媲美逐问线性探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化病理报告是固定答案集的多选题，许多题目由其他题组合而成。现有两种方案各有缺陷：病理基础模型需为每题单独训练分类器（缺乏组合性）；前沿语言模型无法在上下文中容纳约1.4万tile的切片，只能用"事先写好的描述"（与问题无关）或"看缩略图+少量tile"（覆盖有限），在189道乳腺报告题上仅0.50–0.66，低于多数类基线0.67。

Method: 在冻结的病理编码器之上构建类型化问句接口：切片仅编码一次；问题在运行时指定算子、参数与候选答案，输出为候选上的概率分布。实例化为Opsis-S（冻结Virchow2 token上的10M参数解码器）与Opsis-V（集成进Qwen3-VL-8B）。

Result: 四款前沿模型借助Opsis-S在乳腺题上达0.78–0.79；3,797道泛癌题中"提问"比"阅读"高8.6分；995道未训练的复合题上，前沿模型组合Opsis-S答案达0.76（看图0.69，逐复合题训练的ABMIL为0.78）；10,712张TCGA切片泛癌集合上，单个Opsis-S解码器与逐题训练的TITAN/PRISM2线性探针持平（0.69 vs 0.70/0.68）；零样本分离CAMELYON16转移AUROC 0.86，仅凭报告标签定位坏死tile AUROC 0.93。

Conclusion: 将病理切片表征暴露为可被语言模型"提问"的类型化接口，可同时兼顾覆盖度与组合性：单一轻量解码器在多项任务上媲美逐题定制探针，并支持零样本迁移与弱标签定位，为报告式结构化问答提供了可扩展范式。

Abstract: A synoptic pathology report is a checklist of questions with fixed answer sets, many composed from others. Pathology foundation models answer them accurately with one classifier trained with labels for each, and frontier language models, which compose freely, cannot fit a slide of fourteen thousand tiles in their context. On 189 breast report questions, frontier models that read a written description of the slide or look at a thumbnail and tiles of it answer at 0.50 to 0.66, below the majority class at 0.67. A description is written before the question is known, and a look covers a handful of tiles. We present Opsis, which lets the model ask instead: a typed question interface on a frozen pathology encoder. A slide is encoded once, a question names an operator, arguments and candidates given at runtime, and the answer is a probability over the candidates. We build it as a 10 M parameter decoder on frozen Virchow2 tokens (Opsis-S) and inside Qwen3-VL-8B (Opsis-V). Asking Opsis-S, four frontier models answer the breast questions at 0.78 to 0.79, and on 3,797 pan cancer questions asking is 8.6 points above reading. On 995 composite questions that Opsis-S was never trained on, a frontier model composing its answers reaches 0.76, against 0.69 when it looks at the slide and 0.78 for ABMIL trained on each composite. On a pan cancer question set over 10,712 TCGA slides, one Opsis-S decoder matches linear probes trained per question on TITAN and PRISM2 embeddings (average accuracy 0.69 against 0.70 and 0.68). Opsis-S also separates CAMELYON16 metastases without training on that cohort (AUROC 0.86) and localises necrosis from report labels alone (tile AUROC 0.93).

</details>


### [93] [What Does a Zoom Actually Add? Decomposing Fine-Grained Perception Failures in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26904)
*Zhixuan Ge,Anqi Li,Kexin Geng,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过解耦"放大"操作中的多重因素，发现VLM被zoom救回的失败大多并非"缺像素"，而是需要对已见过的像素做更高密度重编码（重token化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献普遍把"放大区域后答案变对"解读为模型缺少该区域的像素信息，但zoom同时改变了四件事：去除周边上下文、对已见像素高密度重token化、以及（若初看被下采样）暴露新细节。作者希望为每个失败归因到最廉价的修复手段，从而区分"缺像素"与"渲染不足"两种解释。

Method: 固定首轮pass，仅改变"第二眼"。对每个被zoom救回的失败，按成本递增顺序测试四种修复：(1)复用缓存token、(2)隔离区域、(3)对已见像素重token化、(4)获取新像素，并归因到最廉价的有效项。实验覆盖Qwen2.5-VL、Qwen3-VL、InternVL3，在V*、ZoomBench、TextVQA上共2,753个被救回的失败，首轮预算从源图的1/17像素到原生分辨率。

Result: (1) 多数被救回失败并不缺像素：即便17倍下采样，对已见像素上采样可修复47–73%，而随机区域仅1–3%；(2) 首轮预算决定新像素的必要性：17x时需新像素占23–50%，4x时降至12–23%；(3) 有效放大倍率大且模态不同：物体在每边6–12x前持续受益，文本在4x饱和，且原生像素在超原生分辨率渲染下仍有增益；(4) 若允许任意放大，17x下68–94%的失败无需新像素。

Conclusion: "缺像素"解释与反对它的解释各自成立于不同预算区间。VLM更常需要的是对已有证据的更好渲染而非新像素，这支持在获取区域前"先重token化该区域"的设计原则。

Abstract: Zooming into a region often turns a wrong answer from a vision-language model (VLM) into a right one, and a growing literature takes this as evidence that the model lacked the pixels. But a zoom changes several things at once. It removes the surrounding context, re-tokenizes, at a higher density, pixels the model has already seen, and, if the first pass was downsampled, exposes detail the model never received. We separate these factors for each failure. Holding the first pass fixed, we vary only the second look and assign every failure to the cheapest one that fixes it: reusing cached tokens, isolating the region, re-tokenizing the seen pixels, or acquiring new ones. The study covers 2,753 zoom-rescued failures of Qwen2.5-VL, Qwen3-VL and InternVL3 on V*, ZoomBench and TextVQA, at first-pass budgets from 17x fewer pixels than the source to native resolution. First, most rescued failures did not lack the pixels: upsampling the pixels the model had already seen fixes 47-73% of them even at 17x downsampling, against 1-3% for a random region. Second, the first-pass budget decides how often new pixels are needed: depending on the benchmark, new pixels are required for 23-50% of the rescues at 17x and for 12-23% at 4x. The missing-pixel reading and the accounts that dispute it thus hold at different budgets. Third, the useful magnification is large and differs between objects and text: seen pixels fix more failures up to 6-12x their first-pass size per side on objects and saturate by 4x on text, and even native pixels gain from being rendered beyond native resolution. Allowing any magnification, 68-94% of the failures at 17x need no new pixels. These models more often need a better rendering of evidence they already have than new pixels, which motivates re-tokenizing a region before acquiring it.

</details>


### [94] [Spatial Query Execution as Verifiable Rewards for Multi-step Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26787)
*Jiyoon Pyo,Dongwon Jung,Zekun Li,Nayeon Kim,Muhao Chen,Yao-Yi Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FARON框架，用合成场景中的空间查询执行自动生成中间推理轨迹与可验证答案，统一为SFT和RLVR提供监督，显著提升LVLM多步空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在需要跨多个物体组合grounded关系的空间推理任务上仍不可靠；而人工标注的视觉数据集依赖固定关系标注与粗糙物体定位，难以在多样空间配置与复杂度下获得精确的中间推理状态，导致推理监督难以规模化。

Method: FARON构建具有显式几何的合成2D/3D场景，可配置物体布局、空间关系与多步组合；通过确定性查询执行得到中间集合与最终答案，无需人工关系标注。流水线将中间状态转写为自然语言推理轨迹用于SFT，执行得到的答案作为RLVR的可验证奖励，并进一步用GRPO优化。

Result: 受控实验表明基于轨迹的SFT优于仅用最终答案的监督，后续GRPO带来额外提升；所得模型在无需基准特定微调的情况下可迁移到多个外部空间推理基准及多种LVLM骨干。

Conclusion: 空间查询执行可作为统一、灵活的多模态空间推理监督来源，同时提供推理过程监督与可验证结果奖励。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) remain unreliable on spatial reasoning problems that require composing grounded relations across multiple objects. Yet, scaling reasoning supervision is challenging since human-annotated visual datasets often rely on fixed relational annotations and coarse object localization, making it difficult to obtain precise intermediate reasoning states across diverse spatial configurations and complexities. We introduce FARON (Fully Automated Reasoning Over spatial relatioNs), a framework that executes spatial queries to provide both execution-grounded reasoning supervision and verifiable outcome rewards. Rather than relying on existing annotated data, FARON constructs synthetic 2D and 3D scenes with explicit geometries, enabling configurable object arrangements, spatial relations, and multi-step compositions. Deterministic query execution yields intermediate sets and final answers, providing precise supervision without manual relation annotation. Our pipeline converts intermediate states into natural-language reasoning traces for supervised fine-tuning (SFT), while the execution-derived answers provide verifiable rewards for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Controlled experiments show that trace-based SFT improves multi-step spatial reasoning over final-answer supervision and that subsequent group relative policy optimization (GRPO) provides further gains. The resulting models also demonstrate transfer across external spatial reasoning benchmarks and multiple LVLM backbones without benchmark-specific fine-tuning. These results show the potential of using spatial query execution as a unified, flexible source of reasoning supervision and verifiable outcome rewards for multimodal spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [95] [Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Qwen3-VL for Handwritten Mathematical Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27208)
*Manh Tuan Ha,Xuan Bao Viet Tran,Vo Minh Dat Nguyen,Minh Nhat Vo,Quoc Trung Nguyen,Van Bay Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将HMER形式化为多模态结构化生成任务，用4-bit量化Qwen3-VL-4B加LoRA在有限算力下生成SLG，CROHME测试集精确匹配43.74%，显著优于ViT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写数学表达式识别需同时恢复符号身份与二维语法结构，局部合理的转写仍可能结构错误；同时希望在有限计算资源下验证紧凑视觉语言模型能否胜任该任务。

Method: 以4-bit Qwen3-VL-4B为起点，对视觉、语言、注意力与MLP模块施加rank-16 LoRA，用指令格式的CROHME 2019样本训练以生成线性化Symbol Label Graph（SLG）；与内部实现的ViT-Transformer基线、零样本Qwen3-VL及若干内部替代方案对比，并做数据组成消融。

Result: 在1,198个有效CROHME测试样本上，仅用CROHME训练的模型取得524个精确匹配（43.74%）、语料BLEU-4为77.67；ViT任务基线分别为4.92%与43.26。额外加入2,000个手写公式引导样本的消融使精确匹配降至42.32%。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是给出资源高效、有证据支撑的HMER多模态结构化生成范式，配合受控的数据组成消融，并分析了局部token重叠与全局结构正确性之间仍存在的差距；增数据非自动增益，反映域对齐问题。

Abstract: Handwritten mathematical expression recognition (HMER) requires joint recovery of symbol identity and two-dimensional syntax, so a locally plausible transcription may still be structurally wrong. We study whether a compact vision-language model can be adapted to generate linearized Symbol Label Graphs (SLGs) under limited computing resources. Starting from a 4-bit Qwen3-VL-4B checkpoint, we apply rank-16 Low-Rank Adaptation to the vision, language, attention, and MLP modules and train with instruction-formatted CROHME 2019 examples. We compare this model with an internally implemented ViT-Transformer baseline, a zero-shot Qwen3-VL configuration, and additional internally evaluated alternatives. On the 1,198 valid CROHME test examples, the CROHME-only model obtains 524 exact matches (43.74%) and a corpus BLEU-4 score of 77.67, compared with 4.92% and 43.26 for the task-specific ViT baseline. An ablation that adds 2,000 manually written, formula-guided samples decreases exact match to 42.32%, exposing a domain-alignment issue rather than an automatic benefit from more data. The principal contribution is therefore an evidence-backed, resource-efficient formulation of HMER as multimodal structural generation, together with a controlled data-composition ablation and an analysis of the remaining gap between local token overlap and globally correct structure.

</details>


### [96] [PhiJEPA: Denoising Latent Predictions for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27516)
*Makoto Yamada,Yao-Hung Hubert Tsai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhiJEPA——将PhiNet两阶段预测适配到掩码Transformer的联合嵌入预测架构，用两阶段潜在的预测从稀疏、光度退化的上下文中学习更适合退化与遮挡场景的视觉表征，并把通常被丢弃的预训练预测器复用于遮挡补全以提升世界模型规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA类方法多采用单阶段预测，且预训练预测器在迁移时通常被丢弃；本文关注：当物体部分离开视野且可见上下文被模糊等光度退化时，预测器能否仍帮助世界模型规划，以及两阶段潜在预测能否学到更鲁棒的稠密特征。

Method: PhiJEPA把PhiNet的两阶段预测移植到掩码Transformer上：第一阶段由一个Transformer块从稀疏、经光度破坏的上下文预测独立加噪教师视图的隐藏token；第二阶段用逐token MLP把这些预测映射到干净的教师token。通过受控消融比较单阶段替代方案；预训练于ImageNet-1K，训练量5.6×10^19 FLOPs。应用层面将冻结编码器用于DINO-WM的PushT任务，并无微调复用预训练预测器补全被遮挡token。

Result: 线性PASCAL VOC分割达69.3 mIoU，超过所评估的I-JEPA与DSeq-JEPA检查点，且训练FLOPs比I-JEPA少25%；隐藏区域分割优于Simple I-JEPA（49.1 vs. 44.3 mIoU）。在PushT开环规划中，同时存在物体遮挡与高斯模糊（σ=2像素）时，预测器补全把成功率从33%提升到65%，高于Simple I-JEPA配合其预测器的25%和iBOT像素修复的40%。

Conclusion: 两阶段潜在预测是一种高效学习视觉表征的途径：所得表征在光度退化下仍然有用，并能在部分观测下支持补全；预训练预测器值得保留并复用于下游世界模型规划。

Abstract: Can a predictor trained to infer hidden image regions still help a world model plan when part of an object disappears from view and the visible context is blurred? We introduce PhiJEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architecture that adapts PhiNet's two-stage prediction to masked Transformers. From sparse, photometrically corrupted context, a Transformer block predicts hidden tokens of an independently corrupted teacher view. A token-wise MLP then maps these predictions to clean teacher tokens. Controlled ablations show that the combined objective improves dense features over the tested one-stage alternatives. Pre-trained on ImageNet-1K with $5.6\times10^{19}$ training FLOPs, PhiJEPA achieves 69.3 mIoU on linear PASCAL VOC segmentation, exceeding the evaluated I-JEPA and DSeq-JEPA checkpoints in segmentation accuracy, with 25\% fewer training FLOPs than I-JEPA. It also improves hidden-region segmentation over Simple I-JEPA (49.1 vs.\ 44.3 mIoU). As an application, we use the frozen encoder in DINO-WM on PushT, and reuse the pre-training predictor, which JEPAs usually discard, to complete occluded tokens without fine-tuning. In open-loop planning with simultaneous object occlusion and Gaussian blur ($σ=2$ pixels), predictor completion raises success from 33\% to 65\%, compared with 25\% for Simple I-JEPA with its predictor and 40\% for iBOT with pixel inpainting. Together, these results support two-stage latent prediction as an efficient approach to learning visual representations that remain useful under photometric degradation and support completion under partial observation.

</details>


### [97] [BladeR: Scalable, Shape-Guided 3D Reconstruction of Wind Turbine Blades from UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27407)
*Jonathan Sterckx,Michiel Vlaminck,Hiep Luong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BladeR is a scalable pipeline for high-quality 3D reconstruction of wind turbine blades from drone inspection imagery, combining a hierarchical sparse reconstruction with a tiled Gaussian Splatting dense reconstruction at native 45 MP resolution.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Accurate 3D reconstruction of wind turbine blades enables precise localization and quantification of wear and early damage detection. Drone-based inspection is standard, but high-resolution imagery introduces weak texture, lighting variability, and high computational cost, which challenge conventional reconstruction pipelines.

Method: A hierarchical sparse reconstruction strategy uses GPS/IMU data and blade shape priors to guide advanced keypoint matchers on full-resolution images. For dense reconstruction, a tiled Gaussian Splatting framework processes 45 MP images at native resolution, isolates the blade from the background, and adaptively enhances detail recovery along the leading edge.

Result: Validation against commercial and open-source baselines shows significant improvements in model completeness and detail.

Conclusion: BladeR provides a scalable, high-fidelity 3D reconstruction solution for wind turbine blade inspection, improving completeness and detail over existing baselines and supporting downstream damage localization and quantification.

Abstract: The structural integrity of wind turbine blades is critical for safe, efficient energy production. Accurate 3D reconstruction allows precise localization and quantification of wear and early damage detection. While drone-based inspections are standard, their high-resolution imagery poses challenges such as weak texture, lighting variability, and high computational cost. We present BladeR, a scalable pipeline for high-quality 3D reconstruction from inspection imagery. A hierarchical sparse reconstruction strategy leverages GPS/IMU data and blade shape priors to guide advanced keypoint matchers on full-resolution images. For dense reconstruction, a tiled Gaussian Splatting framework processes 45 MP images at native resolution, isolates the blade from background, and adaptively enhances detail recovery along the leading edge. Validation against commercial and open-source baselines shows significant improvements in model completeness and detail.

</details>


### [98] [PAVE: A Harness for Geometrically Consistent Road Network Modeling with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27352)
*Yajun Liu,Jinxi Wu,Juan Pablo Millán Raya,Xuesong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 PAVE（Perceive, Anchor, Verify, Edit）框架，将测量交给代码、规则交给交通工程知识、开放判断交给VLM，并以网络ID锚定检查，从而把信号交叉口正确车道布局比例从单次请求的49–64%提升至86–91%，网络编辑18项中16项通过全部校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通模型（仿真与规划）的可靠性取决于其路网数据（车道、交叉口转向）的精度。实践中路网需人工从GIS录入或由开放地图生成，但OSM车道标签在测试的44个进口道中仅13个布局正确；卫星与街景影像虽覆盖全部44个且反映实际标线，现有VLM直接读取时缺乏几何一致性（同一车道在卫星裁剪图、不同位姿街景、带ID路网间难以对应）。

Method: PAVE四阶段流水线：代码负责可量化测量；交通工程知识以通用规则与检查形式注入；模型仅做开放判断，且每个判断锚定到网络ID，使检查能捕获与测量结果、二次读取或交通规则相矛盾的答案。关键策略是逐进口道在描摹车道上读取。编辑任务中模型仅命名设施各部分的链路与节点，由代码依据设计模板构建编辑。

Result: 对信号交叉口人工标注评分：两个VLM的精确正确车道布局比例由单次请求的49–64%提升至86–91%，增益主要来自逐进口道在描摹车道上读取。编辑任务中18项编辑有16项通过全部网络检查（模型自行写代码时为0项）；错误项为2项（对照7项）。分析揭示了VLM几何一致性失效的位置及各失败对应的检查。

Conclusion: 将测量、规则与开放判断分工并由ID锚定校验，可显著提升VLM在路网提取与编辑中的几何一致性与正确率；该框架兼具可解释性，能定位并捕获特定失败模式。

Abstract: Transportation models, whether they simulate traffic or support planning, are only as reliable as the road networks they run on, down to the lanes and turns at each intersection. Practitioners still take such a network from GIS files and supply its junction and movement data by hand, a task that recurs with each new base year and design alternative, or they generate it from open map data. OpenStreetMap's lane tags give the correct layout for only 13 of the 44 approaches we test; open map data are often incomplete or inaccurate. Satellite and street-level imagery, in contrast, cover all 44 and show their lanes as painted. Vision-language models (VLMs) can read such imagery, but asked directly they lose geometric consistency when the same lane must be matched across a satellite crop, street-level photos taken from different poses and an ID-labelled network. We therefore present PAVE (Perceive, Anchor, Verify, Edit), a harness in which code measures what can be measured, traffic engineering knowledge enters as general rules and checks, and the model makes only the open judgements, each anchored to network IDs so that checks catch answers that contradict a measurement, a second reading or a traffic rule. Scored against human labels at signalised intersections, PAVE raises the share of exactly correct lane layouts from 49-64% with a single request to 86-91% for two VLMs, with most of the gain coming from reading one approach at a time on traced lanes. The same division of work edits a real network to a planning brief: the model names the links and nodes of each part of a facility, and code builds the edit from a design template. Of 18 edits, 16 pass every network check, against none when the model writes its own code, and 2 contain a design or coding error, against 7. An analysis shows where VLMs lose geometric consistency and which check catches each failure.

</details>


### [99] [EgoLAM: Learning Latent Actions from Hand and Head Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27290)
*Ziyi Ding,Yiwei Wang,Zhongxing Wei,Xianxin Lai,Yechi Liu,Xiaodi Huang,Jinghua Hou,Xiaoquan Sun,Haijier Chen,Zhe Liu,Yinan Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoLAM是一种潜动作模型：在训练中用实测腕部运动监督动作码，并把相机运动单独送入前向模型，从而在自我中心视频中把"操作"与"佩戴者转头"解耦；仅用两帧RGB即可提码，腕部运动解码得分居八模型之首，LIBERO三套任务平均成功率79.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频中相邻帧的变化同时包含手部操作与相机自身运动（如佩戴者转身），二者都有助于预测下一帧，但只有前者描述真正的操作。已有潜动作模型未做此区分，导致从视频中学到的动作表示被相机运动污染。

Method: 训练阶段用实测腕部运动监督动作码，并将相机运动作为独立输入提供给前向模型；动作编码器对每只手周围的目标槽（object slots）做池化，腕部平移与旋转可在统一参考系下从动作码解码；推理时仅需两帧RGB即可提取动作码。

Result: 在LARYBench分类与回归任务之外，额外评测冻结动作码对腕部运动的保留程度及其对合成视角/光照变化的响应强度；EgoLAM在八个模型中腕部运动解码得分最高。LIBERO三套任务上，预训练码+专家示范微调的策略平均成功率79.38%，分别高于专家行为克隆28.73个百分点、高于各套中最强其他潜动作模型平均9.76个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式解耦相机运动与操作运动可得到更纯净、更具可解码性的潜动作表示，并能显著提升下游策略学习效率与成功率，说明"相机运动不应混入动作目标"是自我中心视频表示学习的关键设计原则。

Abstract: Latent action models let a robot policy learn from video by turning the change between two frames into a training target. In egocentric video, a cup can move across the image because a hand pushes it or because the wearer turns. Both changes help predict the next frame, but only the first describes manipulation. During training, EgoLAM supervises the action code with measured wrist motion and supplies camera motion separately to the forward model. Even when the wearer turns while pushing a cup, the training target still describes how the hand pushed it. The action encoder pools object slots around each hand, and wrist translation and rotation can be decoded from the code in a common reference frame. Extracting a code then requires only two RGB frames. Alongside LARYBench classification and regression, we measure how much wrist motion a frozen code retains and how strongly it responds to a synthetic view or lighting change. EgoLAM has the highest hand-motion decoding score among eight evaluated models. Across three LIBERO suites, policies pretrained on its codes and fine-tuned on expert demonstrations achieve 79.38% mean success, 28.73 points above expert behavior cloning and 9.76 points above the strongest other latent action model in each suite on average.

</details>


### [100] [Budgeted Safety Verification for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27268)
*Yuxin Cao,Ziqi Ding,Jiayu Zhang,Xianglin Yang,Wei Song,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以"曝光量"（观众实际看到的有害帧数）衡量流式视频生成的安全护栏，并利用生成器已有的隐状态作为廉价触发信号，在固定审查预算下按需调用昂贵的视频语言模型检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成器逐块即时释放内容，等待整段生成完成再判断的安全性决策在危害已发生后才能生效；而逐块调用精确视频语言模型检测器成本过高，因此需要研究在有限审查预算下把预算花在何处。

Method: 以"曝光"（被展示的有害帧数）为指标；用生成器已计算的隐状态训练廉价打分器，据此决定何时调用基于视频语言模型、读取解码帧的精确检测器；在599条蒸馏因果生成器流上做对比，包括无防御、逐块全检、随机触发、以帧级检测器替换视频级检测器等基线，并进行扩展性与迁移性测试。

Result: 无防御时81帧中平均有46.25有害帧；逐块全检降至0.91帧但每条良性流需打80.1分；护栏触发时仅需5.3分即降到2.35帧；随机分配同等预算仍剩37.20帧；帧级检测器在同一角色下多出近6帧；排序收益随流长度增加而增大且可迁移到第二系统；独立护栏模型与oracle一致度κ=0.74，50人盲审给出精确率96.1%、召回率82.8%。

Conclusion: 安全投入的关键在于"排序"而非"全检"：廉价隐状态护栏能显著提升固定预算下的安全性，且判定阶段必须以整块片段（视频级）而非单帧为输入。

Abstract: A streamed video generator releases each chunk the moment that it is produced, so a safety decision that waits for the finished clip arrives after the harm has been seen. We measure a guardrail by exposure, the harmful frames a viewer is shown, and ask where a fixed moderation budget should be spent. The accurate detector reads decoded frames with a video language model and is too expensive for every chunk, so our guard reads hidden states the generator has computed already and spends a cheap score on deciding when to call it. On 599 streams from a distilled causal generator the undefended model shows 46.25 harmful frames of 81. The video model on every chunk leaves 0.91 frames at 80.1 scored per benign stream, and the same model triggered by our guard leaves 2.35 at 5.3. Spending that budget at random leaves 37.20 frames, so the gain is in the ordering, and a frame detector in the same role gives up almost six, so the judging stage must read the chunk as a clip. The ordering is worth more as the stream lengthens, and the guard carries to a second system. An independent guardrail model agrees with our oracle at $κ=0.74$, and a blinded audit by 50 participants puts its precision at 96.1% and recall at 82.8%. Warning. This paper contains examples of generated content that some readers may find disturbing or offensive in nature.

</details>


### [101] [DiTraR: Diverse Scorers and Transcript-Guided Re-ranking for Video Corpus Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27931)
*Mohammad Alijanpour Shalmani,Alale Rezvani Boroujeni,Ali Amini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiTraR 通过“见证区间精确打分 + 多打分器能量集成 + 冻结语言模型对话验证器”三条互补路径解决语料级视频语料级时刻检索中的视频选择错误，在 TVR 验证集上取得 24.36/13.55 的 R@1@IoU0.5/0.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现强语料级检索器的 top-ranked 错误主要源于选错视频而非边界不精确，且对现有打分器已有证据重新加权无法修复该类错误，因此需要从视频选择层面专门建模。

Method: 提出 DiTraR，包含三个组件：(1) witness-interval scorer，将查询分解为 probes，对每个语料视频的每个候选区间进行精确打分；(2) multi-scorer energy ensemble，在一次穷举搜索前对多个独立训练打分器的能量取平均；(3) transcript verifier，用冻结语言模型阅读 top-ranked 视频的带时间戳对话，在不改变边界的前提下重排时刻。

Result: 在 TVR 验证集上，所有设置在开发集冻结并仅评测一次，IoU 0.5 与 0.7 处 R@1 分别为 24.36 和 13.55；集成提升视觉内容类查询，验证器提升对话类查询，二者收益基本可叠加。

Conclusion: 针对视频选择错误可从精确区间打分、能量集成与对话验证三个互补方向有效缓解，且不同组件对查询类型的增益具有可加性，为该任务提供了模块化改进框架。

Abstract: Video corpus moment retrieval asks a system to find, in a large collection of untrimmed videos with subtitles, the video and the time interval that a natural-language query describes. We find that most top-ranked errors of a strong corpus-level scorer come from choosing the wrong video, not from imprecise boundaries, and that re-weighting the evidence the scorer already uses does not fix them. We present DiTraR, which addresses video selection from two complementary directions. A witness-interval scorer decomposes the query into probes and scores every candidate interval of every corpus video exactly; a multi-scorer energy ensemble averages the energies of independently trained scorers before one exhaustive search; and a transcript verifier, a frozen language model that reads the time-stamped dialogue of the highest-ranked videos, re-orders the moments without changing their boundaries. With all settings frozen on development data and a single evaluation, DiTraR reaches 24.36 and 13.55 R@1 at IoU 0.5 and 0.7 on the TVR validation split. The ensemble improves queries about visual content, the verifier improves queries about dialogue, and their gains largely add up.

</details>


### [102] [Interpretable Tissue Architecture Complements Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27881)
*Zeeshan Shahid,Hammam M. AlGhamdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出139维"细胞-社区"组织架构表征收（9类特征组），通过与CTransPath、PLIP基础模型嵌入在MIL框架下做特征级融合，验证可解释的组织架构测量能为基础模型表征提供互补预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织架构对组织病理学评估具有重要价值，但其与病理基础模型表征之间的互补预测价值尚不明确。作者希望探究可解释的细胞级与多细胞级组织组织化测量能否提供超越学习表征的额外预测信息。

Method: 构建139维细胞到社区的表征，涵盖核形态、细胞组成、空间连通性、群落组织与细胞间相互作用共9个特征组；以多示例学习（MIL）方式，分别在独立使用与与CTransPath、PLIP嵌入做特征级融合两种设定下系统评估其预测贡献。BCNB数据集上，对ER、PR分类在四种MIL架构下采用五折患者不交叉交叉验证；LungHist700上评估四个分类任务。

Result: BCNB上，选定的CTransPath融合配置ER/PR平均ROC-AUC达0.9144/0.8360，PLIP融合为0.8791/0.8015；患者级分析发现ER相关差异体现在核形态、图连通性、细胞群落与细胞间相互作用。LungHist700上，融合配置在四个任务上平均ROC-AUC普遍更高，如腺癌vs鳞癌0.7967（CTransPath单独为0.7137）。

Conclusion: 可解释的组织架构描述子能够补充基础模型表征，同时提供对组织病理学组织化结构的可测量洞见。

Abstract: Tissue architecture provides important information for histopathological assessment, yet its complementary predictive value alongside pathology foundation-model representations remains insufficiently understood. This study investigates whether interpretable measurements of cellular and multicellular tissue organization provide additional predictive information beyond learned representations. We introduce a 139-dimensional cellular-to-community representation comprising nine feature groups characterizing nuclear morphology, cellular composition, spatial connectivity, community organization, and cell-cell interactions. Their predictive contributions are systematically evaluated independently and through feature-level fusion with CTransPath and PLIP embeddings using multiple-instance learning (MIL). On the BCNB dataset, estrogen receptor (ER) and progesterone receptor (PR) classification is evaluated across four MIL architectures using five-fold patient-disjoint cross-validation. Selected CTransPath fusion configurations achieved mean ROC-AUC values of 0.9144 (ER) and 0.8360 (PR), while PLIP fusion achieved 0.8791 and 0.8015, respectively. Patient-level analyses identified ER-associated differences in nuclear morphology, graph connectivity, cellular communities, and cell-cell interactions. On LungHist700, selected fusion configurations achieved higher mean ROC-AUC across four classification tasks, including adenocarcinoma versus squamous cell carcinoma (0.7967 versus 0.7137 for CTransPath alone). Overall, these findings suggest that interpretable tissue-architecture descriptors can complement foundation-model representations while providing measurable insights into histopathological organization.

</details>


### [103] [GameScrub: Learning HUD Removal from Real Gameplay with Recovered Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27547)
*Mohammad Areeb Qazi,Yiyan Hu,Yanan Wang,Xuezhi Liang,Xin Li,Fan Yang,Xin Gao,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出用真实游戏录像+视频扩散模型修复HUD区域来自动构造伪干净配对数据（GameScrub-Data），并据此训练无掩码、无参考帧的HUD去除扩散模型GameScrub，在Game2World基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏视频是学习虚拟世界动态的优质数据，但画面中的HUD遵循界面规则而非场景动态。去除HUD需成对数据（有/无HUD），真实录像只有带HUD版本；现有合成配对法需单独干净语料，且合成HUD与真实HUD差异大，模型残留40–50%真实HUD元素。

Method: 直接从真实录像反推伪干净目标：用预训练视频扩散模型对HUD区域做修复。关键技术点：编码前用光流引导的背景估计预填充HUD区域；将HUD排除在场景条件之外；通过像素空间合成将修复限制在掩码区域内，防止潜空间自编码器跨掩码边界信息泄漏。用所得配对训练GameScrub视频扩散去除模型。

Result: 在in-the-wild Game2World基准上，GameScrub取得伪影校正去除分数71.95（最强无参考GameCleaner配置为57.86），HUD元素去除率90.06% vs 59.07%。

Conclusion: 真实录像配合反推目标可支撑有效HUD去除，无需推理时掩码或干净参考帧，可为世界模型与可控视频生成提供无HUD游戏数据。

Abstract: Gameplay videos are a rich source of experience for learning virtual-world dynamics, yet their frames contain heads-up displays (HUDs) that follow interface rules rather than scene dynamics. Removing HUDs would make this footage directly usable for learning world dynamics. However, training a removal model requires paired clips with and without HUDs, whereas real recordings contain only HUD-overlaid footage. Existing work synthesizes pairs by compositing HUD graphics onto HUD-free footage, requiring a separately curated clean corpus and producing HUDs that only approximate real ones. Models trained on such pairs leave 40 to 50% of real HUD elements untouched. We instead recover pseudo-clean targets directly from real recordings by inpainting HUD regions with a pretrained video diffusion model. Preventing HUD traces from leaking into recovered targets is challenging because latent video autoencoders mix information across mask boundaries. Our training-free recovery pipeline, GameScrub-Data, pre-fills HUD regions with flow-guided background estimates before encoding, excludes HUDs from scene conditioning, and confines restoration to masked regions through pixel-space compositing. The resulting pairs train GameScrub, a video diffusion removal model that handles diverse HUDs without inference-time masks or clean reference images. On the in-the-wild Game2World benchmark, GameScrub achieves an artifact-adjusted removal score of 71.95, compared with 57.86 for the strongest reference-free GameCleaner configuration, and removes 90.06% of annotated HUD elements versus 59.07%. These results demonstrate that real recordings with recovered targets can support effective HUD removal, advancing HUD-free gameplay data for world models and controllable video generation.

</details>


### [104] [Ref-YOLO-World: Decoupling Text-Side Computation for Efficient Referring Expression Comprehension on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27756)
*Yuki Aoki,Hiroo Bekku,Tadashi Okoshi,Jin Nakazawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ref-YOLO-World，将仅依赖指代表达的文本计算与图像处理解耦并跨帧复用缓存的分词级文本特征，通过两个轻量模块注入YOLO-World检测路径，在保持高精度的同时显著提升效率并实现端侧部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高精度REC方法产生图像相关的多模态表示，需对每一帧重新计算，即使指代表达不变；而YOLO-World等快速开放词汇检测器虽可一次编码文本并复用，但将整个指代表达编码为单一嵌入，缺乏显式的token级对应关系。

Method: Ref-YOLO-World分离文本侧计算（仅依赖指代表达）与图像处理，并在帧间复用其输出；采用两个轻量模块将缓存的分词级（per-token）文本特征注入YOLO-World的检测路径，从而保留token级对应并避免逐帧重算。

Result: 在RefCOCOg val上达到81.29% Acc@0.5，为具有解耦文本分支的REC模型中最高，并持续优于微调的YOLO-World；预计算使单图推理时间减少18.5%，达47.0 FPS；Core ML版本在iPhone 15 Pro上以15.73 FPS运行。

Conclusion: 通过解耦文本分支并缓存复用分词级文本特征，可在不牺牲精度的前提下显著提升REC推理效率，并具备高效的端侧（on-device）部署能力。

Abstract: Many high-accuracy referring expression comprehension (REC) methods produce image-dependent multimodal representations. These representations must be recomputed for every video frame, even when the referring expression remains unchanged. Fast open-vocabulary detectors such as YOLO-World instead pre-encode text once and reuse it throughout the stream. However, encoding each referring expression as a single embedding prevents explicit token-level correspondence. We propose Ref-YOLO-World, which separates text-side computation that depends only on the referring expression from image processing and reuses its outputs across frames. Two lightweight modules inject cached per-token text features into YOLO-World's detection path. Ref-YOLO-World achieves 81.29% Acc@0.5 on RefCOCOg val, the highest among REC models with a decoupled text branch, and consistently outperforms a fine-tuned YOLO-World. Precomputation reduces per-image inference time by 18.5%, yielding 47.0 FPS. A Core ML version runs at 15.73 FPS on an iPhone 15 Pro, demonstrating efficient on-device REC. Code is available at https://github.com/sakusaku3939/Ref-YOLO-World.

</details>


### [105] [CanopyFormer: Attention-Guided SegFormer for Explainable Forest Cover Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27674)
*Dhinanjaya Fernando,Dinura Ginige,Kalana Lakshan,Chanupa Gurusinghe,Lasana Pahanga,Dileesha Manamperi,Thimira Sahan,Sandareka Wickramanayake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CanopyFormer：为 SegFormer-B0 引入注意力一致性损失，使注意力对齐冠层掩膜（AAMO 0.0334→0.7476），Dice 略降至 0.8577，且发现对齐提升不等于忠实性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空森林分割中，注意力通常仅作为架构组件、仅在训练后做定性可视化，缺乏定量对齐与忠实性评估，注意力监督在此领域尚未被系统研究。

Method: 基于 SegFormer-B0（SegFormer-B0）构建注意力引导模型 CanopyFormer，用 Attention Consistency Loss 将注意力与真实森林掩膜对齐；针对空间降采样注意力（SRA），将 Gradient-weighted Attention Rollout 限定在注意力矩阵为方阵的最后一层编码器阶段；提出 Average Attention-Mask Overlap（AAMO）量化注意力-掩膜对齐度，并引入删除测试评估忠实性；同时考察边界细化模块与 MSE/KL 两种注意力损失的影响。
