### [172] [LLMs Memorize Their Own Hallucinations During Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26863)
*Anirudh Atmakuru,Tanya Goyal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将"以推理为中心"的后训练配方（如结果奖励RL、认知行为注入）迁移到知识任务时，发现其会诱导模型记住自己在长推理链中产生的幻觉；作者在6个开源模型、2个模型族、2种架构上验证了该假设，并刻画了幻觉记忆的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型的后训练配方深受推理域（数学、代码）影响，但实际部署场景远超数学与代码。已有工作关注幻觉的产生与检测，却未系统考察"训练过程本身"如何让模型习得幻觉。作者提出一个此前未被审视的路径：推理导向训练决策虽利于推理，却可能在知识任务上无意间强化模型对自生成幻觉的记忆。

Method: (1) 以标准后训练流程训练模型，在RL阶段抽取模型自生成的幻觉内容；(2) 在后续训练中持续追踪这些幻觉是否被记忆（recall）；(3) 设置对照：被奖励/有训练信号的幻觉 vs. 无训练信号的自生成幻觉；(4) 考察回撤（backtracking）模板下的虚假事实是否仍被记住；(5) 分析记忆强度与训练问题难度的关系，以及奖励幻觉对后续弃答（abstention）行为的影响；实验覆盖6个开源模型、2个模型族、2种架构。

Result: 训练中被强化的幻觉其召回率平均提升4.83%，而无训练信号的自生成幻觉仅提升0.17%；模型甚至会记住在推理中立即被自我否定的虚假事实，尽管该回撤模板源自推理训练、对知识回忆毫无益处；记忆效应在最难的训练问题上最强；奖励某个幻觉会使模型后续对该幻觉弃答的概率平均下降7.23%。

Conclusion: 直接移植推理中心的训练配方到知识任务会带来幻觉记忆这一副作用：奖励机制无意中把自生成幻觉固化为模型知识，并削弱其弃答能力。该工作识别并系统研究了一条此前未被考察的"训练期幻觉习得"路径，提示需针对知识任务重新设计奖励与推理行为注入策略。

Abstract: Reasoning domains have shaped the post-training recipes of frontier models even though they are trained and deployed in domains beyond math and code. In this paper, we study the consequences of directly adopting reasoning-centric training recipes, e.g., the use of outcome-based rewards for reinforcement learning (RL), injection of "cognitive" behaviors such as proposing and backtracking from hypotheses in reasoning chains, etc., for knowledge tasks. We hypothesize that these decisions, while useful for reasoning, inadvertently encourage models to memorize hallucinations they themselves generate in their long reasoning chains when trained on knowledge tasks. To study this, we train models with standard post-training, extract their self-generated hallucinations during RL, and track memorization throughout the rest of training. Our experiments on six open models across two model families and two architectures confirm our hypothesis. We find that models' recall of hallucinations reinforced during training increases by 4.83% on average, compared to just 0.17% for self-hallucinations that receive no training signal. In fact, models even memorize false facts that they immediately retract in their reasoning; a backtracking template inherited from reasoning-centric training that has no benefit for knowledge recall. Furthermore, we find that memorization is strongest on the hardest training questions, and rewarding a hallucination makes the model less likely to abstain from it later (by 7.23% on average). Overall, our work identifies and systematically studies a previously unexamined pathway through which models learn hallucinations during training.

</details>


### [173] [Weight-Space Programming Writes In-Context Learning and Matches Fine-Tuning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26841)
*Konstantinos Skianis,Aristidis Likas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 前沿语言模型可将few-shot与zero-shot的残差提取为"ICL任务向量"并写入较弱模型权重，使弱模型无需提示即可完成任务，效果接近ICL与微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究示例能否通过权重更新（而非提示）传递给较弱语言模型，并分析编辑器究竟修改了什么。前沿模型具备"权重空间编程"能力，即无需梯度下降即可读写另一网络的权重，本文基于此能力展开系统研究。

Method: 覆盖6个任务、4个前沿编辑器、3类较弱语言模型，且编辑器未被告知任何关于ICL的信息。在多数运行中，将few-shot与zero-shot的残差在最终提示token处测量为ICL任务向量，并将其写入权重，最稀疏处作用于与提示格式绑定的近静默MLP单元。

Result: 弱模型准确率达到ICL与同示例微调（含LoRA）的水平，且通常不改变困惑度；但在措辞改变时性能下降。计入未完成运行后，有一个编辑器低于微调；仅给予已见示例、无任务对时，只有最强编辑器保持相近表现。

Conclusion: 权重空间编程可实现有效的示例迁移，但存在对提示措辞的敏感性及编辑器间差异；给定任务与无标签输入时，最强编辑器可自行生成示例，在未见样本上表现接近有标签情形。

Abstract: Weight-space programming is a frontier language model's recently reported ability to read and rewrite another network's weights without gradient descent. We investigate how examples can provide a weight update so that a weaker language model performs the task with nothing in the prompt, and study what it edits. Our experiments cover six tasks, four frontier editors and three families of weaker language models, without the editors being told anything about in-context learning (ICL). In most runs, the few-shot minus zero-shot residual is measured at the final prompt token, the ICL task vector, and write it into the weights, at its sparsest into near-silent MLP units keyed to the prompt format. Results reach the accuracy of ICL and of fine-tuning on the same examples, LoRA included, usually leaving perplexity untouched, with worse performance under changes of wording. We also qualify the comparison by counting unfinished runs, observing that one editor falls below fine-tuning; held only to the seen examples, without task pairs of their own, only the strongest editor has similar performance. Given a task and unlabelled inputs, that editor writes its own examples and does nearly as well on unseen items as with labels.

</details>


### [174] [How Long Will My Language Model Run? A Benchmark for Pre-Execution Runtime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26875)
*Ashish Molakalapalli,Kangda Wei,Haonan Duan,Alex Carter,Ruihong Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 PreRun 基准，用执行前可得信息预测深度学习训练/推理运行时长；结果显示主流 LLM 在零样本下严重低估时延，远不如传统回归基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享 GPU 集群用户需要运行时估计来设定时限、选择硬件与预算算力；直接用 LLM 描述任务并询问是一种诱人的捷径，但其可靠性缺乏系统评测。

Method: 构建 PreRun 基准：801 条训练与 1,884 条推理记录（语言与视觉模型，GPU 集群采集），配套负载配置与明确定义的计时目标；另含 64 个标准化文本生成配置、每个在三次独立程序启动下测量。对比 Qwen3.5-9B、GPT-OSS-120B、Claude Opus 5.5 三个 LLM 与回归基线，并在受支持的前向传播子集上评估解析估计。

Result: 在 16 个留出生成配置上，无示例时开放权重 LLM 平均低估时延 90–99%；Claude 零样本 MAE 为在 32 个配置上拟合的随机森林的 4.5 倍。提供 5 个标注示例可显著降低 Claude 的生成误差，但收益随计时目标而变化。

Conclusion: LLM 零样本直接预测运行时并不可靠，仅少量示例的上下文学习收益不稳定；需以数据驱动的回归基线或专用方法为准，基准数据与评测流程将开源。

Abstract: Researchers submitting deep learning jobs to shared GPU clusters need runtime estimates to request time limits, choose hardware, and budget compute. Large language models (LLMs) offer a tempting shortcut: describe the job and ask. We introduce PreRun, a benchmark for predicting deep learning training and inference runtime from information available before execution. PreRun contains 801 training and 1,884 inference records of language and vision models collected on a GPU cluster, paired with workload configurations and explicitly defined timing targets. It also includes 64 text-generation configurations measured under standardized conditions, each in three separate program launches. We compare three LLMs (Qwen3.5-9B, GPT-OSS-120B, and Claude Opus 5.5) with regression baselines and evaluate analytical estimates on a supported forward-pass subset. On 16 held-out generation configurations, the open-weight LLMs underestimate latency by 90-99% on average without examples. Claude's zero-shot mean absolute error is 4.5 times that of a Random Forest fit on 32 configurations. Five labeled examples substantially reduce Claude's generation error, but their benefit varies across timing targets. We will open-source our data and evaluation pipeline upon acceptance.

</details>


### [175] [Modeling Attributional Style at Scale: A Dataset and Analysis for Psychological Attribution Assessment and Reframing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26900)
*Qiang Zhou,Hanzhen Zhu,Pan Wang,Rui Tu,Huaizhi Qu,Zhuoran Wang,Xin Hu,Lei Li,Tianlong Chen,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出归因风格迁移数据集ASTD（42,000个真实事件，覆盖七种归因风格）及配套评估指标，用于大规模建模、评估与重述归因解释，并用该指标构建偏好数据、以DPO微调LLM显著提升重述质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 根据重构的习得性无助理论，反复经历不可控负性事件会形成致抑郁的归因风格，增加抑郁易感性，但该风格仍是认知疗法的可干预靶点；然而归因认知的计算研究受限于缺乏大规模数据集与稳健评估协议。

Method: 提出首个归因风格基准ASTD：通过Prevent-Filter-Validate流程（LLM生成+专家验证）构建，含42,000个真实事件及七种心理学归因标注。任务包括：(1) 基于监督分类器与零/少样本LLM的归因风格可扩展评估；(2) 归因重述及其自动评估指标（量化心理效度）；并利用该指标构建偏好数据集，以DPO微调LLM。

Result: 监督分类器与零/少样本LLM在归因风格评估上得到系统比较；所提自动指标可量化重述的心理效度；基于该指标构建偏好数据并经DPO微调后，重述质量取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 数据集、指标与方法共同构成理解与建模归因风格的新范式，对可扩展、自适应的心理健康干预具有直接意义。

Abstract: According to the reformulated Learned Helplessness theory, repeated exposure to uncontrollable negative events can foster a depressogenic attributional style, increasing susceptibility to depression yet remaining a tractable target for cognitive therapy. Computational research on attributional cognition, however, is hampered by the lack of large-scale datasets and robust evaluation protocols. In this work, we introduce the Attributional Style Transfer Dataset (ASTD) along with dedicated evaluation metrics, the first benchmark designed to model, assess, and reframe attributional explanations at scale. Constructed via a Prevent-Filter-Validate pipeline that integrates LLM-based generation with specialist validation, ASTD contains 42,000 real-world events paired with psychologically grounded attributions spanning seven styles. Using this dataset, we address two key challenges: (1) scalable assessment of attributional style via both supervised classifiers and zero/few-shot LLMs; and (2) attributional reframing and evaluation, where we propose automatic evaluation metrics to quantify psychological validity. Furthermore, we leverage our proposed metrics to construct a preference dataset, fine-tuning LLMs with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and achieving substantial gains in reframing quality. Together, our dataset, metrics, and methodology offer a new paradigm for understanding and modeling attributional style, with direct implications for scalable and adaptive mental health interventions. Code and data: https://github.com/qzhou711/ASTD

</details>


### [176] [Search From the Start: Search-Native Pretraining for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26809)
*Kaustubh Ponkshe,Gyung Hyun Je,Stefan Taga,Colin Raffel,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Search-Native Pretraining：在预训练语料中规模化注入网络搜索查询并检索本地索引响应，使模型在预训练阶段即学会发起搜索调用并利用检索证据；QueryLM 在知识密集任务上优于常规预训练模型，且优势经SFT后仍保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LM虽在推理阶段可调用搜索工具，但预训练仍以静态文本预测为主，搜索能力通常仅在post-training引入，导致搜索技能缺乏预训练阶段的基础性构建。作者希望以普通文本语料为起点，在预训练阶段即习得搜索能力。

Method: 对预训练语料进行规模化增强：生成网络搜索查询，并借助本地索引检索对应的网络响应，构造包含"搜索调用—检索证据—语言建模"结构的增强语料；在此语料上训练模型，使其在语言建模过程中学会生成搜索调用并整合检索到的证据。实验覆盖Wikipedia与DCLM-Edu两个语料、360M–3.3B参数规模，并与FLOP-matched基线及SFT后的模型对比。

Result: QueryLM在知识密集任务上性能提升，且对检索证据的可引导性（steerability）更强；该优势在两类模型均经监督微调后依然存在，说明post-training流程未能完全弥补预训练阶段形成的差异。1.4B QueryLM在所有知识密集任务上持续优于或匹敌FLOP-matched的3.3B基线。

Conclusion: Search-native pretraining是一种在预训练阶段培养基础搜索能力的可行路径，其收益无法被现有post-training充分替代，并具备参数效率优势；作者开源了模型检查点与数据等训练产物。

Abstract: Modern language models (LMs) are increasingly deployed with access to search tools at inference. Yet pretraining remains predominantly focused on predicting static text, with search capabilities typically introduced during post-training. We propose Search-Native Pretraining as a practical route to teach search capability during pretraining from ordinary text corpora. Specifically, we augment pretraining corpus at scale with web-search queries and retrieve web response using a local index. Training on this augmented corpus teaches models to generate search calls and incorporate retrieved evidence during language modeling. Across Wikipedia and DCLM-Edu corpora and 360M to 3.3B parameter scales, search-pretrained models ("QueryLM") show improved performance on knowledge-intensive tasks and higher steerability toward the retrieved evidence. Crucially, their advantage persists after supervised fine-tuning of both search-pretrained and conventionally pretrained models, indicating that the tested post-training procedures do not fully recover the capabilities developed during pretraining. Throughout our experiments, we find that a 1.4B QueryLM consistently beats or matches the performance of a FLOP-matched 3.3B baseline model across all knowledge intensive tasks. These results establish search-pretraining as a practical way to develop foundational search capabilities during pretraining. We release the training artifacts, including model checkpoints and data.

</details>


### [177] [Evaluating Prompting Strategies for Zero-Shot Multilingual Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27807)
*Jakub Šm{í}d,Pavel Kr{á}l*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文系统评估七个开放权重指令微调LLM（8B–35B，稠密与MoE）在12种语言、3个领域上进行零样本多语言TASD，比较单体、思维链、序列、序列精炼四种提示范式。结果显示单体提示最优，稠密模型优于MoE，初始方面词召回是硬瓶颈，多步分解反而降低质量且带来最高2.38倍token开销而无F1收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ABSA研究多集中于英语或依赖微调的专用模型，开放权重LLM在零样本多语言TASD（复合情感三元组抽取）上的能力尚缺乏系统认识，尤其在不同语系、领域及稠密/MoE架构间的差异不明。

Method: 选取七个开放权重指令微调模型（8B–35B，含稠密与MoE），在12种语言、3个领域的零样本设置下执行TASD；对比单体、思维链、序列、序列精炼四种提示范式；采用细粒度错误分类画像与步骤级流水线追踪进行分析，并统计生成token量以衡量效率。

Result: 单体直接提示即取得较强零样本性能并优于多步流水线；稠密架构稳定优于稀疏MoE。错误画像与步骤追踪表明：初始方面词召回构成硬瓶颈，多步分解损害抽取质量；迭代精炼仅在较大模型上部分挽回损失，在较小架构上产生净负向的过度精炼。生成量分析显示多步策略带来最高2.38倍token开销且无F1提升。

Conclusion: 单体提示是多语言ABSA中最高效且有效的范式；方面词召回是性能上限的关键制约，多步分解与迭代精炼在当前开放权重LLM上收益有限、成本较高。研究为多语言TASD的提示设计与模型选择提供实证依据。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have advanced natural language understanding, their ability to perform complex target-aspect-sentiment detection (TASD) -- a key task within aspect-based sentiment analysis (ABSA) -- in zero-shot multilingual settings remains underexplored. Existing ABSA research predominantly focuses on English or relies on fine-tuned task-specific models, leaving a gap in understanding how open-weight LLMs handle compound sentiment triplet extraction across diverse language families and domains. We present a systematic evaluation of zero-shot multilingual TASD across 12 languages and three domains using seven instruction-tuned open-weight models -- ranging from 8B to 35B parameters across dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures -- under four prompting paradigms: monolithic, chain-of-thought, sequential, and sequential refine. Our results show that direct monolithic prompting achieves strong zero-shot performance, outperforming multi-step pipelines, while dense models consistently outperform sparse MoE architectures. Through fine-grained error taxonomy profiling and step-level pipeline tracking, we demonstrate that initial aspect term recall forms a hard bottleneck, whereas multi-step decomposition degrades extraction quality; iterative refinement only partially recovers this loss on larger models while inducing net-negative over-refinement on smaller architectures. Finally, generation volume analysis shows that multi-step strategies incur up to 2.38x token overhead without F1 gains, establishing monolithic prompting as the most effective and efficient paradigm for multilingual ABSA.

</details>


### [178] [LongCat Recall Sparse Attention: Co-Designing Key Organization and Retrieval for Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27233)
*Hao Zhang,Bin Xiao,Kefeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LRSA 提出一种增量维护的分页稀疏注意力索引，用细粒度中心代表+分页检索把 KV 选择从全历史搜索降为页级粗筛+页内精排，并在 SGLang 中实现，1024K 上下文中 key 选择比 DSA 快 4.70×，选择+注意力比全注意力快 35.60×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于检索的稀疏注意力（如 DeepSeek Sparse Attention, DSA）虽只计算少量 KV 条目，但选出这些条目本身仍需遍历全部可见历史，长序列下检索成为主要瓶颈，抵消了稀疏注意力的效率收益。

Method: 1) 把序列按时间分桶（time bucket），新 key 到达时各桶可独立增量索引，无需重组既有历史；2) 桶内将索引键组织为容量均衡的方向性分组（directional groups），每页打包固定数量的完整分组，并为每组保留独立中心向量；3) 用中心作为细粒度代表做页级选择，再对选中页成员与 sink、滑动窗口及可见未索引候选统一精排，得到最终 Top-K 逻辑位置；4) 工程优化：成员按重排页存储以支持规则访问，按组大小融合构建阶段，chunked prefill 时跨查询共享重叠固定候选的存储，减少 kernel 启动与候选搬运；5) 集成进 SGLang，异步构建新桶索引并与后续计算重叠，前缀命中后复用兼容的桶索引与成员页。

Result: 在 1024K token 上下文下，LRSA 的 key 选择相比 DSA 加速 4.70×，选择+注意力合计相比全注意力加速 35.60×。

Conclusion: 通过增量分桶索引、分组中心代表与分页两级检索，并结合存储/融合/共享等系统级优化及 SGLang 运行时集成，LRSA 有效消除了长上下文稀疏注意力的检索瓶颈，使端到端效率随序列增长保持优势。

Abstract: Retrieval-based sparse attention, exemplified by DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA), reduces attention computation by selecting a small subset of key-value entries. However, selecting these entries can still require searching the entire visible history. For long sequences, retrieval becomes a major bottleneck, limiting the efficiency gains from sparse attention. To address this problem, we propose LongCat Recall Sparse Attention (LRSA), which combines fine-grained representatives with paged retrieval in an incrementally maintained index. LRSA partitions the sequence into time buckets that can be indexed independently as new keys arrive, without regrouping existing history. Within each bucket, it organizes indexing keys into capacity-balanced directional groups and packs a fixed number of complete groups into each page, retaining a separate center for every group. These centers provide fine-grained representatives for page selection, after which refinement scores members of the selected pages together with sink, sliding-window, and visible unindexed candidates to select the final Top-$K$ logical positions. To execute this retrieval process efficiently, we store members in reordered pages, fuse construction stages according to group size, and share storage for overlapping fixed candidates across queries during chunked prefill. These choices support regular member access while reducing kernel launches during construction and repeated candidate transfers. Moreover, we integrate LRSA into SGLang to maintain the index during inference, overlapping asynchronous index construction for new buckets with subsequent computation and reusing compatible bucket indices and member pages after a prefix match in the original key-value pool. With a 1024K-token context, LRSA accelerates key selection by 4.70$\times$ over DSA and combined selection and attention by 35.60$\times$ over full attention.

</details>


### [179] [Alliance Rupture and Repair in Simulating Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27544)
*Jian Chai,Yitian Yang,Siqi Liu,Yi-Chia Wang,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ALLy是一个将联盟破裂、修复与重规划引入持续CBT对话的模拟框架，其咨询师智能体仅凭对话推断隐式联盟状态并动态修复与调整方案，在多维度评测中优于现有合成数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBT模拟器主要建模来访者阻抗并遵循结构化治疗方案，但很少追踪联盟状态，也不支持咨询师在多轮对话中修复破裂并据此修改计划，与真实CBT临床工作存在差距。

Method: 提出ALLY模拟框架：为每位来访者设置隐式联盟状态，控制其投入与开放程度；咨询师智能体仅能看到对话内容，需识别来访者退缩或对抗的信号，完成破裂修复，并基于所获信息重规划后续会话。

Result: 自动评测显示，ALLY的咨询师在策略流程与情绪动态上比现有公开合成数据集的咨询师更接近真实CBT咨询师；在咨询质量（CTRS）与人际技能（FIS-T）上总体评分最高。20名持证治疗师的专家评估认为模拟破裂可信，修复与重规划大体恰当。

Conclusion: ALLY将联盟破裂—修复—重规划纳入多轮CBT对话模拟，提升了模拟咨询师的真实性与咨询质量，并经专家评估验证其临床可信度，为咨询师训练与评估提供了更贴近真实情境的平台。

Abstract: In cognitive behavioral therapy (CBT), routine work can strain the client-counselor working alliance, and the counselor must then notice the rupture, repair it, and adapt the work that follows. Existing CBT simulators model client resistance and follow structured treatment plans, but rarely track alliance state or allow counselors to repair ruptures over multiple turns and revise their plans accordingly. We present ALLY, a simulation framework that brings alliance rupture, repair, and replanning into ongoing CBT dialogue. Each client has a hidden alliance state that governs how openly the client engages. Like a real counselor, the counselor agent sees only the dialogue. It must notice when the client pulls back or pushes against the work, repair the rupture, and replan the rest of the session around what it has learned. Automatic evaluation shows that ALLY's counselors are closer to real CBT counselors than those of existing public synthetic datasets in strategy flow and emotional dynamics, and that ALLY is rated highest overall in counseling quality (CTRS) and interpersonal skill (FIS-T). An expert evaluation with 20 licensed therapists also finds the simulated ruptures plausible and the repairs and replanning largely appropriate.

</details>


### [180] [The Assistant Persona Differs Across Languages in Activation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27504)
*Daniel Yoo,Naama Rozen,Evan Harris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文在Qwen3-32B中提取31种语言的"助手人格方向"，发现各语言方向差异显著（与英语轴余弦相似度0.378–0.911），且方向与模长同语言资源水平相关；低资源语言的默认助手状态更远离有益助手极；沿本语言轴施加反向引导比沿英语轴更易触发人格类越狱。结论是模型并不存在跨语言统一的助手表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM跨语言行为差异（低资源语言安全性更差）已有行为学证据，但缺乏机制层面解释。激活空间方向（如助手人格方向）几乎都从英语提取并被默认可跨语言泛化，这一假设未被检验。

Method: 在Qwen3-32B上对31种语言分别提取助手人格方向（激活空间中的轴），计算各语言轴与英语轴的余弦相似度与模长；将方向/模长与语言资源水平做相关分析；测量默认助手状态到有益助手极的距离；并进行对比性引导（steering）实验，比较沿本语言轴与沿英语轴反向引导对人格类越狱的放大效应。

Result: 各语言轴与英语轴余弦相似度仅0.378–0.911，差异显著；方向与模长均与语言资源水平相关；低资源语言下模型默认助手状态离有益助手极更远；沿本语言轴反向引导对人格类越狱的放大强于沿英语轴。

Conclusion: 模型不存在单一、跨语言共享的助手表征；助手人格应作为多语言对齐的显式优化目标，而非默认由英语方向泛化覆盖。

Abstract: Large language models behave differently across languages, and are noticeably less safe in lower-resource languages. This gap is well established behaviorally, but not mechanistically. Directions in activation space, including the assistant persona direction, are almost always extracted from English and assumed to generalize across languages. We extract the assistant persona direction in 31 languages in Qwen3-32B, and find that the resulting axes differ substantially: their cosine similarity to the English axis ranges from 0.378 to 0.911. Moreover, each language's assistant persona direction and its magnitude are correlated with the language's resource level. We also find that the model's default assistant state is farther from the helpful assistant pole in lower-resource languages. Finally, steering against a language's own axis amplifies persona-based jailbreak more than steering against the English axis. These results indicate that the model does not carry a single assistant representation across languages. More broadly, the assistant persona may need to be an explicit target of multilingual alignment.

</details>


### [181] [Alignment without Transfer: Tokenization Impacts Cross-lingual Representations during Multilingual Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27491)
*Jonathan von Rad,Pontus Stenetorp,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过 39 个仅分词器不同的 1B 单语/双语模型，发现更好的词表分配显著提升早中层跨语言表示对齐与 FFN 参数知识共享，但对跨语言迁移几乎无影响，存在"对齐—迁移解耦"，且跨书写系统迁移差距并非分词器伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言 NLP 中分词器普遍对低资源语言分配不足的词表，且虽已知改进分配可提升性能，但分词化如何影响预训练中的跨语言表示与迁移仍缺乏理解。现有工作多只观测输出层，无法捕捉内部表示的差异。

Method: 在 FineWeb2-HQ 上针对英、德、法、阿、中五种语言预训练 39 个 1B 规模 decoder-only 单语与双语模型，唯一变量为分词器（词表分配不同），并系统分析各层跨语言表示对齐、FFN 神经元中的参数知识共享（相关性与因果证据）以及跨语言迁移表现。

Result: (i) 改进词表分配显著提升早至中层跨语言表示对齐；(ii) 增加前馈神经元中的跨语言参数知识共享（有相关性与因果证据支持）；(iii) 出现对齐—迁移解耦：对齐提高但跨语言迁移基本不变；跨书写系统双语模型的迁移在公平词表分配下仍显著更低。

Conclusion: 分词器词表分配塑造跨语言内部表示与知识共享，但不足以决定跨语言迁移；书写系统壁垒并非单纯分词器伪影。检查点与代码公开。

Abstract: Tokenization represents one of the most crucial steps in mulilingual NLP pipelines. Yet, tokenizers routinely under-allocate vocabulary to low-resource languages. While improved allocation leads to better performance, it remains poorly understood how tokenization shapes cross-lingual representations and transfer during pretraining. To address this, we pretrain 39 mono- and bilingual 1B decoder-only models on FineWeb2-HQ across English, German, French, Arabic and Chinese, varying only the tokenizer. Our results demonstrate that improved vocabulary allocation (i) substantially raises cross-lingual representation alignment in early-to-mid layers, an effect that prior work measuring only the output layer could not observe, (ii) increases cross-lingual parametric knowledge sharing in feed-forward neurons, supported by correlational and causal evidence, and (iii) an alignment-transfer dissociation, where cross-lingual transfer remains essentially unchanged despite higher alignment. Moreover, transfer is significantly lower for cross-script bilinguals even under fair vocabulary allocation, indicating that the script barrier is not merely a tokenizer artifact. All of our checkpoints and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [182] [Silent Arbitration: How Large Language Models Resolve Co-Authoritative Conflicting Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27444)
*Sihyeon Lee,Jiwoo Kum,Jihun Song,Chanwoo Kim,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"共同权威人格冲突"(co-authoritative persona conflict)概念：单一提示同时赋予两个对立人格且无优先级规则时，模型会在任务回答中"静默仲裁"(silent arbitration)——仅22%的任务回答显式揭示冲突，45%由支配性人格主导，35%标注为"妥协"的回答仍保留方向性倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 日益接收关于角色、价值观、利益与沟通风格的高层指令，这类指令即便被赋予同等权威、且无优先级规则，也可能相互冲突。现有指令遵循评测通常只看是否服从，缺乏对"同等权威冲突如何被处理、是否被披露、最终偏向哪方"的系统刻画。

Method: 构建基于已发表原始材料的八类冲突类型，在5个LLM上测量三项指标：冲突承认(conflict acknowledgment)、解决策略(resolution strategy)、方向性立场(directional stance)；设置"直接检查"与"任务回答"两种问法，并对比提示框架(prompt framing)与普通复述(paraphrase)变异的影响。

Result: 直接检查中72%承认不兼容，但任务回答中仅22%显式呈现冲突；45%的双人格回答呈支配性(dominance)；35%的"妥协"类回答仍带方向性倾向；提示框架对仲裁可见形式的影响显著大于普通复述变异。

Conclusion: 指令遵循评测应超越"是否服从"，转向三问：模型是否披露冲突、如何解决冲突、回答偏袒哪一人格。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly receive high-level instructions about roles, values, interests, and communication style. Such instructions can conflict even when a prompt assigns them equal authority and provides no priority rule. We study co-authoritative persona conflict, in which a single prompt jointly assigns two opposing personas. Across eight conflict types grounded in published source material and five LLMs, we measure conflict acknowledgment, resolution strategy, and directional stance. Models acknowledge incompatibility in 72% of direct checks but explicitly surface it in only 22% of task responses, revealing silent arbitration. Dominance accounts for 45% of dual-persona responses, and 35% of compromise-labeled responses retain directional lean. Prompt framing further changes the visible form of arbitration substantially more than ordinary paraphrase variation. These findings show that evaluating instruction following should assess whether models disclose conflicts, how they resolve them, and which persona their responses favor.

</details>


### [183] [Reasoning in Fewer Tokens: Concept-Normalized Multilingual Scratchpads and Reversible Model-Adaptive Reasoning Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27326)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Suryabhan Singh Hada,Hemanth Hegadehalli Madhavarao,Qun Gu,Libin N. George,Wenhuan Sun,Neha Monga,Richard Williamson,Prakhar Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将BPE应用于CoT推理语料，学习表示高频语义片段的PUA私有区压缩词元并扩展词表，再以严格1:1可逆映射微调LLM，在保持可解释性的前提下实现无损的推理链长度压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT提示显著提升LLM推理能力，但冗长的自回归解码带来高昂推理成本。现有加速方法通过丢弃词元或使用连续隐表示来降本，属有损压缩，从根本上牺牲了可解释性与可审计性。

Method: 对推理语料做BPE，学出由PUA词元构成的压缩词表，每个词元对应一个高频语义片段；扩展LLM词表后，以严格的1:1词元映射进行微调，保证压缩可逆（无损）；该方法与已有有损方法正交，可叠加使用。在Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-14B、Qwen2.5-32B及四个基准上评测。

Result: 生成总词元平均减少14.7%，推理轨迹长度最多缩短27.1%，平均准确率仅损失1.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 压缩的中间语言可作为一种高效计算资源，在加速推理的同时完整保留LLM推理过程的透明性，并可与不可逆的有损压缩技术叠加。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting significantly improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) but incurs high inference costs due to lengthy autoregressive decoding. While previous efficiency approaches reduce costs by discarding tokens or utilizing continuous latent representations, these lossy methods fundamentally sacrifice interpretability and auditability. In this work, we introduce a novel compression framework for intermediate reasoning. We apply Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) to reasoning corpora to learn a compressed vocabulary of Private Use Area (PUA) tokens, each representing frequent semantic spans. After expanding the LLM's vocabulary, we fine-tune the model via strictly 1:1, our method is lossless. We evaluate our approach on Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-14B, and Qwen2.5-32B across four diverse benchmarks. Our method reduces total generated tokens by 14.7% on average with reasoning trace lengths dropping by up to 27.1%, while incurring a negligible average accuracy cost of 1.6 percentage points. Crucially, this approach is orthogonal to existing lossy methods, allowing practitioners to stack lossless and irreversible compression techniques. We demonstrate that a compressed intermediate language provides a highly efficient computational resource that accelerates inference while fully preserving the transparency of LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [184] [Partially canonical axes in large language model representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26920)
*Andrea Gregor de Varda,Ferdinando D'Elia,Greta Tuckute,Evelina Fedorenko,Meenakshi Khosla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在6个模型家族共12个LLM中，原生坐标轴比保持相同几何结构的随机旋转轴更与其它模型表征、人脑语言响应及可解释语言特征对齐，但远未达到最优对齐，因此LLM坐标"部分规范化"：既非完全任意，也非完全规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM的内部表征中，单个坐标轴是否具有意义，还是语义仅编码于表征空间的方向中；挑战"个体轴是任意的"这一常见假设。

Method: 跨6个模型家族12个LLM，将原生轴与保持相同几何（协方差）结构的随机旋转轴进行对比，评估其与其它模型表征、人脑语言响应、可解释语言特征的对齐程度，并以可达到的最优对齐作为上界参照。

Result: 原生轴在所有评估维度上均可靠地比随机旋转轴对齐更好；但原生轴的对齐程度显著低于可达到的最优对齐。

Conclusion: LLM坐标是"部分规范化"（partially canonical）的，既非完全任意也非完全规范。LLM语言表征理论须同时解释两点：其学习到的群体几何结构，以及为何个体单元会获得跨系统复现的响应模式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) develop rich internal representations of language, but whether their individual coordinate axes carry meaning, or whether meaning is encoded only in directions in representation space, is still an open question. A common assumption is that individual axes in LLMs are arbitrary. We here challenge this view: across 12 LLMs from six model families, the native axes are reliably more aligned with other models' representations, with human brain responses to language, and with interpretable linguistic features than randomly rotated axes that preserve the same geometry. The native axes nonetheless fall well short of the best achievable alignment, so LLM coordinates are not fully arbitrary but also not fully canonical: they are partially canonical. A theory of how LLMs represent language must explain both the population geometry they learn and why individual units acquire patterns of response profiles that recur across systems.

</details>


### [185] [How Language Models Compress and Compare: Understanding Selection with Token Covariance Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26874)
*Moshe Eliasof*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"token covariance maps"——一种提示级几何视角，追踪每层隐藏状态空间中token位置如何协变，从而揭示decoder-only transformer在结构化选择任务中呈现"先压缩、后比较"（compress-then-compare）的深度组织规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多语言模型任务本质上是"从结构化备选中做选择"，但学界尚缺乏对decoder-only transformer如何在深度上组织整个选择提示的简单图景，需要一种可跨模型、跨提示、跨训练阶段比较的诊断工具。

Method: 1) 构造token协方差图：在每一层计算隐藏状态空间中token位置之间的协方差，形成随深度索引的图序列，并用谱结构与块结构对齐instruction、context与候选答案；2) 在14个decoder-only模型（4个家族）与12个结构化选择基准上验证；3) 通过更大固定模板样本、确定性提示改写、pairwise reranking重述、受控答案集操作做稳健性检验；4) 观察训练过程中该几何剖面的形成；5) 用低秩干预检验瓶颈区因果作用，并操纵单一候选的内部表示。

Result: 发现稳健的三阶段组织：浅层弥散表征提示，中层压缩为共享低秩瓶颈，深层再展开为context-option与option-option几何。该组织在改写、rerank重述与答案集操作下重复出现，并在训练中从初始平坦的深度剖面逐渐涌现。时间与行为对齐：有效答案区分出现在瓶颈之后；瓶颈附近的低秩干预优先破坏深层比较几何。相同几何剖面亦见于指令微调模型、多语言及开放式生成。改变压缩区附近某一候选的内部表示，可改变模型后续偏好。

Conclusion: token covariance maps是一种可用的诊断工具，用于比较不同模型、不同提示与不同训练阶段下信息在深度上的组织方式，并揭示了结构化选择任务中"压缩-再比较"的共性机制。

Abstract: Many language-model tasks reduce to selecting among structured alternatives, yet we still lack a simple picture of how a decoder-only transformer organizes an entire selection prompt across depth. We introduce token covariance maps, a prompt-level geometric view that tracks how token positions co-vary in hidden-state space at each layer. This turns a structured prompt into a depth-indexed sequence of maps whose spectral and block structure can be aligned with instructions, context, and candidate answers. Across 14 decoder-only models from 4 families and 12 structured-selection benchmarks, token covariance maps reveal a robust compress-then-compare organization. Early layers represent prompts diffusely, middle layers compress them into a shared low-rank bottleneck, and late layers re-expand into context-option and option-option geometry. This organization recurs across larger fixed-template samples, deterministic prompt rewrites, pairwise reranking reformulations, and controlled answer-set manipulations, while also emerging during training from an initially flat depth profile. Its timing aligns with behavior: useful answer discrimination appears well after the bottleneck, and low-rank intervention around the bottleneck preferentially disrupts late comparison geometry. We additionally show that the same geometric profile appears in instruction-tuned models, across several languages, and during open-ended generation. We further show that changing the internal representation of one candidate around the compressed region can shift which candidate the model later prefers. These results show that token covariance maps are a diagnostic tool for comparing how models organize information across prompts, model families, and stages of training.

</details>


### [186] [Reinforcement Learning Unlearns Corrupted SFT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26756)
*Anika Misra,Shreyas Vinod,Huteng Dai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在GSM8K上系统性注入错误构造361种受污染SFT配置后发现：SFT精度随污染加剧而下降（43.61%→13.80%），但GRPO后训练可将准确率恢复至金标基线水平（约60%），且仅用8条极简"形式化"错误轨迹初始化即可达69.30%，说明SFT主要赋予结构形式、RL独立习得功能推理，二者精度近乎不相关（r≈0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究推理任务中强化学习（RL）对SFT数据质量的依赖程度：SFT数据常含错误，而RL能否在严重污染甚至完全错误的监督信号下仍恢复/提升推理能力，此前缺乏系统性的受控实验证据。

Method: 在数学推理数据集GSM8K上，通过注入真实错误类型、改变错误位置、轨迹-答案一致性以及最高达100%的污染比例，系统构造361种训练轨迹配置；对比SFT-only与GRPO后训练的精度；并比较不同RL初始化方式（金标SFT初始化、8条三行"形式化"错误轨迹初始化、无SFT直接GRPO）；进一步分析SFT精度与RL后精度之间的相关性。

Result: SFT-only精度随污染加剧由43.61%降至13.80%；GRPO后训练稳定恢复至约60%的金标基线水平；以仅N=8条极简、错误的三行"形式化"轨迹初始化RL，后RL精度达69.30%，优于金标SFT初始化的60.71%及无SFT的GRPO（35.00%）；SFT精度与后RL精度基本不相关（r≈0）。

Conclusion: 后训练存在根本性非对称性：SFT主要建立结构布局（form），RL则独立发现功能性推理（function）；RL对SFT数据质量与正确性高度鲁棒，极少量的形式化轨迹即可作为有效初始化，表明RL在推理能力获取中起独立且关键的作用。

Abstract: We show that reinforcement learning (RL) in reasoning tasks can succeed even when the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data is severely corrupted. In a mathematical reasoning dataset (GSM8K), we systematically corrupt training trajectories across 361 configurations by injecting realistic error types, varying error locations, trace-answer consistency, and corruption ratios up to 100%. While aggregated SFT-only performance steadily deteriorates under corruption (dropping from 43.61% to 13.80%), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) post-training consistently restores model accuracy back to gold-baseline levels (approximately 60%). Crucially, initializing RL with as few as N = 8 minimal, incorrect, three-line "form-only" traces boosts post-RL performance to 69.30%, outperforming standard gold SFT initialization (60.71%) and GRPO without SFT (35.00%). Further, we show that SFT accuracy is largely uncorrelated with post-RL accuracy (r approximately 0), demonstrating a fundamental asymmetry in post-training: SFT primarily establishes structural layout (form), while RL independently discovers functional reasoning (function).

</details>


### [187] [Cross-Platform Production Signatures of State-Linked Information Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27137)
*Lin Tian,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"production signature"（生产签名）概念，仅用时间戳与粗粒度动作类型刻画信息操作账号的作业规律；在Twitter上训练的模型可零标签迁移到Reddit的IRA账号（AUC 0.90–0.94），显著优于内容检测器的跨平台衰减（0.98→0.81）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息操作在多平台使用不同名称、人设与议题，作业流程难以改变；现有内容/元数据/网络检测器跨平台迁移时性能大幅下降，需要一种难以伪装、可跨平台复用的行为层特征。

Method: 仅用时间戳与粗动作类型（发帖、回复、转发），不使用内容、资料元数据或网络关系；对账号建模时间/动作画像，对人群学习动作间隔策略（action-gap policies）。在四个国家关联操作的Twitter档案上训练，零Reddit标签迁移至Reddit IRA账号；并分析日相位、发帖量、动作构成的迁移性，以及在938个Telegram频道上检验多账号协同的时间同步性。

Result: 跨平台迁移对IRA账号排序AUC 0.90–0.94；同路径内容检测器由平台内0.98降至0.81。日相位（daily phase）贡献主要迁移信号；发帖量方向在平台间反转；动作构成随账号在各平台承担的角色变化。该签名非IRA独有：另一操作共享相同工作时段；action-gap策略可将各操作与自然账号区分（AUC 0.96–1.0），但不能稳定地把本操作排到首位。Telegram上协同转发同一来源的频道组保持常规作息与节奏，但原创帖落在相同十分钟窗口的频率高于随机，且单账号分析无法发现该模式。

Conclusion: 时间与动作轨迹构成难以改写、可在平台间迁移的操作指纹；在一平台学到的"生产签名"可用于在另一平台标记疑似信息操作，并需结合群体层面的同步性分析。

Abstract: An information operation active on several platforms presents different names, personas, and topics on each. Its working routine is harder to change. We call this routine the operation's production signature: the regularities in when and how its accounts post, reply, and repost. We measure it from timestamps and coarse action types only, using no content, profile metadata, or network ties, as timing and action profiles for accounts and learned action-gap policies for populations. We then ask which parts of a signature carry over to another platform and which belong to a single operation. We train models on Twitter archives of four state-linked operations and apply them to Internet Research Agency (IRA) accounts on Reddit, with no Reddit training labels. They rank the IRA accounts at AUC 0.90-0.94. Content detectors on the same route fall from 0.98 within a platform to 0.81. Daily phase accounts for most of the transfer. Posting volume reverses direction between platforms, and action mix follows the role accounts hold on each platform. What transfers is not unique to the IRA: a second operation shares the same working-hours window, and action-gap policies separate every operation from organic accounts (AUC 0.96-1.0) without consistently ranking their own operation first. On 938 Telegram channels, groups that forward the same sources keep ordinary hours and pace but publish original posts in the same ten-minute windows more often than chance. This pattern is invisible when accounts are examined one at a time. Our results suggest that timing and action traces learned on one platform can help flag likely operations on another.

</details>


### [188] [How Many Bytes Has a BPE Model Seen? Estimating Byte-Token Exposure in Pretraining from Corpus Statistics and Embedding Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27506)
*Liwei Zhang,ZhiMing Tong,Mochen Guan,Linzhe Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 估计 Qwen3.8 等模型中 256 个单字节回退 token 所接受的训练曝光量，用"语料分词统计"与"嵌入/输出矩阵行范数分析"两条独立路径交叉验证，得出真实 UTF-8 回退字节约占分词 0.15–0.27%、约每万亿预训练 token 中 20 亿次出现的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Byte-level BPE 词表保留 256 个单字节回退 token，在转为纯字节模型时它们充当初始嵌入，但这些 token 的实际训练曝光量未知；不清算这一点，就无法判断其初始化的可靠性与后续训练的意义。

Method: 路线一：对 540 MB、覆盖 40 个领域 33 种语言的公开语料分词，将各领域 token 占比与假设的预训练混合比例加权；路线二：分析四个开源模型的嵌入矩阵与输出矩阵行范数，以未训练的 padding 行为"零曝光"基线、以频率已知的普通 token 做校准，并反演频率。

Result: 单字节 token 占 13–36%（以 ASCII 为主）；真正的 UTF-8 回退字节（0x80–0xFF）仅占 0.15–0.27%，约每万亿 token 20 亿次。回退率跨语言相差四个数量级，反映各文字系统被词表覆盖的程度。四个模型中 13 个非法 UTF-8 字节均停留在初始状态，而所有合法高字节均处于高频带；Qwen3.8 的范数估计与语料估计相差在 4 倍以内。

Conclusion: 单字节回退 token 的真实曝光高度不均衡：非法字节基本未被训练，合法高字节曝光充足。范数–频率关系最终会发生折叠，故范数反演只宜作模型特定的数量级估计；两种方法的一致性支持该结论的稳健性，且全程未使用私有数据。

Abstract: Byte-level BPE vocabularies retain 256 single-byte fallback tokens. When converting such a model into a pure byte-level model, these tokens provide the starting embeddings. How much training have they received? We estimate byte-token exposure in Qwen3.8 using two independent routes. First, we tokenize 540 MB of public corpora covering 40 domains and 33 languages, then combine per-domain token shares with assumed pretraining mixtures. Single-byte tokens account for 13 to 36% of tokens, mostly ASCII; genuine UTF-8 fallback bytes (0x80-0xFF) account for 0.15 to 0.27% under mainstream mixtures, or about 2 billion occurrences per trillion pretraining tokens. Fallback rates vary by four orders of magnitude across languages, reflecting how well each script is covered by the vocabulary. Second, we analyze embedding and output-matrix row norms for four open models, using untrained padding rows as a zero-exposure baseline and ordinary tokens with known frequencies for calibration. In all four models, the 13 bytes that cannot occur in valid UTF-8 remain at initialization, while every legal high byte lies in a high-frequency band. For Qwen3.8, the norm-based estimate agrees with the corpus estimate within a factor of four. The norm-frequency relation eventually folds, so inversion is useful only as a model-specific, order-of-magnitude estimate. No private data were used.

</details>


### [189] [Tasklets: A Unit-Test Benchmark for Language Model Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27607)
*Zayd Muhammad Kawakibi Zuhri,Munachiso Samuel Nwadike,Kentaro Inui,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Tasklets，一个小型合成任务基准，用于评估语言模型序列混合层的计算能力，统一召回、序列操控、状态追踪等基础任务的选取、构造与评测，便于跨研究可比与低成本复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列混合层（如各类线性/注意力替代结构）研究常依赖召回、序列操控、状态追踪等合成任务，但各研究在任务选取、任务构造与评测方式上差异较大，导致不同工作之间难以公平比较。作者希望整合这些任务并提供共享评测设置。

Method: 构建 Tasklets 基准：按所测能力对合成任务进行组织归类，统一任务构造与评测流程；在小模型上开展受控实验，对多种层类型进行横向比较；并公开任务实现与评测代码以支持可复现、低成本的架构验证。

Result: 给出多种序列混合层类型在小规模受控实验下的基础计算能力对比结果，展示不同层类型在学习召回、序列操控与状态追踪等能力上的差异。

Conclusion: Tasklets 为序列混合层的架构研究提供统一、可复现且低成本的评测平台，提升跨研究比较的可靠性，并方便新架构提案的快速验证。

Abstract: We introduce Tasklets, a benchmark of small synthetic tasks for evaluating the computational capabilities of language model sequence-mixing layers. These tasks probe foundational capabilities such as recall, sequence manipulation, and state tracking. Such tasks are widely used in architecture research, but differences in task selection, formulation, and evaluation make comparisons across studies difficult. Tasklets brings these tasks together, organizing them by the capabilities they test and providing a shared setup for evaluating how different architectural layer types learn fundamental computations. We compare a broad range of layer types through controlled experiments with small models. Our task implementations and evaluation code support reproducible, low-cost testing of new architectural proposals: https://github.com/zaydzuhri/flame/tree/tasklets.

</details>


### [190] [Explicit Style Extraction for Personalized Long-Form Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27211)
*Jenitha Reddy Patel,Sameeksha Kini,Amulya Rayasam,Anisa Dye,Abhinav Sharma,Sai Karthik Navuluru,Bo Ni,Li Li,Junda Wu,Tong Yu,Nedim Lipka,Xiaolei Huang,Tyler Derr,Yue Zhao,Zhengzhong Tu,Yu Wang,Hongjie Chen,Thien Huu Nguyen,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出风格分类体系 StylePrism（7 类 28 个风格维度）与推理阶段方法 SP-Refine，将风格抽取显式化：先填充 StylePrism 生成用户画像，再基于样本起草并用自评风格分数两轮修订；在 LongLaMP 上 27 项 ROUGE/METEOR 比较中 25 项优于 RAG 基线，平均提升 10.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化长文本生成通常依赖检索增强生成（RAG），仅把用户历史文本放入提示，让模型自行决定借鉴内容，风格信息抽取过程隐式且不可控。

Method: 1) StylePrism：7 大类别、28 个风格维度的可核查分类体系；2) SP-Refine：推理期三阶段——先用风格模式、引用证据、应避免的习惯填充 StylePrism 形成用户画像，再依据用户样本起草，最后依据自身风格评分对草稿进行两次修订。

Result: 在 LongLaMP 三个长文本任务、三个 LLM 上，SP-Refine 在 27 项 ROUGE 与 METEOR 比较中胜出 25 项，平均提升 10.2%，最高 35.8%。用抽取画像标注用户原文显示，StylePrism 捕获的是具体可核查的写作习惯（如修辞顺序、署名前缀）。消融：仅凭画像写作在 27 项比较中全部更差，说明画像的价值在于修订指导而非替代样本；单次隐式推理调用是成本更低、效果接近的替代方案。Oracle 变体与无参考对照表明，更大的修订收益依赖推理时不可获得的信息。

Conclusion: 显式风格抽取作为修订指导能有效提升个性化长文本生成，但增益并非来源于更多生成轮次，而是受限于推理时可用信息；单次隐式推理为性价比更高的折中方案。

Abstract: Personalized long-form generation asks a language model to write new text the way a specific user would. The standard recipe, retrieval-augmented generation, places a few of the user's past texts in the prompt but leaves the model to decide what to take from them. We introduce StylePrism, a taxonomy of 28 stylistic aspects in seven categories, and SP-Refine, an inference-time method that makes style extraction an explicit step: the model first fills StylePrism with the user's patterns, quoted evidence, and habits to avoid, then drafts from the user's samples and revises the draft twice against this profile, guided by its own style scores. On the three long-form tasks of LongLaMP with three LLMs, SP-Refine outperforms LongLaMP's retrieval-augmented baseline in 25 of 27 ROUGE and METEOR comparisons, by 10.2% on average and by up to 35.8%. Annotating users' own texts with the extracted profiles shows that StylePrism captures concrete, checkable habits, from the order of rhetorical moves to a reviewer's sign-off. Ablations show that the profile helps as revision guidance, not as a replacement for the samples: writing from the profile alone is worse in all 27 comparisons, while a single implicit-inference call is a cheaper alternative that comes close. Oracle variants that let the reviser see the reference, together with reference-free controls, show that larger refinement gains require information unavailable at inference time.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [A Continuous Relaxation of Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27056)
*Kartikeya Bhardwaj,Mohammed Almousa,Chaitanya Mullapudi,Ananth Balasubramanian,Nithin Ramesan,Tuc Nguyen,Harris Teague*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RelaxQAT，将量化感知训练（QAT）中的位宽作为连续可微变量进行渐进松弛，以缓解低比特（2-bit及以下）通道级量化的平坦损失面/鞍点问题，在视觉与NLP任务上较主流QAT方法取得显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大生成模型部署中低比特量化（尤其2-bit及sub-2-bit）极为困难，通道级方案硬件高效但精度损失大；核心难点包括量化级数有限与损失面存在鞍点或平坦区域。现有方法要么突变位精度、要么固定位宽，均未针对平坦损失面有效处理。

Method: 以"动态改变位宽"为视角处理平坦量化损失面：不采用位宽突变或固定，而是在训练过程中渐进调整位宽；具体将QAT过程做连续松弛，使位宽成为随训练平滑演化的连续量（RelaxQAT），并给出简要理论与实证分析解释其有效性。

Result: 在视觉任务上相较领先QAT方法取得最高约6.3%的平均精度增益；在自然语言任务上平均精度提升最高约7.7%。

Conclusion: 通过位宽的连续松弛与渐进演化，RelaxQAT能有效绕开低比特通道级量化的平坦损失面困境，显著优于现有QAT方法，为极低比特量化训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Quantization is a critical piece of the deployment pipeline in the era of large generative models. Low-bit quantization, particularly 2-bits or sub-2-bits, is a notoriously difficult problem, particularly when using a channelwise quantization scheme which has been shown as a leading candidate for hardware-efficiency. The key challenges with low-bit quantization range from limited quantization levels to saddle points or flat regions in the loss surface. In this paper, we look at the problem of flat quantization loss surfaces through a lens of dynamically changing bitwidth. Unlike existing techniques which either change the bit-precision abruptly or keep it fixed, we propose to gradually alter the bitwidth as the model trains. To this end, we propose RelaxQAT, a continuous relaxation of the QAT process. We also provide simple theoretical and empirical insights behind why RelaxQAT helps the QAT performance. Our extensive experiments demonstrate up to 6.3% average accuracy gains on vision and up to 7.7% better average accuracy on natural language tasks, compared to the leading QAT methods.

</details>


### [192] [Memorization and Generalization in Generative Models: A Perspective From Energy-based Modeling and Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26797)
*Bao Pham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将生成模型的智能机制归因于联想记忆（AM）：模式以能量极小值存储、经吸引子动力学检索。作者论证扩散模型（含图像与语言）本质上是AM，其"伪状态"（spurious states）即生成能力的萌芽，记忆与泛化是同一检索过程的共存结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型已能写代码、生成逼真图像、长对话，但其智能机制仍未被理解。传统AM理论将伪状态视为记忆失败，作者欲反转这一视角，探究记忆与泛化如何共存，并寻找统一框架来回答生成模型何时"回忆"、何时"创造"。

Method: 1) 建立扩散模型与AM的对应关系，分析训练样本周围吸引域的形成与收缩；2) 通过测量吸引域的体量（volume）、能量（energy）与曲率（curvature），跨数据集与架构（含Stable Diffusion）刻画从记忆到生成的相变；3) 转向语言，证明均匀态离散扩散模型（uniform-state discrete diffusion）在仅依赖条件似然最大化的情况下即为AM，可检索未见数据；4) 以条件熵（conditional entropy）作为廉价诊断指标，定位模型在相变中的位置。

Result: 数据量小时，扩散模型为每个训练样本形成独立吸引域；随数据增长，吸引域收缩，伪状态作为混合存储样本但不匹配任一者的吸引子出现——这是生成能力的最初迹象，并在Stable Diffusion等模型上得到验证。语言模型中，训练样本周围的吸引域收缩、未见样本周围的吸引域扩张，直至被破坏的测试句子能被与训练样本同等可靠地恢复；条件熵可在单条序列内揭示这种共存并诊断模型所处阶段。

Conclusion: AM是以吸引域为核心的一般性原理，记忆与泛化可在单一模型中并存。该框架为判断生成模型何时回忆、何时创造提供了理论语言与可操作指标。

Abstract: Generative models now write working code, produce realistic images, and hold long conversations, yet the mechanisms behind their intelligence still evade our understanding. This thesis explores that puzzle through Associative Memory (AM), in which patterns are stored as energy minima and retrieved through attractor dynamics. Beyond their capacity, AMs form new minima known as spurious states, long regarded as a failure of memory. We argue that this failure is where creativity begins, and that memorization and generalization can coexist as outcomes of one retrieval process. First, we show that diffusion models behave as AMs. With little data, a diffusion model forms a distinct basin of attraction around each training sample. As the data grows, these basins shrink, and spurious states emerge as attractors that blend stored samples yet match none of them. They are the first signs of generative capability. We characterize this transition by measuring the volume, energy, and curvature of these basins across datasets and architectures, including Stable Diffusion. We then turn to language and move beyond the energy-based view of AM. We show that uniform-state discrete diffusion models are AMs capable of retrieving unseen data, with basins of attraction that arise from conditional likelihood maximization alone. As the training set grows, basins around training examples contract while basins around unseen examples expand, until corrupted test sentences are recovered as reliably as training ones. Conditional entropy exposes this coexistence within a single sequence and cheaply diagnoses where a model lies along the transition. Together, these results suggest that AM is a general principle defined by basins of attraction, and that memorization and generalization can coexist within one model. AM thus offers a framework for asking when a generative model recalls and when it creates.

</details>


### [193] [When Are Images Needed in Multimodal Machine Translation? Text-Only Routing for On-Demand Visual Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27712)
*Xinyu Wang,Yingfeng Luo,Yuxin Gao,Anxiang Ma,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将图像调用问题重新建模为源文本充分性预测，并据此构建细粒度视觉依赖数据集与轻量文本路由器 PreVIR，在视觉编码前决定走纯文本还是多模态分支，从而在英译中任务上以显著更少的视觉调用维持接近全程多模态的翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像引导多模态机器翻译（MMT）要么无条件处理图像，要么仅在视觉编码后才做门控，冗余视觉计算不可避免；要降低开销，路由决策必须依赖视觉编码之前的源语言信号。同时，表层歧义并不等于视觉依赖——句内文本上下文往往已足以消歧，因此需要仅凭源文本判断"翻译相关不确定性是否仍未解决"。

Method: 将视觉调用形式化为源文本充分性预测任务；构建带三级细粒度标签（no_ambiguity、text_resolved、vision_needed）的视觉依赖数据集，并验证 vision_needed 样本具备可区分的视觉证据；提出 PreVIR——一个轻量、与后端无关的纯文本路由器，在计算视觉特征之前选择纯文本分支或多模态分支。

Result: 英译中 MMT 实验中，PreVIR 相较强路由基线取得更优的 COMET–预算权衡；在默认工作点上大幅减少视觉调用次数，同时翻译质量与全程开启多模态推理相当。

Conclusion: 该权衡来源于模型能区分真正的视觉依赖与已被上下文消解的歧义；细粒度标签级分析与案例研究予以支持，表明以源文本为信号的前置路由是实现高效 MMT 的可行路径。

Abstract: Image-guided multimodal machine translation (MMT) leverages visual context to resolve translation-relevant uncertainty. However, prevailing systems either process images unconditionally or apply gating only after visual encoding, leaving redundant visual computation unavoidable. To mitigate this computational overhead, routing decisions must rely on a pre-visual source signal. Crucially, surface-level ambiguity does not equate to visual dependence, as intra-sentence textual context often suffices for disambiguation. We therefore formulate visual invocation as source-text sufficiency prediction: determining exclusively from the source text whether translation-relevant uncertainty remains unresolved. To support this formulation, we construct a visual-dependency dataset with three fine-grained labels (no_ambiguity, text_resolved, and vision_needed) and verify discriminative visual evidence for vision_needed instances. We further propose PreVIR, a lightweight, backend-agnostic text-only router that selects between text-only and multimodal branches before computing visual features. Experiments on English-to-Chinese MMT demonstrate that PreVIR achieves a favorable COMET-budget trade-off relative to strong routing baselines. At its default operating point, it substantially reduces visual calls while maintaining translation quality comparable to always-on multimodal inference. Label-wise and case analyses show that this trade-off stems from distinguishing genuine visual dependency from contextually resolved ambiguity.

</details>


### [194] [Reward Integrity in Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for LLM Guardrail Red-Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26998)
*Chengming Liu,Leon Viti,Hongxu Chen,Erdem Kemik,Mary-Anne Williams,Ruth Oliver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究红队测试金融语言模型防护栏的对抗强化学习循环中"奖励保真度"问题：攻击看似成功可能只因缺少规定的PII，或奖励评判模型误判未违规响应为成功；据此提出用专门决策模型分别校验攻击输入中的PII与真实违规结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗强化学习红队循环中存在虚假"获胜"：攻击未包含规定的PII，或奖励评判器错误地给未突破政策的响应记功，导致奖励信号失真、优化方向偏移。

Method: 以TypeSafe Jev校验攻击生成样本与公开榜单样本中规定PII是否存在；在结果打分上，将TypeSafe Jev、Laya（预训练与后训练版本）以及预训练CLM-8B的决策结果，与重放语料中记录的Opus 4.8判定进行一致性比较；并构建经作者人工复核的"偏靶集合"验证Opus误判。

Result: 后训练显著提升Laya与评判模型判定的一致性；作者复核的偏靶集合证实Opus确实会给出虚假胜利（false wins）。

Conclusion: 红队奖励需专门化决策模型评判：分别检查攻击输入是否符合规定PII、并按目标政策对响应打分，以提升奖励保真度。

Abstract: We study reward fidelity in an adversarial reinforcement-learning loop for red-teaming financial-services language-model guardrails. Apparent wins can arise when an attack omits prescribed personally identifiable information (PII), or when a reward judge credits a response that did not break the targeted policy. On attacker-generated and public benchmark examples, TypeSafe Jev checks whether the prescribed PII is present. For outcome scoring, we compare the decision models TypeSafe Jev, Laya (pretrained and post-trained), and pretrained CLM-8B with logged Opus 4.8 judgements from a replay corpus. Post-training markedly improves Laya's agreement with the judge. A separate author-reviewed off-target set establishes that Opus can award false wins. The findings motivate specialised decision-model judges for checking prescribed PII in attack inputs and scoring target responses against the intended policy.

</details>


### [195] [From Letter-of-Credit Requirements to Executable Specifications: Evaluating Verification Accuracy and Completeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26988)
*Ji Sub Choi,Whan Choi,Seo Yeon Gu,Suhyun Kim,Inyong Yun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出按"逐条要求"评估信用证单据审核的方法：用语言模型将提单与商业发票要求转写为原子条件与逻辑结构，再由确定性验证器检索证据并计算每条结果，使每项检查可与金标注对比并追溯至日志证据；同时指出终判准确率具有误导性（常数PASS规则最高）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用证单据审核中，最终判定正确并不代表所有要求都被审查过，传统以终判准确率为指标的评估无法反映审查完备性；且LC记录保密，难以获得真实训练数据。

Method: 将信用证中提单（BL）与商业发票（IV）要求转化为原子条件及逻辑结构；确定性验证器检索被命名证据并计算各项结果；因数据保密，9B解析模型仅在由模板及经人工复核的公开单据字段构造的合成数据对上微调，全流程本地部署。

Result: 345个真实LC案例上：常数PASS规则原始准确率最高却未做任何审查；微调解析器（训练中未见真实记录）覆盖其本体范围内的原子要求检测率达93%，86%的覆盖要求可精确重构；82%的可评估要求验证正确（工具型agent为54%），在满足项与不符项上均领先；完整审查案例数为同模型少样本提示的1.8倍，而直接预测与agent均未完成任一案例。

Conclusion: 用同一验证器执行金标注编码表明部分残余错误（含漏检不符点）源自算子与归一化，仅靠更优解析器无法修复；每次拒付均附单元与证据轨迹，适用于审查员在环复核。

Abstract: In letter-of-credit (LC) document examination, a correct final decision does not show that every requirement was examined. We therefore evaluate examination requirement by requirement and make each requirement executable. A language model converts the bill of lading (BL) and commercial invoice (IV) requirements of a credit into atomic conditions and logical structures, and a deterministic verifier retrieves the named evidence and computes each outcome, so every check can be compared with a gold encoding and traced to logged evidence. Because LC records are confidential, our on-premises 9B parser is fine-tuned only on synthetic pairs built from templates and human-reviewed public document values. On 345 real LC cases, final-decision accuracy proves misleading: a constant PASS rule has the highest raw accuracy while examining nothing. Although it never sees a real record during training, the fine-tuned parser detects 93% of the atomic requirements its ontology covers and reconstructs 86% of the covered requirements exactly. It verifies 82% of the evaluable requirements correctly, against 54% for a tool-using agent, and leads the agent on both satisfied requirements and discrepancies. It also completely examines 1.8 times as many cases as few-shot prompting of the same model, while direct prediction and the agent complete none. Executing the gold encodings with the same verifier shows that some of the remaining errors, missed discrepancies included, come from the operators and normalization, which a better parser alone cannot fix. Every rejection comes with a trace of the units and evidence behind it, which suits examiner-in-the-loop review.

</details>


### [196] [Right Numbers, Wrong Story: When LLMs Narrate Financial Changes Beyond Their Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26968)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Junhyeok Kang,Jaehoon Lee,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FrameCheck插件，检测并移除LLM财报草稿中"无依据框定"（输入未提供的判断性用词与因果解释），使判断率接近专业10-K撰稿人水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM撰写的财报即使数字全对，也常超出输入范围，加入无人提供的判断与原因（称之为unsupported framing），此类现象远多于专业撰稿人；而用"逐词用法"提示的方式修复反而使问题更频繁。

Method: FrameCheck为置于LLM与人工审核之间的插件：1) 用报告相似幅度变化的专业范例引导草稿生成；2) 标记专业人士不会使用的判断词及输入未提供的因果；3) 按规则移除，无需额外模型调用。

Result: 在17个开源LLM、300万份草稿上实验，几乎移除所有无依据框定，判断率接近美国企业10-K年报撰稿人水平。

Conclusion: 通过范例引导加规则化移除，可在不调用模型的前提下有效约束LLM财报写作范围，使其贴近专业报告规范。

Abstract: A financial report drafted by an LLM can state every number correctly but still go beyond its input, adding judgments and causes that no one supplied. We call these additions unsupported framing and find them far more often in LLM drafts than in reports written by proInput facts Revenue +3% Higher prices 10-K filings Revenue increased 3% on higher prices. 10-K writer Revenue surged 3% on higher prices, due to strong demand. LLM Fits the size? Surged: Unusual for 3% In the input? In the input? Strong demand: Not in input Higher prices: Kept fessionals, and attempts to fix them by prompting with how each word is used instead make such judgments more frequent. We therefore propose FrameCheck, a plug-in that sits beFrameCheck Revenue increased 3% on higher prices. No extra model call Judgment word Unsupported cause Supported cause tween the LLM and human review to keep each draft within its input. Specifically, FrameCheck 1) guides the draft with professional examples that report a change of similar magnitude, 2) flags judgments that professionals would not use and causes the input does not give, and 3) removes them by rule, without a model call. In experiments on 17 open LLMs and 3M drafts, FrameCheck removes almost all unsupported framing from drafts and brings the judgment rate close to that of the writers of 10-K filings, the annual reports that US firms file with regulators. Code is available at github.com/seunghan96/framecheck.

</details>


### [197] [Self-similar Organization of Information in Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26935)
*Leon Lufkin,Cutter Dawes,Kamesh Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于一族信息测度的标度行为，建立预测信息组织理论，并在长文本中观测到标度塌缩，证明预测信息在所有测量尺度上存在且呈自相似结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言统计结构及大语言模型能学到什么，关键在于弄清上下文中携带多少对预测后续文本有用的信息（预测信息），以及该信息如何沿历史分布。LLM 通过预测下一词元必然利用这些信息，其组织方式决定了语言结构如何被模型习得。

Method: 提出基于一族信息测度标度行为的预测信息组织理论。理论预测：若信息组织在所有测量长度尺度上具有相同形式（自相似），则不同长度下评估的信息测度会塌缩到统一形式。随后在长文本上检验该标度塌缩。

Result: 在长文本中观察到标度塌缩，表明预测信息在所有测量长度尺度上均存在，且具有自相似结构。自相似性是比长程依赖存在更强的性质，对文本生成过程构成约束。

Conclusion: 结果为"依赖更远文本能持续为 LLM 揭示语言新结构"这一经验观察提供了定量支持，并揭示了语言结构如何随尺度组织而可被模型学习。

Abstract: How much information relevant to predicting the future of a passage (i.e. predictive information) is carried by the preceding text, and how is this information distributed along that history? These questions are fundamental to understanding both the statistical structure of language and what large language models (LLMs) can learn about it. Indeed, LLMs trained to predict the next token in text must exploit this predictive information, so its organization determines how the structure of language becomes available to the model to learn. We develop a theory for the organization of predictive information based on the scaling behavior of a family of information measures, and the theory predicts that if this organization has the same form at all measured length scales -- i.e., it has a self-similar structure -- then the information measures evaluated at different lengths collapse onto a common form. We observe this scaling collapse in long text documents, showing that predictive information is present at every length scale we measure and has a self-similar structure. Self-similarity is a considerably stronger property than showing the existence of long-range dependencies, and it constrains the process generating the text. More broadly, our results give quantitative support to the empirical observation that conditioning on more distant text continues to reveal new structure in language to LLMs.

</details>


### [198] [AstronautBench: Measuring Agent Self-Improvement Across Episodes Through Knowledge, Evidence, and Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26910)
*Congyuan Yu,Runping Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AstronautBench，基于游戏 Outer Wilds 构建一个三视角（知识正确性、证据支撑性、世界进展）同轨迹评测基准，用于衡量固定权重 LLM 智能体的自我改进能力。实验表明三视角并不同步增长，单一指标无法完整刻画智能体是否真正在学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准分别衡量任务进展、世界知识问答与情节记忆，但正确答案不能证明智能体曾观察到支撑该答案的证据，任务进展也无法说明智能体究竟学到了什么。因此需要一个在固定权重、可重置世界中的多视角自我改进评测。

Method: 选用 Outer Wilds 作为环境：每回合为 22 分钟循环，世界重置、仅智能体知识保留，且进展受知识门槛限制。在同一次运行中从三个视角测量：(1) 到达地点与飞船日志传闻事实的揭示数量；(2) 164 道世界知识问题的答案正确性；(3) 每个答案是否由智能体自身上报的录像帧与位置证据支撑，并由两个不同模型家族的 LLM 评审共同核查。基线为 8 回合，规划器为 GPT-6.1 Sol 与 Gemini 4 Argon，通过学习到的控制器或游戏手柄输入行动。

Result: 三视角并不同步变化。GPT-6.1 Sol 智能体的回答正确率从 14.6% 升至 26.8%，但第 8 回合仅有 59%–68% 的正确答案有引用证据支撑；第 3 回合之后未再揭示新的传闻事实，而到达地点与记忆仍在增长；第 3 回合后新增的正确答案中，超过半数仅引用此前回合的证据。

Conclusion: 三视角中任一单独指标都只能给出智能体是否改进的不完整图景；将知识、证据可溯性与世界进展联合评测，能揭示"正确但未被观察支撑"和"知识停滞而进展继续"等被单指标掩盖的失败模式。

Abstract: LLM agents are expected to improve themselves from their own experience across episodes, with fixed weights. Self-improvement in an embodied world cannot be read from one measure. Existing benchmarks measure task progress, world-knowledge question answering (QA), and episodic memory, but a correct answer does not show that the agent ever observed the evidence for it, and progress does not show what the agent learned. We introduce AstronautBench, which measures one agent's self-improvement through three views on the same run: what it knows, whether its own evidence supports that knowledge, and how far it gets in the world. It is built on the game Outer Wilds, where each episode is a 22-minute loop after which the world resets, only the agent's knowledge carries over, and progress is gated by knowledge. After every episode, we measure the places reached and ship-log rumor facts revealed, the correctness of answers to 164 questions about the world, and whether each answer is supported by the frames and position the agent cites from its own recorded play. Two LLM judges from different model families check every answer. In eight-episode baselines with GPT-6.1 Sol and Gemini 4 Argon planners, which act through a learned controller or their own gamepad inputs, the three views do not move together, so any one of them alone gives an incomplete picture of whether the agent is improving. The GPT-6.1 Sol agent's correct answers grow from 14.6% to 26.8% of all questions, but at episode 8 only 59 to 68% of them are supported by its cited evidence. It reveals no new rumor facts after episode 3, while the places it reaches and its memory keep growing. More than half of the answers that become correct after episode 3 cite only evidence from earlier episodes.

</details>


### [199] [TOUMAII: A 4.5M-Pair Tool-Calling Preference Dataset and Its Source-Agnostic Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26722)
*Moritz Pötzsch,Andrea Gentilini,Lisa Rossgoderer,Tom Osterfeld,Kesava Prasad Sanjivi Arul,Swanand Ravindra Kadhe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 TOUMAII，一个约 450 万验证偏好对的大规模工具调用偏好数据集，并提供可复用的偏好数据生成流水线 TOUMAII-Gen。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体需可靠地调用工具、API 或查询数据库，而 SFT 只优化生成正确调用、不与合理错误调用作对比；工具调用中细微格式错误即导致调用失效，DPO 正好针对这一对比，但现实多轮场景下的工具调用偏好对被极度稀缺。

Method: 从 Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M 对话派生 ~4.5M 经校验的偏好对，涵盖单轮与多轮交互、并行与串行函数调用；同时发布 TOUMAII-Gen 合成流水线，消费工具调用 SFT 数据集并生成带有自然出现的模型工具调用失败模式的偏好数据；另以 ToolACE 派生第二个小规模偏好数据集验证流水线来源无关性。

Result: 用该 DPO 数据微调 30 亿参数模型，性能与其他高质量工具调用偏好数据集持平；基于 ToolACE 的第二个小数据集体现流水线的来源无关性。

Conclusion: TOUMAII 缓解工具调用偏好数据稀缺问题，TOUMAII-Gen 提供可迁移至不同 SFT 数据源的偏好数据生成方案，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: To autonomously execute multi-step tasks, enterprise agents must reliably interact with tools, call APIs, or query databases, making tool-calling a fundamental capability of AI models. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) optimises for producing the correct call, but never contrasts it with a plausible incorrect one. In an environment where even a minor formatting error can invalidate a call and an incorrect decision can derail the interaction, direct preference optimisation (DPO) targets exactly this contrast. DPO, therefore, requires preference pairs rather than plain demonstrations. For tool-calling, such data, especially in realistic multi-turn settings, remains extremely scarce. To address this scarcity, we introduce TOUMAII, a large-scale tool-calling preference dataset containing ~4.5M validated preference pairs, derived from Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M (apache-2.0) conversations. The dataset consists of multi- and single-turn interactions and includes parallel and sequential function calls. Additionally, we release TOUMAII-Gen, a synthetic data generation pipeline that consumes tool-calling SFT datasets and generates a preference dataset with naturally occurring model-generated tool-calling failure modes. To validate the effectiveness of our DPO data, we fine-tune a 3-billion-parameter model, achieving performance parity with other high-quality tool-calling preference datasets. Furthermore, we release a second small preference dataset derived from ToolACE (apache-2.0), showcasing the source-agnostic nature of our pipeline.
  We release the complete code and datasets: https://github.com/tum-ai/toumaii-gen and https://huggingface.co/collections/tum-ai/toumaii-generated-datasets

</details>


### [200] [Efficient Natural Language to Temporal Logic Translation for Real-Time Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27489)
*Oliver Schön,Adrien Banse,John Lygeros,Lars Lindemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出：将自然语言到线性时序逻辑（LTL）的翻译问题重构为回答一组具有预定义答案的问题，并用System-One分类器实现翻译器，从而在本地部署条件下以更低算力/能耗达到或超过最佳本地LLM的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务需以形式语言给出指令才能保证正确性，而用户习惯用自然语言；借助LLM把NL翻译为形式语言时，机器人端算力与能源受限，云端卸载可能不可行或不被接受。同时LLM是通用文本生成器，而固定形式语言片段的公式只需满足明确的语法约束，因此值得质疑"是否真的需要文本生成"。

Method: 针对LTL形式语言，作者证明NL→LTL翻译可分解为回答一组答案预定义的问题；据此构建System-One模型（一类基于自然语言的分类器，即单次前向、非生成的判别式模型）来回答这些问题，从而完成自然语言指令到LTL指令的翻译。

Result: 在三个基准上，该翻译器的精度与最佳本地可用LLM相当或更优，运行时间与能耗最多低一个数量级；并能泛化到未见的原子命题与时序模式。

Conclusion: 对于形式语言翻译任务，无需依赖通用生成式LLM；将其分解为预定义问题的分类式求解可在保持甚至提升精度的同时显著降低本地部署的计算与能耗开销，具备良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Robots can be controlled to perform tasks with guaranteed correctness when the task instructions are stated in a formal language. Large language models (LLMs) have been widely used to allow users to provide such instructions in natural language (NL), by translating NL into formal language. When deploying such translators on a robot, however, we face limited computing and energy resources, while offloading computation or data to the cloud may be infeasible or undesirable. Yet an LLM is a general-purpose text generator, whereas a formula in a fixed formal language fragment follows a well-defined syntax. We therefore ask whether text generation is needed at all. For the formal language of linear temporal logic (LTL), we show that this translation can instead be broken down into answering a set of questions with pre-defined answers. We then construct a System-One model, a recent class of NL-based classifier, to provide such answers and enable us to translate a natural language instruction into an LTL instruction. On three benchmarks, our translator matches or exceeds the accuracy of the best local LLMs available, at up to one order of magnitude lower runtime and energy consumption. We show that it generalizes to unseen atomic propositions and temporal patterns.

</details>


### [201] [AMARIS: Merging Generalist and Specialist LLMs via Adaptive Subspace Inheritance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27369)
*Hwee Young Kang,Irwin De Tao Chin,Wen-Haw Chong,Hai Leong Chieu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AMARIS——一种基于协方差与排序广义特征方向的低秩子空间投影门控模型合并框架，在无需训练的情况下实现专家能力注入并保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型领域特化通常提升目标域性能却损害通用能力；现有参数空间合并策略（插值、稀疏平均）对任务间干扰缺乏可控性。

Method: 将合并建模为“预算约束下的子空间继承”：在正则化的基模型输出扰动预算下最大化专家激活捕获，由排序广义特征方向构建低秩投影门，仅在选定激活方向上传导专家更新，得到单一静态合并检查点。

Result: 在Qwen3（8B/32B）的金融、指令遵循、多语种SEA特化上，AMARIS达到与强免训练基线相当的Pareto工作点：8B金融专家保持98.37%/核心保持95.16%；8B指令遵循核心103.55%/专家90.28%；32B SEA核心99.20%/专家94.80%。

Conclusion: 子空间约束式合并是无需额外训练即可实现组合式能力集成的可行路径。

Abstract: Domain specialization of large language models (LLMs) often improves target-domain performance but degrades broad capabilities. Model merging is an attractive training-free alternative, yet common parameter-space strategies (for example, interpolation and sparsified averaging) provide limited control over task-specific interference. We introduce Adaptive Model Alignment via Ranked Inherited Subspaces (AMARIS), a covariance-driven framework that transfers specialist updates only along selected activation directions. AMARIS formulates merging as budgeted subspace inheritance: it seeks high specialist activation capture under a regularized base-output perturbation budget, then builds a low-rank projection gate from ranked generalized eigendirections. This yields a single static merged checkpoint that concentrates transfer in high-utility, low-interference directions. We evaluate AMARIS on Qwen3-based merges (8B and 32B) across finance, instruction-following, and multilingual SEA specialization. In our evaluated settings, AMARIS reaches Pareto operating points that are competitive with strong training-free baselines, including 98.37% specialist retention with 95.16% core retention for the 8B finance setting, 103.55% core retention with 90.28% specialist retention for the 8B instruction-following setting, and 99.20% core retention with 94.80% specialist retention for the 32B SEA setting. These results support subspace-constrained merging as a practical route to compositional capability integration without additional training.

</details>


### [202] [Reasoning in Two Dimensions: Quantifying Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27100)
*Chenruo Liu,Quan Zhang,Qi Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将CoT表示为语义推理图，把模型的预测依赖分解为"语义支持 vs. 额外推理历史"和"到达答案路径 vs. 偏离路径"两条互补轴，据此定义全局与步骤级的不忠实/低效度量，并推导图感知微调目标以改进推理性质而不牺牲准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT虽暴露中间步骤，但答案正确并不说明模型真正依赖这些步骤表达的依赖关系，也不说明推理高效；现有评估缺少对忠实性与效率的统一、可量化框架，且常以准确率、简洁性作为替代指标。

Method: 把CoT构造为语义推理图；沿两轴分解预测依赖：语义支持 vs. 额外推理历史、答案路径 vs. 偏离路径；由此得到全局与步骤级的不忠实度、低效度指标；用受控干预验证指标对隐藏依赖与诱导偏离路径的敏感性；并在数学与多跳推理任务上分析指标与预测不确定性、答案正确性的关联；最后推导理论上具备依据的图感知微调目标。

Result: 受控干预显示指标按预期响应隐藏依赖与诱导的偏离推理；跨数学与多跳任务，答案级不忠实度与更高的预测不确定性一致相关，而不忠实度与低效度同答案正确性仅呈弱或不一致关联；图感知微调在不直接优化昂贵指标、不损害准确率的前提下改善了目标推理性质。

Conclusion: 准确率与简洁性不足以评估推理质量；可靠的CoT评估还应考察模型的预测依赖是否被忠实表示、是否被高效分配。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning exposes intermediate steps, but a correct answer does not establish that an LLM relies on the dependence expressed by those steps or reasons efficiently. We operationalize CoT faithfulness and efficiency within a common framework. Specifically, we represent a CoT as a semantic reasoning graph and decompose the model's predictive dependence along two complementary axes: dependence explained by semantic support versus additional reasoning history, and dependence allocated to answer-reaching versus off-path reasoning. This decomposition yields global and step-level measures of unfaithfulness and inefficiency. Controlled interventions show that the proposed metrics respond to hidden dependence and induced off-path reasoning as intended. Across mathematical and multi-hop reasoning tasks, answer-level unfaithfulness is consistently associated with greater predictive uncertainty, whereas both unfaithfulness and inefficiency exhibit weak or inconsistent associations with answer correctness. We further derive theoretically grounded, graph-aware fine-tuning objectives that improve the targeted reasoning properties without directly optimizing the computationally expensive metrics or compromising answer accuracy. Results indicate that accuracy and brevity provide incomplete assessments of reasoning quality: reliable CoT evaluation should also consider whether the LLM's predictive dependence is faithfully represented and efficiently allocated.

</details>


### [203] [Adding One More Agent: How AI Team Size and Formation Shape Productivity and Diversity in Human-AI Brainstorming Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26663)
*Rong-Ching Chang,Hao-Chuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在N=168的头脑风暴实验中，将AI伙伴数量（1个 vs 2个）与行为形成方式（按人类平均行为校准 vs 由参与者定制）交叉，并以人-人双人组为参照：双AI房间共享板上的想法总数更多，但人类产出的想法大幅下降；仅有一个AI时"校准方式"起作用（与平均校准AI配对的人类贴出的想法最多），有两个AI时该效应消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI提升了个人生产力，但增加多个AI伙伴究竟有利还是有害尚无定论；且单智能体实验结论能否推广到多智能体团队存疑。

Method: 2×2组间设计头脑风暴实验（N=168）：AI伙伴数量（1 vs 2）× 行为形成方式（按人类平均行为校准 vs 由参与者定制），并设置人-人双人组作为参照基线；记录共享板发帖量、人类发言与帖子转化率、AI社交性发言与发帖量、以及人类想法的全局多样性与会话内差异化。

Result: 双AI房间共享板想法总数更多，但人类撰写的想法显著减少；单AI条件下形成方式有影响，人-平均校准AI组合的发帖量高于其他所有条件，双AI下该效应消失；人类持续交谈但转化为板帖的想法更少；AI社交性发言正向预测人类发帖，AI发帖量负向预测人类发帖；AI在场提升人类想法的全局多样性，但只有第二个AI提升了会话内差异化。

Conclusion: AI伙伴的数量及其行为形成方式会重塑人类的贡献结构；单智能体情境下的结论不能直接推广到多智能体团队，多AI并未带来叠加收益，反而可能挤出人类的想法产出。

Abstract: Generative AI has improved individual productivity, but whether adding a plurality of AI partners helps or hurts remains open. We crossed AI partnership (one versus two) with behavioral formation (calibrated to average human behavior versus customized by the participant) in a brainstorming experiment ($N$=168) with human-human dyads as reference. Two-AI rooms posted more ideas to the shared board, but human-authored ideas dropped substantially. With one AI partner, formation mattered: humans paired with an average-calibrated AI posted more than any other condition. With two, this effect vanished. Humans continued talking but converted fewer ideas into board posts. AI social talk predicted more human posts, while AI posting volume predicted fewer. AI presence increased global diversity of human-authored ideas, but only a second AI increased within-session differentiation. The number of AI partners and their formation can reshape what the human contributes; single-agent results do not generalize to multi-agent teams.

</details>


### [204] [Belief-State Management Meets Rubric Rewards for Agentic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27286)
*Jingyi Chai,Yun Liao,Hao Lu,Kemeng Zhang,Bingyang Zheng,Yujie Ge,Xiangrui Liu,Linfeng Zhang,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结合"主动信念状态管理(PBM)"脚手架与"SEIPH"过程奖励的预测训练框架，训练出35B的DeepForecaster-PPO/GRPO，在ForecastBench与Polymarket-Sim上取得最佳Brier分数，超越多个前沿大模型智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体难以预测真实世界事件，原因有二：i) 标准ReAct脚手架在单一不断增长的上下文中累积推理与证据；ii) 预测任务的结果型奖励信号缓慢且噪声大，无法区分严谨推理与幸运猜测。

Method: 脚手架端：Proactive Belief-State Management (PBM)，为智能体配备fold工具，将冗长上下文压缩为紧凑信念状态并重置上下文以继续探索。训练端：SEIPH——五维、outcome-blind评分量表，提供过程质量奖励；据此用PPO与GRPO对35B模型进行后训练，得到DeepForecaster-PPO与DeepForecaster-GRPO。理论上证明基于fold的停止准则在理想化条件下收敛，且校准后的局部奖励降低分段级梯度方差。

Result: DeepForecaster-PPO在ForecastBench与Polymarket-Sim上取得最佳Brier分数，优于DeepSeek-V4-Pro(1.6T)、Kimi-K2.6(1T)、GPT-5.5驱动的前沿智能体；DeepForecaster-GRPO在两个FutureX指标上排名第二。消融显示PBM在无训练情况下也能提升基座模型。校准分析显示DeepForecaster-PPO的高置信预测比Qwen3.6与GPT-5.5更准确；轨迹分析表明预测随信念状态从首至末逐步改进。

Conclusion: 将上下文管理（PBM）与过程质量奖励（SEIPH）相结合的训练框架，可显著提升LLM智能体的真实事件预测能力，且脚手架改进与过程监督各自独立有效。

Abstract: Current large language model (LLM) agents still struggle to forecast real-world events. This stems from two primary limitations: i) standard ReAct harness accumulates reasoning and evidence within one growing context, and ii) outcome-based rewards in forecasting lead to a slow and noisy signal that cannot distinguish sound reasoning from lucky guesses. To address these, we introduce a forecasting training framework including harness design and post-training. For the harness side, we propose Proactive Belief-State Management (PBM), equipping agents with a fold tool to compress lengthy context into compact belief states and reset the context for subsequent exploration. For the training side, we develop SEIPH, a five-dimensional, outcome-blind rubric, providing process-quality rewards. Using the rubric-based reward, we post-train a 35B model with PPO and GRPO, yielding DeepForecaster-PPO and DeepForecaster-GRPO. Theoretically, we prove that idealized fold-based stopping converges and calibrated local rewards reduce segment-level gradient variance. Empirically, DeepForecaster-PPO (35B) achieves the best Brier score on both ForecastBench and Polymarket-Sim, outperforming frontier agents based on DeepSeek-V4-Pro (1.6T), Kimi-K2.6 (1T), and GPT-5.5. DeepForecaster-GRPO ranks second on both FutureX metrics. Ablations reveal that PBM also improves the untrained base model without any training. Calibration analysis shows that DeepForecaster-PPO makes more accurate high-confidence predictions than Qwen3.6 and GPT-5.5. Trajectory analysis further shows that forecasts generally improve from the first to the final belief state as agents gather evidence and revise their judgments.

</details>


### [205] [What Does Matching a Human Intervention Effect Establish? Benchmark Checks for LLM Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27186)
*Tengfei Shao,Asma Rezaei,Ali Reza Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM仿真研究中的"等价性检验"常因零参照(zero reference)而虚高通过：当人类95%区间落在裕度内时，任何零效应都会被判为等价。作者提出以计划精度(planned precision)给出独立仿真的裕度，使通过与否成为对"响应"的5%检验，并据此重分析实验数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM仿真通常以"与人类效应等价"作为效度证据，但现有等价性检验存在零参照漏洞：若人类90%区间本就在裕度内，则零参照(无响应的仿真)也会自动通过，导致通过的等价性并未真正证明仿真复现了人类响应。

Method: 提出在每次等价性判定旁报告"零参照阈值"；当零参照不通过时，用计划精度为独立仿真设定裕度，使通过等价性成为对响应存在的5%检验。开展三部分分析：(1) 70个实验的探索性重分析，统计GPT-4在±0.20 SD下通过中等价性属于零参照通过的比例；(2) 预注册审计，考察Claude Sonnet 5.5在意图目标、信念梯度对照及规范信念上的表现；(3) 组审计的精度下限核算。

Result: GPT-4的±0.20 SD通过中有72%属于零参照通过；GPT-4在零参照失败的16个对比上仍通过(若无响应预期仅1.58个)。预注册审计中Sonnet 5.5通过三个意图目标，均为零参照通过，并通过注册的信念梯度对照；但规范信念仅变化0.065 SD，远低于人类的0.351。注册检验未检测到高估，平均效应区间排除了0.08 SD以上的高估。组审计的精度下限为1.33个事实，而效应为2.9个事实。

Conclusion: 等价性检验的通过可能大量源于零参照而非真实响应复现，因此每项等价性判定应同时报告零参照阈值；在零参照失败后使用计划精度，可将等价性检验转化为对响应的实质性5%检验，从而更可靠地评估LLM仿真效度。

Abstract: LLM simulations are validated by equivalence with human effects, yet a zero reference passes whenever the human 90 percent interval lies inside the margin, as the human benchmark shows before simulation. When the zero reference fails, planned precision gives independent simulations the margin up to which passing is a 5 percent test of response. In an exploratory 70-experiment reanalysis, 72 percent of GPT-4's $\pm$0.20 SD passes were zero-reference passes; GPT-4 passed 16 contrasts the zero reference failed (1.58 expected without response). In a preregistered audit, Claude Sonnet 5.5 passed three intention targets, all zero-reference passes, and the registered belief-gradient control; norm beliefs changed 0.065 SD against humans' 0.351 (zero-reference check added after unblinding; norm beliefs emphasized after inspection). Registered tests detected no overestimation; average-effect intervals excluded overestimation above 0.08 SD. A group audit's precision floor is 1.33 facts against a 2.9-fact effect. Report the zero-reference threshold beside every equivalence decision.

</details>


### [206] [Double counting in LLM evidence fusion: exact posteriors separate rule compliance from posterior gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27138)
*Tengfei Shao,Asma Rezaei,Ali Reza Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文构建"分级依赖精确参照"(graded-dependence exact reference)：在二元世界、单报告可靠性0.65、四个依赖水平下给出精确后验与每条规则的目标准风险，用配对的"增益协议"评分原始回答与"每源只计一次"指令回答，在2,976个世界上评估四种配置。结果表明一致性与增益两个维度相互独立，指令的收益取决于接收者基线，需按接收者与提示条件逐一决定是否采纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在检索、多智能体共识与智能体记忆等场景中会融合报告；若把同源报告视为独立证据，会重复计数。常见补救是加入"每个来源只算一次"的指令，但该指令的实际价值取决于接收者的原始基线，缺乏精确参照就无法为其"定价"，因此需要一个可计算目标风险、可在调用模型前给出答案的精确参照框架。

Method: (1) 构建分级依赖精确参照：二元世界、单报告可靠性0.65、四个依赖水平，公开参数使每个世界有精确后验、每条规则有目标准风险；(2) 配对增益协议：对四种配置在2,976个世界上同时评分原始答案与加指令答案；(3) 以δ=0与δ=1的预设首子端点对比（增益差）作为主要检验，并做原始源权重对比与分组端点差异的预注册检验，报告95%区间与Holm校正。

Result: 一致性与增益在两个方向上均可分离。δ=0时claude-opus-5-5与gpt-6.1-sol在744/744个世界上与规则一致（误差<0.001），但指令使其后验失配上升0.044；gpt-6-luna仅165/744一致，增益0.0140（95%区间0.0064–0.0219）；claude-haiku-5-5增益由−0.0295(δ=0)转为+0.0302(δ=1)（描述性）。主要端点对比为0.0342（0.0303–0.0382）。预注册检验中原始源权重对比为正（0.259，0.182–0.337，Holm拒绝零），分组端点差异未检出（0.0004，−0.0023–0.0031），该区间排除幅度大于0.0031的差异且落在±0.0046精度界内。

Conclusion: 精确参照可在调用任何模型之前为"每源只计一次"规则定价；一致性并不预示增益，增益随接收者与依赖水平变化。以带标签部署样本上可估计的Brier差值定义配对增益，可作为按接收者、按提示条件决定是否采纳该指令的判据。

Abstract: Language models fuse reports in retrieval, multi-agent consensus and agent memory, where treating shared-source reports as independent can double count evidence.
